We compare supervised fine-tuning, rejection-sampled self-training, on-policy distillation, and verifier-based reinforcement learning as post-training methods and find that they elicit the missing behavior. The strongest post-trained model, the 8B model trained with a graded ranking reward, gives the best SID-only ranking and matches strong text-reading LLMs whose prompts are about 20x longer. It ranks above random with no history at all, where a caption-reading reference does not, and it keeps its gains at long histories, where text prompts pass 50k tokens and the caption-reading reference stops improving. In a paired rationale audit, this model argues trade-offs better than that reference but describes items less accurately. The common post-training methods we test thus elicit ranking from SIDs but not faithful item descriptions, which calls for post-training that improves the reasoning itself, not only the ranking it produces.

</details>


### [107] [Jev-as-a-Judge for Brazilian Portuguese Information Retrieval: Relevance Assessment and Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23346)
*Leandro Carísio Fernandes,Jayr Pereira*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究了Jev（一种System One模型）在信息检索中的相关性判断和重排序能力。实验表明，Jev生成的相关性判断与官方判断具有较高一致性（完全一致率52.9%-64.9%，Kappa 0.52-0.82），且产生的qrels能产生相似的系统排序（Kendall's tau 0.90-0.93）。作为重排序器，Jev显著提升了BM25基线的nDCG@10（+90.2%）和MRR（+31.8%），验证了其作为相关性评估器和重排序器的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索快速结构化决策模型Jev在信息检索任务中的应用潜力，特别是作为相关性判断生成器（Jev-as-a-judge）和重排序器的有效性，以减少人工标注成本并提升检索性能。

Method: 使用Jev模型对四个巴西葡萄牙语IR数据集生成相关性判断，与官方qrels对比一致性（完全一致率、二次加权Cohen's Kappa、相邻等级差异分析）；基于Jev生成的qrels评估检索系统排序的一致性（Kendall's tau）；在Quati测试集上将Jev作为重排序器对BM25 top-1000候选文档重排，对比nDCG@10和MRR指标。

Result: 相关性判断一致性：完全一致率52.9%-64.9%，Kappa 0.52-0.82，≥94.5%的判断完全一致或仅相差一级。系统排序一致性：Kendall's tau 0.90-0.93。重排序效果：nDCG@10从0.3329提升至0.6331（+90.2%），MRR从0.6852提升至0.9033（+31.8%）。

Conclusion: Jev作为相关性评估器能生成高质量判断，产生的qrels能可靠复现系统排序；作为重排序器能显著提升BM25基线性能，证明了其在信息检索中的实用价值。

Abstract: This study investigates Jev, a System One model designed for fast, structured decision-making, in information retrieval. We use Jev-as-a-judge to generate relevance judgments for four Brazilian Portuguese IR collections covering different domains and compare them with the official relevance judgments of each collection. We also evaluate Jev as a reranker. For relevance assessment, exact agreement ranged from 52.9% to 64.9%, while quadratic weighted Cohen's kappa ranged from 0.52 to 0.82. Most disagreements involved adjacent relevance levels, with at least 94.5% of annotations matching exactly or differing by only one relevance level. Despite differences at the individual query--document level, Jev-generated qrels produced similar retrieval system rankings, with Kendall's tau ranging from 0.90 to 0.93 for NormasTCU and Ulysses-RFCorpus across the two evaluation metrics. For reranking, Jev reordered the top 1,000 BM25 candidates for each of the 50 Quati test queries, increasing nDCG@10 from 0.3329 to 0.6331 (+90.2%) and MRR from 0.6852 to 0.9033 (+31.8%). These findings support Jev as a relevance assessor and show improved ranking effectiveness over BM25 in the evaluated reranking setting.

</details>


### [108] [QD-Rec: Open-Ended Evolution of Recommendations via Quality-Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23470)
*Takashi Ishida,Taro Makino,Issa Sugiura*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 QD-Rec，一种面向决策制定的开放式推荐方法，结合上下文多臂老虎机选择项目、基础模型生成假设，构建高质量多样化推荐集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统多输出单一排序列表，但在高风险决策（如并购、客户定位）中，决策者更需要多样化、有理由支撑的选项集合，以便比较权衡。

Method: QD-Rec 采用质量-多样性框架：用上下文多臂老虎机选择候选项目，用基础模型基于档案库中已有假设生成新假设，新推荐若新颖或优于最近邻则加入档案库。

Result: 在并购、客户定位、论文及书籍推荐四个任务上，QD-Rec 实现最高平均质量，且在多数设置下构建最大档案库。

Conclusion: 开放式推荐范式结合质量-多样性搜索与基础模型，能为复杂决策提供多样化、高质量且可解释的推荐集合，优于传统单一排序推荐。

Abstract: Open-ended search combined with foundation models has produced advances such as faster algorithms, new results on open mathematical problems, and self-improving agents. We extend this paradigm to recommendation for decision-making, focusing on decisions where the set of possible choices is fixed but the reasons for choosing them are not. In high-stakes decisions, e.g., which company to acquire or which customer to target, comparing diverse, well-reasoned options can be more useful than a single ranked list. We formalize open-ended recommendation as building a diverse, high-quality set of recommendations, each an item together with a hypothesis, a natural-language reason why the item fits the needs of a decision maker. We propose QD-Rec, a quality--diversity method that selects items with a contextual bandit and generates hypotheses with a foundation model from earlier ones kept in an archive as stepping stones. A new recommendation enters the archive if it is novel or better than its nearest neighbor. Across M&A, customer targeting, and paper and book recommendation, QD-Rec gives the highest mean quality and builds the largest archive in most settings.

</details>


### [109] [LSNR: Learned Sparse News Recommendations with Attentive User Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23510)
*David Vos,Andrew Yates,Maarten de Rijke*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出LSNR（Learned Sparse News Recommendations），解决STAR等稀疏推荐方法在新闻场景下的两大瓶颈：用户兴趣扁平化和长文本截断。LSNR独立编码每篇文章，通过注意力加权聚合稀疏表示并结合密集投影扩展构建用户向量。在MIND和EBNeRD数据集上显著优于STAR，在短历史场景下超越SOTA密集推荐器，且索引大小比未压缩密集基线小一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型稀疏推荐方法（如STAR）将用户交互历史拼接为单一查询字符串，在新闻推荐中导致两个瓶颈：1) 拼接操作将用户多样化的阅读兴趣压扁为单一平坦查询，丢失细粒度兴趣信息；2) 新闻文章为长文本，拼接多篇文章快速超出语言模型上下文窗口，强制截断降低质量。

Method: LSNR采用逐篇独立编码策略：每篇文章单独通过骨干语言模型编码生成稀疏表示；用户层面通过学习型注意力机制对各文章稀疏表示加权聚合，并结合密集投影扩展构建最终稀疏用户向量。此设计避免了拼接截断，保留了细粒度兴趣，并通过注意力实现动态兴趣融合。

Result: 在MIND和EBNeRD数据集实验中：1) LSNR显著优于STAR，增益在短历史设置（无截断影响）下依然成立；2) 与SOTA密集推荐器相比，标准设置下竞争力相当，短历史场景下超越密集方法；3) 消融实验证实注意力聚合优于简单池化替代方案；4) 索引大小比未压缩密集基线小一个数量级。

Conclusion: LSNR通过独立编码+注意力聚合+密集扩展的架构，有效解决了新闻推荐中的兴趣扁平化和长文本截断问题，在效果和索引效率上均取得显著提升，特别适合短历史冷启动场景，为学习型稀疏推荐在长文本领域的应用提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Current learned sparse approaches for recommender systems, such as STAR, represent a user by concatenating their interaction history into a single query string. For news, this creates two bottlenecks: concatenation collapses a user's distinct reading interests into one flat query, and, because news articles are long-form text, concatenating several news articles quickly overruns the context window of the language model used as backbone and forces quality-degrading truncation.
  To address both bottlenecks, we introduce learned sparse news recommendations (LSNR). LSNR encodes each article independently and constructs a sparse user vector by combining learned, attention-based weighting of the per-article sparse representations with a dense-projected expansion at the user level. Experiments on the MIND and EBNeRD datasets show that LSNR significantly outperforms STAR, with these gains holding even in short-history settings where truncation is not a factor. Compared to state-of-the-art dense recommenders, LSNR is competitive in standard settings and it outperforms them when the user history is short. An ablation of the user encoder confirms that learned attention-based aggregation outperforms simpler pooling alternatives. Beyond effectiveness, an additional advantage of LSNR is that it serves from an index an order of magnitude smaller than an uncompressed dense baseline.

</details>


### [110] [Humanity's Last Choice: A Common Evaluation Standard for Generative Engine Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23541)
*Qianfeng Wen,Yifan Simon Liu,Difan Jiao,Junda Wu,Di Bai,Jintao Liu,Xidong Wu,Zhenwei Tang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文探讨了人类最后的选择将是信任哪个AI系统来做决策，这些系统依赖外部源，内容提供商可通过生成引擎优化（GEO）影响推荐。现有基准测试虽有进展，但各自定义GEO策略空间和编辑约束，导致难以跨基准比较。作者呼吁建立通用评估标准，提出了元分类法描述源编辑的位置、方式和目标信号，并制定了受控比较协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统成为决策代理，用户需要可比较的证据来判断不同系统在面对GEO操纵时执行用户请求的能力。现有基准测试定义各异的策略空间和编辑约束，将优化算法的提升与编辑自由度的增大混淆，且评估覆盖面难以跨基准比较，阻碍了公平评估和排行榜建立。

Method: 基于代表性文献，构建元分类法（meta-taxonomy），从三个维度刻画GEO策略：源编辑位置、编辑方式、目标信号。将现有策略映射为可搜索空间，制定带显式覆盖报告的受控比较协议，为通用排行榜奠定基础。

Result: 建立了统一的GEO策略分类框架和标准化评估协议，使不同基准间的策略空间、编辑约束、覆盖面可显式比较，支持跨系统的公平评测和公共排行榜构建。

Conclusion: 通过元分类法和受控比较协议，为AI系统在GEO环境下的鲁棒性评估提供了通用标准，有助于用户基于可比较证据做出'最后的选择'——信任哪个AI系统。

Abstract: Humanity's Last Choice describes the idea that the last choice people need to make is which AI system they entrust with the decisions that follow. These systems rely on external sources that content providers can edit through generative engine optimization (GEO) to increase source visibility and influence recommendations. Choosing among these systems therefore requires comparable evidence of how well they carry out user requests when exposed to GEO. Benchmarks have made progress in standardizing tasks, models, and metrics, but they often define their own spaces of GEO strategies and use different editing constraints. These differences can conflate gains from better optimization algorithms with those from greater freedom to edit sources, while making evaluation coverage difficult to compare across benchmarks. We therefore call for a common evaluation standard and propose a meta-taxonomy that describes where sources are edited, how they are edited, and what signals those edits target. Drawing on representative literature, we map existing strategies into a searchable space and outline a protocol for controlled comparisons with explicit coverage reporting. This provides a foundation for common leaderboards that can inform which systems people choose to rely on.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [When is a large language model a good calibration agent? Physics-labeled prompting for budgeted engineering simulator calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22379)
*Ruo-Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨利用大语言模型（LLM）结合物理诊断描述来校准昂贵工程模拟器，提出混合两阶段工作流：LLM作为热启动后转交给传统优化器，在严格评估预算下显著优于单独使用LLM或传统优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程模拟器校准受限于高昂计算成本，现有无导数优化器收敛慢。研究旨在验证LLM是否能利用物理语义信息在极少评估次数下快速提出有效参数更新，解决小样本、高成本场景下的校准难题。

Method: 在圆柱尾流大涡模拟（LES）和阻尼振子反问题两个基准上，对比物理标注提示词与四种文本消融版本（移除、匿名化、填充、误导）及四种传统优化器（Nelder-Mead、贝叶斯优化、CMA-ES、随机搜索）。引入混合工作流：LLM前5次评估作为热启动，累积数据后切换到传统优化器进行数值延续。

Result: 物理信息LLM首次提议在25次试验中23次改进初始设计；5次评估后中位校准误差比无物理对照低21.9倍。但优势随评估增加而消失（15次后消失），LLM解码变异性导致9.8倍性能差异。混合两阶段策略仅需9次模拟运行达目标精度，优于单独LLM（12次）、Nelder-Mead（17次）、贝叶斯优化（>60次）。灵敏度分析验证测试用例有效性。

Conclusion: 物理标注LLM作为校准代理在极低评估预算下展现强语义优势，但存在瞬态性和变异性。混合热启动+传统优化器的数值延续策略有效融合语义引导与数值鲁棒性，为昂贵模拟器校准提供高效实用范式。

Abstract: Calibrating expensive engineering simulators requires tuning model parameters to match reference data, a process heavily constrained by computational costs. This study investigates whether Large Language Models (LLMs), conditioned on physical diagnostic descriptions of simulation errors, can propose informative parameter updates more efficiently than conventional derivative-free optimizers. We test this on a cylinder-wake Large-Eddy Simulation and a damped-oscillator inverse problem. Physics-labeled prompts are evaluated against four text ablations (removed, anonymized, padded, misdirected) and standard optimizers (Nelder-Mead, Bayesian optimization, CMA-ES, and random search). Results demonstrate a strong early semantic advantage: the first physics-informed LLM proposal improves the initial design in 23 of 25 trials. After five evaluations, the median calibration error is 21.9 times lower than physics-free controls. However, this advantage is transient - disappearing by fifteen evaluations - and inherent LLM decoder variability yields a 9.8-fold performance spread. To address this, we introduce a hybrid workflow that uses the LLM as a warm-start, passing accumulated evaluations to a conventional optimizer. This two-stage numerical continuation policy reaches target accuracy in just 9 simulator runs, outperforming the LLM alone (12 runs), Nelder-Mead (17 runs), and Bayesian optimization (>60 runs). Supported by sensitivity analysis validating the test cases, this hybrid physics-labeled LLM scheme proves highly effective as a calibration agent under strict evaluation budgets.

</details>


### [112] [Trust but Verify: Designing Region-Inhomogeneous Forward Dynamics for Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22380)
*Gunmay Jhingran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种改进的离散语言扩散模型，通过结合吸收核和均匀混合核并引入区域依赖的腐蚀率，使模型在训练时能识别上下文中的不确定token，推理时通过启发式采样器重新注入噪声并冻结上下文更新。在170M参数模型上实验显示，该方法能有效检测并修正均匀随机腐蚀（召回率0.96，精确率0.91），显著提升填补任务F1分数（10%腐蚀+3.8，20%腐蚀+6.4），且不损害干净条件下的生成质量（误编辑率<0.4%）。对于语义合理的腐蚀提升较弱（+0.5 F1，不显著）。研究揭示了可检测性与破坏性取决于偏离数据条件的程度，并将吸收扩散解释为信任轴的无限对比极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离散语言扩散模型多采用吸收腐蚀（absorbing corruption），将上下文视为完全可信的ground truth，导致信任机制二元化：被腐蚀的上下文会被直接复制为真值，错误传播到生成片段。作者希望重新开放这一设计空间，引入一种连续的信任机制，使模型能够区分可靠上下文与可疑上下文，从而在训练和推理中更鲁棒地处理噪声上下文。

Method: 1. 前向过程设计：结合吸收核（针对缺失位置）与均匀混合核（针对上下文位置），采用区域依赖的腐蚀率。缺失位置遵循吸收动力学；上下文位置以低速率进行随机替换，使模型在训练时暴露于观测但不确定的token。2. 推理采样器：启发式采样器在退火强度下向可疑上下文重新注入噪声，并在轨迹后期冻结上下文更新。3. 在预训练的170M参数掩码扩散模型上进行8000步微调，单张6GB显存笔记本GPU耗时<5小时。

Result: 基础模型对观测token（即使是均匀随机垃圾）赋予约50%概率。微调后：均匀随机腐蚀下检测召回率0.96、精确率0.91；填补token-F1在10%腐蚀提升3.8点、20%提升6.4点，消除了腐蚀邻域的准确率下降；干净条件无可测回归，误编辑率<0.4%。语义合理腐蚀下提升仅约0.5 F1点，无统计显著性。实验表明可检测性与破坏性联合取决于偏离数据条件分布的程度：邻近噪声难检测且语义温和，远距噪声显眼且破坏大。

Conclusion: 吸收扩散可视为信任轴的无限对比极限，类比扩散修复中已知/未知区域的信噪gap。在单文本域、170M模型设定下，通过修改前向动力学而非架构即可达到中间信任水平。该方法为离散扩散中的上下文信任建模提供了新范式，但在处理语义合理的腐蚀时仍有局限。

Abstract: Continuous diffusion treats the forward process as a design space; discrete language diffusion often uses absorbing corruption, leaving context fully trusted throughout generation. This makes trust binary: corrupted context is copied as ground truth, propagating errors into generated spans. We reopen this design space by combining absorbing and uniform-mixing kernels with region-dependent rates. Missing positions follow absorbing dynamics, while context undergoes low-rate random substitution, exposing the model to observed but uncertain tokens during training. At inference, a heuristic sampler re-injects noise into suspicious context under an annealed intensity and freezes context updates late in the trajectory. An 8,000-step finetune of a pretrained 170M-parameter masked diffusion model takes under five hours on a 6 GB laptop GPU. The base model assigns roughly 50% probability to observed tokens even when they are uniform-random garbage. For uniform-random corruption, finetuning detects corrupted context with 0.96 recall and 0.91 precision, improves infill token-F1 by 3.8 points at 10% corruption and 6.4 points at 20%, and removes the accuracy dip next to corruptions. Clean conditioning shows no measurable regression, with a false-edit rate below 0.4%. Gains for plausible corruptions are weaker: about 0.5 F1 points at 10% corruption, without statistical significance. Our results suggest that detectability and damage depend jointly on departure from the data conditional: nearby noise is hard to detect and semantically mild, while distant noise is conspicuous and destructive. We interpret absorbing diffusion as the infinite-contrast limit of a trust axis, analogous to the signal-to-noise gap between known and unknown regions in diffusion inpainting. In our setting of a 170M model and a single text domain, intermediate trust is reached by changing forward dynamics rather than architecture.

</details>


### [113] [Binding-Specific Relearning Savings for Test-Time Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22381)
*Zhe Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究语言模型在持续学习中的“重学习优势”（relearning savings），发现当前行为准确率会低估先前经验的持久效应。在Qwen2.5-1.5B上通过三组对照实验（LEARNED/CONTROL/WRONG）证明：先前学过的事实绑定在遗忘后重新习得更快，且该优势经预重学习行为校正后依然显著，先前学习使恢复所需优化器更新步数减少约18%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习评估主要依赖当前输出准确率，但这无法捕捉先前经验如何塑造后续更新。核心问题是：当事实难以从模型中激发时，模型是真正“丢失”了知识，还是仅变得“不可访问”？作者旨在测试学习特定事实绑定是否能产生超越实体熟悉度、答案词汇、格式或查询槽位熟悉度的重学习优势。

Method: 在Qwen2.5-1.5B上设计三组匹配、平衡的历史条件：LEARNED接收正确绑定，CONTROL接收不含该绑定的匹配曝光，WRONG接收同一槽位的竞争性关系有效答案。随后进行相同的无排练持续微调流，在预设的部分遗忘检查点测量重学习速度。次要分析使用未配对的未见池，描述性转换测量恢复所需优化器更新量。

Result: LEARNED在部分遗忘检查点处比CONTROL更快重新习得目标绑定。WRONG相对CONTROL无正向优势，与LEARNED的对比提供槽位匹配的佐证（可能受竞争干扰）。优势在校正测量的预重学习行为后依然为正。次要描述性转换显示，先前学习使恢复所需优化器更新减少约18%。

Conclusion: 当前行为准确率会低估先前获取经验的持久效应。模型对事实的“遗忘”更多表现为可访问性下降而非知识彻底丢失，重学习优势（savings）是这种潜在保留的证据。这对持续学习评估和模型编辑等任务有重要启示。

Abstract: When a fact becomes harder to elicit from a language model, has the model lost it, or has it merely become less accessible? Parameter-updating test-time continual learning agents are usually evaluated by current output accuracy; we ask whether that measurement captures how prior experience shapes later updates. We test whether learning a specific factual binding produces a later relearning advantage beyond familiarity with its entity, answer vocabulary, format, or query slot. In Qwen2.5-1.5B, we compare three matched, counterbalanced histories: LEARNED receives the correct binding, CONTROL receives matched exposure without it, and WRONG receives a competing relation-valid answer in the same slot. After an identical rehearsal-free continual fine-tuning stream, LEARNED reacquires the target faster than CONTROL at a prespecified checkpoint of partial forgetting. WRONG shows no positive advantage over CONTROL, while its comparison with LEARNED provides slot-matched corroboration, subject to possible competitor interference. The advantage remains positive after adjustment for measured pre-relearning behavior. In a secondary descriptive conversion using an unpaired never-seen pool, prior learning reduces the optimizer updates required for restoration by about 18%. Current behavioral accuracy can therefore underestimate the lasting effect of previously acquired experience. Code is available at https://github.com/zhezhou1106/relearning-savings

</details>


### [114] [CGDT-CESN: Correlation-Guided and Dynamics-Trained Clustered Echo State Network for Multivariate Time-Series Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22430)
*Hariharan Srinivasan,Masoom Bhargava,Sayan Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于相关性引导和动态训练的聚类回声状态网络(CGDT-CESN)，用于多变量时间序列预测。该模型采用模块化储备库架构，每个聚类专门处理一个变量，利用变量间的经验相关性建立簇间连接，并训练这些连接权重以捕捉时变跨变量依赖关系。在推特信息级联演化和太阳风预测两个不同数据集上，CGDT-CESN优于传统ESN，且在多预测步长和少量数据下均保持性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确预测多变量时间序列需要模型同时捕捉变量特定的动态特性和跨变量交互。传统回声状态网络(ESN)在处理多变量序列时，难以有效建模变量间复杂的时变依赖关系，且缺乏数据驱动的耦合机制。

Method: 提出CGDT-CESN模型：1) 模块化储备库架构，每个聚类专门处理一个变量；2) 基于经验观测到的变量间相关性建立簇间连接，提供数据驱动的耦合机制；3) 除常规读出层训练外，还训练由相关性指导的簇间连接权重，使储备库能捕捉时变跨变量依赖而不牺牲计算效率。

Result: 在推特信息级联演化和太阳风预测两个本质不同的数据集上，CGDT-CESN的预测性能优于传统ESN。性能提升在多个预测步长下均保持，即使在数据量较少时也表现良好。

Conclusion: CGDT-CESN通过将聚类特定的专业化与学习到的跨变量耦合相结合，为多变量时间序列预测提供了一条 principled 的数据驱动路径，有效平衡了变量特定动态建模与跨变量交互捕捉。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of multivariate time series requires models that simultaneously capture variable-specific dynamics and cross-variable interactions. In this work, a Correlation-Guided and Dynamics-Trained Clustered Echo State Network (CGDT-CESN) is proposed, which comprises a modular reservoir architecture with each cluster distinctly specializing in one of the variables in the multivariate time series. An innovative reservoir design is proposed in which inter-cluster connections are enabled by empirically observed correlations among variables, providing a data-driven coupling mechanism. Apart from conventional readout training, inter-cluster connection weights informed by correlations are trained, which in turn allows the reservoir to capture time-varying cross-variable dependencies without sacrificing computational efficiency and further improves forecasting accuracy. The prediction performance of CGDT-CESN is found to be superior to that of the conventional ESN when evaluated on two fundamentally different datasets: Twitter information cascade evolution and solar wind forecasting. Also, the improvement in performance is preserved across multiple prediction horizons, even when presented with a low volume of data. By unifying cluster-specific specialization with learned inter-variable coupling, CGDT-CESN offers a principled, data-driven route to multivariate time series prediction.

</details>


### [115] [Tool Recommendation for Multi-Step Agents: Finding Every Tool a Task Needs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23707)
*Sam Partee,River Stevens,Thierry Damiba,Valerie Fanelle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个名为Tool Recommendation的部署系统，解决了多步骤代理任务因缺少单一工具而失败的问题，该系统通过一个固定的三工具接口，在代理与工具之间的网关层运行，为代理提供海量工具（8200+）的访问，同时在用户凭证下执行并检查同意授权。实验表明，该系统在ToolRet检索基准上比BM25更频繁地将所需工具排在前十（+57%），在多步工作流中首次推荐即覆盖所需工具93%，并显著减少了输入令牌（Codex -17%，Claude Code -40%）和总费用（最高-40%），且不影响任务完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理任务在所需工具缺失时会失败，但平台上的工具数量巨大（约8200个），其定义总长度超过了任何评估模型的上下文窗口，无法一次性全部提供给代理。商业工具搜索通常仅提供词法排序（如BM25），效果有限。因此需要一种机制，在不超出上下文限制的情况下，为代理高效推荐所需工具，并确保安全（用户凭证、同意授权）。

Method: 构建Tool Recommendation系统，部署在代理与工具平台的网关层。系统暴露仅三个固定工具接口，内部维护完整工具库。每次代理调用时，系统在用户自身凭证下执行，并根据用户对各服务的同意授权及代理可用工具策略进行检查。采用检索增强推荐方法，将查询映射到工具集合，返回顶部候选工具。评估包括公开检索基准ToolRet和真实计费的端到端会话成本。

Result: 在ToolRet基准上，Tool Recommendation将查询所需工具置于前十的频率比BM25高57%，性能与参数量大9倍的已发布系统相当。在成对多步工作流中，首次推荐即覆盖所有必需工具的比例达93%（Codex会话）。与固定100工具列表相比，输入令牌分别减少17%（Codex）和40%（Claude Code），14次运行总费用均下降，最高降幅40%，任务完成率无变化。

Conclusion: Tool Recommendation通过网关层的固定三工具接口，有效解决了大规模工具库超出上下文窗口的问题，实现了高精度的工具推荐、显著的令牌与成本节省，且保持了任务完成率和安全性（用户凭证与同意检查）。该系统已实际部署，证明了在真实生产环境中大规模工具推荐的可行性与价值。

Abstract: A multi-step agent task fails when a single tool it needs is missing, yet the tools an agent may need are too many to list: the roughly 8,200 tools on the platform we study have definitions that together exceed the context window of any model we evaluate. We present Tool Recommendation, a deployed system that gives an agent all of them, hundreds of times more than a typical MCP server exposes, through a fixed interface of three tools. It runs at the gateway between agent and tools, executing every call under the end user's own credentials and checking it against the user's consent for each service and the tools the agent may use. We evaluate it on both a public retrieval benchmark and billed end-to-end session cost. On ToolRet, Tool Recommendation places every tool a query needs in the top ten 57% more often than BM25, a lexical ranking that commercial tool search offers, and compares favorably with published systems up to nine times its size. In paired multi-step workflows, its first recommendation covered every required tool in 93% of Codex sessions, and against a fixed list of 100 tools it cut input tokens by 17% with Codex and 40% with Claude Code and the total bill in all 14 runs, by up to 40%, with no change in task completion.

</details>


### [116] [BOLT: Multi-Bottleneck Bound-Guided Learning for Flexible Job-Shop Scheduling with Time Lags and Resource-Coupled Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22433)
*Ziheng Zhang,Wei Zhang,Yunyi Zhao,Zuming Liu,Wei Zhou,Boon Ping Gan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BOLT 通过基于松弛的下界构建奖励信号，实现了面向运输耦合柔性作业车间调度的在线学习调度，显著优于基于惩罚的策略和调度规则，且能快速重规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决运输耦合的柔性作业车间调度问题，其中稀缺车队在工位间移动工件，调度决定运输等待。现有方法如惩罚策略不够有效，需要一种能利用下界指导决策并提供子最优性保证的方法。

Method: BOLT 基于链松弛、机器产能松弛和车队产能松弛构建下界。下界在终止时等于完工时间，其逐步变化形成奖励信号，奖励之和为常数减去完工时间。由于下界从不超过可达成的完工时间，每个候选动作的下界增量即为完工时间下界的已证上升，策略将其视为价格。根下界可在无求解器情况下认证完整调度的子最优性差距。

Result: 在 10 模块预制建筑实例上，显式运输耦合在装载移动占车队时间≥66% 的所有测试单元中优于惩罚策略，在 ≤56% 时则不优。在五个 50/80 模块零样本生产批次中，BOLT 在四个批次上优于最佳调度规则对，在三个长距离移动批次上优于惩罚策略。在三车辆 80 模块批次中，平均完工时间比最佳规则对低 13.0%，比一小时预算 CP 求解器低 4.2%（后者已证差距平均 34%）。决策延迟 2.3-5.9 ms/事件（单核），干扰重规划仅需秒级。

Conclusion: BOLT 通过下界导向学习有效解决运输耦合调度，提供理论保证的子最优性认证，在高运输负载场景下显著优于基线，且具备实时决策和快速重规划能力，适合工业实际部署。

Abstract: Flexible job-shop scheduling becomes resource-coupled when a scarce vehicle fleet moves jobs between stations, because the schedule sets the transport waits. BOLT (bound-oriented learning for transport-coupled scheduling) builds a lower bound from chain, machine-capacity, and fleet-capacity relaxations. The bound equals the makespan at termination, so its per-step changes form a reward that sums to a constant minus the makespan. Because it never exceeds the attainable makespan, each candidate's bound increase is a proven rise of the makespan floor, which the policy reads as a price. The root bound certifies a completed schedule's suboptimality gap without a solver. On industry-motivated 10-module prefabricated-construction instances, explicit transport coupling outperforms a transport-penalty policy in every tested cell where loaded moves occupy at least 66% of fleet time and in none at or below 56%. Across five zero-shot 50- and 80-module production batches, BOLT outperforms the best dispatching-rule pair on four and the penalty policy on the three with long moves. In the three-vehicle 80-module batch, its mean makespan is 13.0% below the best pair and 4.2% below an hour-budget constraint-programming solver whose proven gap averages 34%. BOLT decides in 2.3-5.9 ms per event on one processor core and re-plans a disruption in seconds.

</details>


### [117] [TRACE: Transformer Residual Activation Codebook Encoding for Communication-efficient Distributed Edge Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22435)
*Muhammad Azlan Qazi,Alexandros Iosifidis,Qi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Trace 框架，通过在 Transformer 每层后注意力残差处放置向量量化器 (VQ) 并预计算代码本索引的键值表示，实现分布式边缘设备上 Transformer 推理的通信-计算重叠，显著降低通信开销并提升推理速度，同时支持运行时自适应比特率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式 Transformer 推理在协同边缘设备上受限于通信带宽，序列并行分区间的激活交换主导推理延迟。现有压缩方案虽降低通信量，但接收端仍需执行键/值投影才能进行注意力计算，导致计算串行化在通信之后，形成结构性瓶颈。

Method: Trace 在每层 Transformer 后注意力残差处引入向量量化器 (VQ)，预计算代码本索引对应的键/值表示。推理时将交换的激活编码为紧凑的代码本索引，接收方通过查表直接恢复键值，省去下游投影计算，从而实现通信与计算的重叠。进一步采用层次聚类将代码本构建为多分辨率层级，支持无需重训练的运行时比特率自适应。提供两种运行变体：一类优先精度，另一类通过缓冲区兼容量化平衡精度与效率。

Result: 在 ViT-Base (ImageNet-1K、CIFAR-100) 和 ViViT-B (UCF-101) 上实验表明，Trace 相比最先进的基于 VQ 的分布式推理框架，准确率最高提升 1.89%，端到端推理加速最高 1.27 倍；相比未压缩的序列并行推理，通信开销最高降低 22,624 倍。

Conclusion: Trace 通过在残差路径引入 VQ 并预计算键值查表，消除了接收端的下游计算依赖，实现了通信-计算重叠，有效解决了分布式 Transformer 推理的通信瓶颈。层次化代码本设计赋予了运行时比特率自适应能力，在保持甚至提升精度的同时大幅降低通信量并加速推理，为资源受限边缘设备上的大模型部署提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Distributed Transformer inference across co-located edge devices is fundamentally communication-bound, as exchanging activations between sequence-parallel partitions dominates inference latency under commodity edge bandwidths. Existing compression schemes reduce this cost but retain a structural bottleneck: the received data must still undergo key/value projection before attention can proceed, serializing computation behind communication. This paper presents Transformer Residual Activation Codebook Encoding (Trace), a framework that addresses these limitations. Trace places a Vector Quantizer (VQ) at the post-attention residual of every Transformer layer and precomputes codebook-indexed key/value representations. During inference, exchanged activations are encoded into compact codebook indices, allowing receivers to directly recover keys and values through table lookup instead of executing the downstream computations required to derive them. By eliminating the received data downstream computation, Trace enables communication--computation overlap and reduces a key latency bottleneck in distributed Transformer inference. A hierarchical clustering further structures the learned codebook into a multi-resolution hierarchy, enabling runtime adaptation of the communication bitrate without retraining. The framework supports two operating variants: one prioritizing accuracy and the other balancing accuracy and efficiency through buffer-compatible quantization. Experiments on ViT-Base (ImageNet-1K and CIFAR-100) and ViViT-B (UCF-101) show that Trace achieves up to 1.89% higher accuracy and up to 1.27x end-to-end inference speedup compared with the state-of-the-art VQ-based distributed inference framework. Compared with uncompressed sequence-parallel inference, Trace reduces communication overhead by up to 22,624x.

</details>


### [118] [Generative adversarial networks with Monte Carlo dropout uncertainty quantification for structural health monitoring: Fibre Bragg grating data augmentation comparison in Hawk T1A aircraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22436)
*Alvaro Peiro,Marios Impraimakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于航空航天结构的新型数据增强框架，结合条件生成对抗网络和蒙特卡洛Dropout不确定性量化，实现了随机信号合成与不确定性估计，并在全尺寸BAE Systems Hawk T1A飞机基准数据集上验证，证明其能准确捕捉结构动力学特性，为结构健康监测提供可靠的数字孪生组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决航空航天结构健康监测中实测数据稀缺、噪声大、不确定性高的问题，通过生成物理真实、统计一致的合成信号，增强有监督损伤分类工作流的鲁棒性，支撑安全关键系统的弹性决策。

Method: 1. 利用条件生成对抗网络学习类条件下的时域与频域结构响应特征；2. 引入蒙特卡洛Dropout作为贝叶斯近似进行不确定性量化；3. 从时域、频域、不确定性、变异性、特征域多维度严格对比生成信号与实测数据；4. 评估合成数据是否应纳入有监督损伤分类流程。

Result: 模型能准确捕捉主导结构动力学，基于不确定性特性指导信号用于分类改进，提供与信号复杂度和激励特性相关的有意义不确定性估计。在加速度、光纤布拉格光栅应变及非线性动力学响应测量上均表现出高保真度。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种现实的生成建模方案，可作为数据驱动结构健康监测的稳健数字孪生组件，支持安全关键航空航天系统状态评估的弹性决策。

Abstract: The signal generation capabilities of a novel data augmentation framework are examined in aerospace structures. First, the framework learns class-conditional temporal and spectral characteristics of the measured structural responses. It uses conditional generative adversarial network training and Monte Carlo dropout uncertainty quantification as a Bayesian approximation. This step enables stochastic signal synthesis and uncertainty quantification. Second, the generated responses are rigorously compared against experimental measurements across time, frequency, uncertainty, variability, and feature domains to assess physical realism, statistical consistency, and distributional fidelity. Finally, the synthetic data are evaluated to determine whether they should be incorporated into a supervised damage classification workflow. The framework is validated on the full-scale benchmark dataset from a BAE Systems Hawk T1A aircraft tested at the University of Sheffield's Structural Dynamics Laboratory for Verification and Validation. The test involves acceleration, fibre Bragg grating strain, and nonlinear dynamic response measurements. The results demonstrate that the model accurately captures dominant structural dynamics, it guides on signal use for classification improvement based on uncertainty characteristics, and it provides meaningful uncertainty estimates linked to signal complexity and excitation characteristics. Importantly, the framework offers a realistic generative modelling plan as a robust digital twin component for data-driven structural health monitoring, supporting resilient decision-making for condition assessment in safety-critical aerospace systems.

</details>


### [119] [CF-NODE: Counterfactual Spatial Node Intervention for Test-Time Adaptation Under Domain Shift, Evaluated Across Five Diabetic Retinopathy Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22437)
*Sabarish G,Deepak Parashar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 CF-NODE，一种基于反事实干预的测试时自适应框架，用于跨域糖尿病视网膜病变分级，无需目标域数据或模型更新，单图独立自适应，在五个外部数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有源域训练模型在不同相机、诊所、患者群体间泛化能力差；现有测试时自适应依赖熵最小化或批量目标信息，易强化错误预测且无法处理独立到达的临床图像。

Method: CF-NODE 利用模型注意力、特征幅度和节点集相似度对内部空间节点排序，逐步衰减最高秩节点，基于期望分级响应的稳定性对预测分级分布进行有界校准；全冻结模型，仅使用几何视图和固定源域先验，单图独立自适应，无需目标域信息或模型更新。

Result: 在五个外部 DR 数据集上，CF-NODE 显著优于 TENT、EATA、SAR、T3A，每个目标域 AUC 均提升，平均 AUC 和宏 F1 最高，实现更强的分级判别力和更平衡的严重程度识别。

Conclusion: 反事实干预敏感性为单图临床推理提供了一致且可部署的域鲁棒性，解决了跨域 DR 分级中测试时自适应的关键挑战。

Abstract: Automated fundus-based diabetic retinopathy (DR) grading can substantially expand screening capacity, but source-trained models degrade sharply across different cameras, clinics, and patient populations. Existing test-time adaptation methods depend on entropy minimization or target information accumulated from image batches and streams. These mechanisms can reinforce confidently incorrect predictions and break down when clinical images arrive independently. We introduce CF-NODE, a novel counterfactual test-time adaptation framework for cross- domain DR grading. Instead of trusting prediction confidence or estimating a target distribution, CF-NODE asks how each prediction responds under a controlled intervention on the evidence supporting it. CF-NODE ranks internal spatial nodes using model-derived attention, feature magnitude, and node-set similarity, progressively attenuates the highest-ranked nodes, and uses the stability of the resulting expected-grade response to apply a bounded calibration of the predicted grade distribution. The framework is fully frozen and adapts every image independently using only geometric views and a fixed source-derived prior, requiring no target-derived information or model updates. We evaluate CF-NODE on five external DR datasets under repeated-seed evaluation against TENT, EATA, SAR, and T3A. CF-NODE outperforms all four methods with statistically significant improvements over the second-best method, improves AUC on every target domain, and attains the highest average AUC and macro-F1, delivering stronger grade discrimination together with more balanced recognition across DR severity levels. These results demonstrate that counterfactual intervention sensitivity provides consistent and deployment-ready domain robustness for single-image clinical inference.

</details>


### [120] [Rank Measures of Fine-Tuning Updates Need a Noise Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22438)
*Muhammad Usman Altaf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究微调对语言模型权重更新ΔW的秩特性，发现常用秩度量需引入噪声基线，且对齐数据并非导致更新结构的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常用秩度量总结微调权重更新，但缺乏适当的基线对比，导致对微调本质及对齐效果的理解可能存在偏差。

Method: 将微调更新的秩度量与同形状随机矩阵、预训练权重、无对齐内容的匹配微调进行对比；在13个高达8B参数的已发布模型对上验证；测试指令数据、Wikipedia、代码、DPO偏好标签真实与随机交换、打乱文本等不同训练数据的影响；评估有效秩变化对模型性能的影响。

Result: 方向计数类秩度量显示微调更新仅略低于噪声；稳定秩显示其远低于噪声——二者皆对：更新包含少数强方向叠加长尾。该模式在所有模型对中一致。指令数据不比Wikipedia或代码突出；DPO在真实偏好标签与半随机交换标签下产生相同更新；打乱文本反而产生最集中的更新。随机更新同规模下几乎不改变有效秩却破坏模型；基于更新有效秩的编辑在测试中无可测效果。

Conclusion: 权重更新的秩度量必须引入噪声基线；关于对齐的主张需要无对齐内容的对照实验。微调更新的结构特性并非对齐独有，有效秩变化不足以刻画后训练的实质性影响。

Abstract: Studies of what fine-tuning does to a language model often summarise the weight update $ΔW$ by its rank. We compare the rank measures used for this against the same measures on a random matrix of the same shape, and against two harder references: the pretrained weights, and matched fine-tunes without alignment content. Measures that count directions put our fine-tuning updates only a little below noise; stable rank, computed from the same singular values, puts them far below. Both are right: an update has a few strong directions on top of a long tail. The pattern holds for thirteen released pairs up to 8B parameters, and it is not a mark of alignment: with training held fixed, instruction data does not stand out from Wikipedia or code, DPO gives the same update whether its preference labels are real or half swapped at random (one model, modest preference strength), and scrambled text gives the most concentrated update of all. Applied to a recent claim that post-training is superficial because the effective rank of the weights barely changes, the baselines show that a random update of the same size leaves that rank almost unchanged while wrecking the model, and that an edit built on the effective rank of the update has no measurable effect in our tests. Rank measures of weight updates need a noise baseline, and claims about alignment need a control without alignment content.

</details>


### [121] [Sparse Autoencoders Can Induce Local Geometry for Mitigating Catastrophic Forgetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22439)
*Kamil Faber,Katarzyna Filus,Łukasz Pawela*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Sparse Autoencoder Geometric Anchoring (SAGA) 方法，通过在任务边界训练的稀疏自编码器 (SAE) 导出的局部低秩几何约束来限制表示漂移，实现了在三个预训练 ViT-B/16 基准上最高的 Class-IL 平均准确率和最低的平均遗忘率，分别比最强基线提升 12.15、4.79 和 3.22 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决监督持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题：适应新任务会导致表示漂移和先前学习信息的丢失。核心挑战在于确定应约束哪些表示变化，同时保持可塑性。

Method: SAGA 方法：1) 在每个任务边界训练 SAE；2) 将 SAE 转换为表示漂移的局部低秩约束；3) 使用回放样本指定约束应用位置；4) 利用编码器雅可比矩阵的前几个右奇异方向指定惩罚哪些变化；5) 生成的几何结构依赖于输入，且无需保留 SAE 即可用于后续任务训练。理论上建立了与潜在代码匹配的局部关系、谱截断误差和冻结读出层漂移的条件界。

Result: 在三个预训练 ViT-B/16 基准上，配合回放和快照历史，SAGA 达到最高平均 Class-IL 准确率和最低平均遗忘率，分别在 CIFAR-100、CUB-200、ImageNet-R 上比最强传统基线（ER、DER++、ER-ACE、SGD）提升 12.15、4.79、3.22 个百分点。从头训练结果较混合，秩消融和几何控制实验表明 SAE 诱导的低秩几何提供了有用的稳定性-可塑性权衡。

Conclusion: SAGA 通过 SAE 导出的输入相关低秩几何约束有效缓解了灾难性遗忘，在预训练模型上显著优于现有基线，且无需在后续任务中保留 SAE。该方法为稳定性-可塑性困境提供了新的几何视角和实用的权衡机制。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting remains a challenge in supervised continual learning, as adapting to new tasks causes representation drift and loss of previously learned information. This raises the question of which representational changes should be constrained while preserving plasticity. We therefore introduce Sparse Autoencoder Geometric Anchoring (SAGA), which converts a Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) trained at each task boundary into local, low-rank constraints on representation drift. Replay examples specify where the constraints apply, while leading right singular directions of the encoder Jacobian specify which changes are penalized. The resulting geometry is input dependent and can be retained without keeping the SAE for subsequent tasks' training. We establish its local relation to latent code matching, define its spectral truncation error, and derive a conditional bound on frozen-readout drift. With replay and snapshot history, the method achieves the highest mean Class-IL accuracy and lowest mean forgetting among the evaluated ER, DER++, ER-ACE, and SGD baselines on three pretrained ViT-B/16 benchmarks, with accuracy gains of 12.15, 4.79, and 3.22 percentage points over the strongest conventional baseline on CIFAR-100, CUB-200, and ImageNet-R, respectively. From scratch results are more mixed, while rank ablations and geometry controls show that SAE-induced low-rank geometry offers useful stability-plasticity trade-offs.

</details>


### [122] [Lean4AgenticRL: Advancing Agentic RL with Verifiable Multi-level Process Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22448)
*Ruida Wang,Gerald Friedland,Tong Zhang,Chris Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Lean4AgenticRL框架，首次将多层级过程奖励建立在Lean4形式化模式上，用于开放式智能体强化学习，训练出LAR-9B/27B模型，在终端基准测试中显著超越同规模模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型通过大规模智能体强化学习增强能力，但主要依赖高层结果奖励，忽视低层工具调用和中层工作流阶段奖励，导致训练效果有限。

Method: Lean4AgenticRL构建问题特定工作流系统平衡智能体能力与可控性，通过工作流形式化和多层级奖励标注在通用工具链中验证智能体行为，采用工作流阶段归一化GRPO算法进行多层级奖励强化学习。

Result: 训练的LAR-27B在TerminalBench-2.1达到77.3%，平均比基础模型提升9.89%，超越同规模第二优模型5.07%。

Conclusion: 将形式化方法融入开放式智能体强化学习具有巨大潜力，多层级过程奖励显著提升智能体在软件工程和终端任务上的表现。

Abstract: Enhancing the agentic capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) to tackle complex software engineering (SWE) and general terminal-usage problems is a pressing challenge. Current state-of-the-art LLMs often go through large-scale agentic Reinforcement Learning (RL). However, they mostly rely on high-level outcome rewards, with minimal consideration of low-level toolcall and mid-level workflow-stage rewards, which makes the training less effective. To overcome this limitation, we propose **Lean4AgenticRL**, which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first framework that grounds multi-level process rewards in the unified Lean4 formalization schema for open-ended agentic RL. **Lean4AgenticRL** first builds a *problem-specific workflow* system that balances the agent's own capability and controllability during execution, laying the foundation for further verification. Subsequently, our workflow formalization and multi-level reward annotation methods enable agent behavior verification within a general harness system. It provides multi-level rewards to support more effective agentic RL with the workflow-stage-normalized GRPO algorithm. Using **Lean4AgenticRL**, we train **LAR-9B/27B**, a family of models with enhanced agentic capability. Extensive experiments indicate that our model outperforms the leading model of similar size. **LAR-27B** achieves **77.3%** on TerminalBench-2.1. On average, our models outperform the base model by **9.89%** and surpass the second-best model of comparable size we know by **5.07%**. These results indicate the potential of integrating formal methods in enhancing open-ended agentic RL.

</details>


### [123] [Progressive Misalignment Can Emerge from Benign Recursive Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22467)
*Karim Abdel Sadek,Federico Di Gennaro,Fanny Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现递归自蒸馏会导致安全模型逐渐变得不安全，即使训练提示是良性的，也会出现“渐进式错位”现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自蒸馏作为一种通过模型自身输出提升能力的方法备受关注，但其安全性质知之甚少。作者旨在探究递归自蒸馏过程中是否存在安全失效模式。

Method: 在 Qwen3 和 Phi-4 模型族上进行实验，使用 SORRY-Bench 和 StrongREJECT 基准测试。设置递归自蒸馏流程：每一轮以安全模型为教师，在良性提示或模拟用户反馈下生成数据，再用这些数据蒸馏下一代模型。同时采用基于梯度的方法搜索能诱发错位的良性提示。

Result: 实验观察到 Progressive Misalignment：安全模型经多轮递归自蒸馏后，在多样化有害查询上逐渐变得不安全。该现象在固定良性提示和模拟良性用户反馈两种设置下均复现。梯度搜索还发现，无需有害训练提示即可触发此失效模式。

Conclusion: 递归自蒸馏存在固有的安全风险，即使训练数据完全由安全模型在良性条件下生成，也可能导致模型逐步错位。呼吁后续研究更系统地审视基于自身数据递归训练的 AI 系统的安全性。

Abstract: Training models on their own outputs has attracted widespread attention as a possible route to advance AI capabilities. One prominent approach is self-distillation, where a model is trained to match the token-level predictions it would have produced when given access to additional context, such as a cue, feedback, or instruction. However, the safety properties of this training procedure are poorly understood. In this paper, we present a surprising safety failure of recursive self-distillation, where each self-distilled model becomes the teacher for the next round of training. We observe that when a safe model is recursively self-distilled on its own (safe) generations while receiving an additional non-harmful cue, it becomes increasingly misaligned across a diverse set of harmful queries; we call this Progressive Misalignment. We show this failure mode in both the Qwen3 and Phi-4 model families on SORRY-Bench and StrongREJECT. Progressive misalignment also occurs in a more realistic setup, where the fixed cue is replaced by feedback from a simulated user with benign intent. Using a gradient-based method, we are also able to find benign prompts that lead to progressive misalignment when applying recursive self-distillation, showing that harmful training prompts are not necessary for this failure mode to emerge. We hope our work encourages future investigations of the safety properties of AI systems recursively trained on their own data.

</details>


### [124] [Generative Value Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22497)
*Alexander Swerdlow,Perry Dong,Chelsea Finn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种生成式Q学习方法，将传统的判别式Q函数预测（预测状态-动作对的回报）转换为生成式建模（预测给定回报下动作的可能性），引入回报条件策略作为评论家的生成组件，可与演员共享架构，利用每个动作作为监督信号而非仅依赖回报，在离线和在线RL任务上均优于现有判别式方法（包括分布式RL）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Q学习采用判别式方法从头学习状态-动作价值函数，仅使用标量或分布式回报目标作为监督，忽略了动作本身这一宝贵的监督信号源。现有方法未充分利用动作数据来学习表示，限制了样本效率和表示质量。

Method: 提出反向建模视角：而非预测Q(s,a)，而是建模P(a|s,z)即给定回报z下动作a的似然。评论家包含一个回报条件策略（生成组件），与演员共享架构。训练时，每个动作都提供监督信号，而非仅靠回报；均方误差损失将评论家的Q值锚定到自举目标。这种生成式评论家利用全动作分布进行表征学习。

Result: 在离线和在线RL基准任务上评估，该方法持续优于领先的判别式Q学习方法，包括分布式RL方法（如QR-DQN、C51等）。实验表明生成式建模带来的额外动作监督显著提升了样本效率和最终性能。

Conclusion: 将Q学习重构为生成式建模问题，通过回报条件策略引入动作作为密集监督信号，是一种简单有效的范式转换。该方法架构共享降低参数量，生成式损失提供更丰富的梯度信息，为价值基RL开辟了新的设计空间。

Abstract: Q-learning is premised on learning a function to predict the value of a state-action pair, with the goal of using such a function to guide policy improvement. Most approaches learn this function from scratch using scalar or distributional return targets. This discriminative approach leaves another source of supervision for learning representations unused: the actions themselves. Rather than directly predict the return of a state-action pair, we ask the reverse question: how likely is the action under each possible return? This perspective provides a critic whose generative component is a return-conditioned policy, which can share the actor's architecture. Training this policy supervises the critic with every action rather than with returns alone, and a squared-error loss anchors the critic's Q-value to the bootstrapped target. We evaluate our approach on both offline and online RL tasks and show gains over leading discriminative methods, including distributional RL methods.

</details>


### [125] [Predicting Privileged Information Effectiveness for On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22512)
*Kaijian Zou,Emiliano Penaloza,MohammadHossein Rezaei,Anisha Gunjal,Mohit Raghavendra,Lu Wang,Yunzhong He,Anas Mahmoud*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）中特权信息（PI）的构建与评估，针对多轮智能体场景评估了16种PI配置，发现PI效果取决于内容、生成源和学生模型；提出基于KL散度的指标（采样token反向KL D和错误步KL对比 Δd⁻_err）可更稳健地预测训练后性能，并验证了结合GRPO的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OPSD工作多将PI视为固定设计选择，缺乏对PI内容、构造方式及其与学生模型交互影响的系统理解，特别是在包含动作、观察、推理的多轮智能体轨迹中，PI的转化方式多样，需明确实证指导。

Method: 在三个多轮智能体基准上，针对三个不同规模和推理模式的学生模型，系统评估16种PI配置；引入两个基于KL散度的预训练评估指标：采样token反向KL（D）和错误步KL对比（Δd⁻_err），对比PI效用；实验验证基于Δd⁻_err选择PI的效果，并结合GRPO目标进行联合训练。

Result: 信息密集的轨迹和更强生成器产生的PI增益更大；思维模型受益不稳定。KL指标比PI效用更一致地追踪训练后性能，PI效用易受信息泄露扭曲。按Δd⁻_err选择PI优于随机选择，候选PI差异大时提升最明显。有效PI与GRPO结合可超越强GRPO基线。

Conclusion: 研究为OPSD中PI的构建与评估提供实用指南：应优先使用信息密集、高质量生成器产出的PI；采用KL基指标（尤其是Δd⁻_err）进行预选；结合强化学习目标（如GRPO）可进一步提升性能。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) enables large language models (LLMs) to learn from privileged information (PI) without a separate teacher model. However, prior work largely treats PI as a fixed design choice, leaving the effects of its content and construction poorly understood. This challenge is particularly important in multi-turn agentic settings, where trajectories contain actions, observations, and reasoning that can be transformed into PI in many ways. We systematically evaluate 16 PI configurations across three multi-turn agentic benchmarks and three student models that vary in scale and reasoning mode. We find that PI effectiveness depends on its content, generation source, and the student model. Information-dense trajectories and PI produced by more capable generators generally yield larger gains, whereas thinking models benefit less consistently. To assess PI before training, we compare PI utility with two KL-based metrics: sampled-token reverse KL ($D$) and error-step KL contrast ($Δd^{-}_{\mathrm{err}}$). The KL-based metrics track post-training performance more consistently than PI utility, which can be distorted by information leakage. Empirically, selecting each task's PI by $Δd^{-}_{\mathrm{err}}$ also improves post-training performance over random selection, most when the PI candidates differ widely. Finally, effective PI remains beneficial when combined with GRPO, allowing the combined objective to outperform a strong GRPO baseline. Overall, our results provide practical guidance for constructing and evaluating PI in OPSD training.

</details>


### [126] [Learning smooth dynamics via Kinematic Interpolants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22519)
*Yiheng,Jiang,Youssef Marzouk,Avishek Joey Bose,Aram-Alexandre Pooladian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出动力学插值框架，扩展随机插值方法以从位置和速度测量（相空间数据）推断非线性动力学，在多个科学数据集上实现更优的动力学重构且训练时间大幅缩短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从实验位置观测推断动力学存在固有歧义，多个合理的动力学系统可解释相同观测。现代科学可观测量越来越多地包含速度估计以消除歧义，但如何可扩展地融合这种丰富信息且无需仿真或不必要近似仍是挑战。

Method: 提出动力学插值框架，扩展开创性的随机插值方法论，直接在相空间（位置和速度）的多个快照上推断非线性动力学，避免仿真和过度近似，实现可扩展的动力学学习。

Result: 在多个科学数据集上实验表明，该方法相比现有基线实现了更优的底层动力学重构性能，且仅需基线训练时间的很小一部分。

Conclusion: 动力学插值框架为从相空间观测推断非线性动力学提供了高效、可扩展的解决方案，有效利用速度信息消除歧义，在科学建模应用中具有广泛前景。

Abstract: Inferring dynamics from real-world experimental data is inherently ambiguous, as many plausible dynamical systems can explain the same positional observations at measurement times. For higher fidelity modeling of applications ranging from single-cell RNA to vortex dynamics and even flocking of birds, scientific observables increasingly also include velocity estimates that serve to resolve this issue. However, incorporating this rich information in a scalable manner, free of simulation or unnecessary approximations, has remained a difficult challenge. In this paper, we introduce Kinematic Interpolants (KI), a novel framework that extends the seminal methodology of stochastic interpolants to infer nonlinear dynamics across multiple snapshots in phase space, i.e., observables with position and velocity measurements. Empirically, we demonstrate that across several scientific datasets, KI achieves superior reconstruction of the underlying dynamics compared to existing baselines while requiring only a fraction of the training time.

</details>


### [127] [Don't Retrain, Remix: Adaptation of Web Agents using Mixture Hypernetworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22539)
*Md Farhan Ishmam,Minh Pham Dinh,Kenneth Marino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WebMix 是一种混合超网络，通过组合预训练的低秩适配器，使网页智能体能够快速适应未见过的网页界面，无需在目标界面上进行梯度更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网页界面具有高度多样性和动态性，同一功能的网站外观和导航差异大，甚至同一网站的重新设计也会改变交互模式。人类能通过识别熟悉的交互模式并复用已有知识来适应新界面，现有网页智能体缺乏这种快速适应能力，通常需要针对每个新界面重新训练或进行大量上下文学习。

Method: WebMix 包含三个核心组件：(1) 适配器库：在源界面上预训练的一组低秩适配器；(2) 侦察智能体：在部署时探索目标界面，将观察总结为紧凑的手册；(3) 轻量级路由器：根据手册预测适配器库上的混合系数，将现有适配器组合成新的适配器用于推理。适应过程仅需一次侦察运行和一次前向传播，无需在目标界面上进行梯度更新。

Result: 在 TimeWarp 和 WebArena-Lite-v2 的域迁移任务，以及 TimeWarp 的版本迁移任务上，WebMix 在 Qwen 和 Llama 模型上均匹配或超越上下文学习和 LoRA 基线，平均成功率常能达到直接在目标界面训练的专家模型水平。在 TimeWarp 域迁移上，平均比最佳基线提升约 8 个百分点。这些增益不以已见界面性能为代价，且泛化到版本迁移。

Conclusion: 适应新网页界面可以依赖探索与组合，而非重新训练。WebMix 证明了通过预训练适配器库、侦察总结和路由组合，网页智能体能实现高效、免梯度的快速界面适应，为处理 Web 的动态性和多样性提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: The web is not static or uniform. Websites that serve the same purpose can look and navigate very differently. Even within a single site, successive redesigns can change how familiar features are presented and accessed. Humans adapt to this by recognizing familiar interaction patterns and reusing what they already know. We give web agents a similar ability with WebMix, a mixture hypernetwork that adapts them to unseen interfaces by composing previously trained low-rank adapters. WebMix begins with a bank of adapters trained on source interfaces. At deployment, a scout agent explores the target interface and summarizes its observations into a compact manual. A lightweight router uses this manual to predict mixing coefficients over the adapter bank, which combine the existing adapters into a new one for inference. Adapting to a new interface takes only a scouting run and a forward pass, without gradient updates on the target. With Qwen and Llama models, we evaluate domain transfer on TimeWarp and WebArena-Lite-v2, and version transfer on TimeWarp. In all three settings, WebMix matches or outperforms in-context learning and LoRA baselines, often matching the average success of specialists trained directly on those interfaces. On TimeWarp domain transfer, it gains about 8 percentage points on average over the best baseline with both models. These gains do not come at the cost of seen interfaces, where WebMix keeps its lead, and extend to version transfer. Adapting to a new interface can therefore rely on exploration and composition instead of retraining.

</details>


### [128] [On the SPOT: Enhancing Speculative Decoding for Diffusion Language Models via Commit-Order Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22543)
*Eliezer Shlomi,Eliron Rahimi,Avi Mendelson,Chaim Baskin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPOT (Speculating Positions Order Trajectory)，一种针对扩散语言模型（DLMs）的无损推测解码方法。通过学习预测生成位置的顺序，一个小型起草模型（0.62M参数）提出候选延续树，目标模型单次前向即可验证所有候选。结合耦合噪声匹配验证，在T=0和T=1下均显著超越基线，目标模型前向次数减少达6.8倍，吞吐率提升达3.2倍，且起草模型可跨六种不同DLMs迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型虽承诺并行生成，但面临速度-质量权衡：精确解码每轮仅填充一个mask位置，多token解码则有损。推测解码是自回归模型的标准无损加速，但难直接迁移至DLMs，因DLMs需同时决定生成内容与位置。作者观察到：模型上一轮预测在92-100%位置已给出正确token，缺失的只是“下一个生成哪个位置”的顺序信息。

Method: SPOT包含两大创新：(1) 位置顺序预测起草器：仅0.62M参数，读取目标模型输出分布，输出候选延续树，每个节点为(位置, token)对；(2) 耦合噪声匹配验证：针对扩散块的树状推测，采用共享Gumbel噪声耦合，使目标模型单次前向即可验证所有候选是否等于其自身下一状态。此法支持任意温度，不假设T=0或顺序提议接口。

Result: 综合实验显示：SPOT将目标模型前向次数降低达6.8倍，吞吐率提升达3.2倍；在所有任务与预算下，于T=0和T=1均超越校准过的无训练起草基线；起草模型在六种不同DLMs间成功迁移，泛化性强。

Conclusion: SPOT通过解决“生成顺序预测”这一缺失环节，实现了扩散语言模型的高效无损推测解码。其轻量级起草器与耦合噪声验证机制协同工作，在保持生成质量的前提下大幅加速推理，并展现出跨模型迁移能力，为DLMs实际部署提供了关键技术突破。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) promise parallel text generation, but in practice face an unresolved speed-quality trade-off. Exact decoding fills in only one masked position per model pass, while faster multi-token decoding schemes are lossy. Speculative decoding is the standard lossless acceleration for autoregressive models. However, it does not transfer directly to DLMs, which must choose both what to generate and where. We show that this order is the missing piece: the model's own predictions from the previous pass already give the right token at 92-100% of positions, but not which position comes next. Based on this observation, we introduce Speculating Positions Order Trajectory (SPOT), which learns to predict this order. A 0.62M-parameter model reads only the target's output distributions and proposes a tree of candidate continuations, and the target verifies all of them in a single pass by checking whether each equals its own next state. On the verification side, our analysis shows that prior block-diffusion verification approaches either assume $T=0$ or a sequential proposal interface. To support tree-based speculation at any temperature, we propose using coupled-noise match verification for diffusion blocks, following shared-Gumbel-noise coupling approaches. Our comprehensive experiments show that SPOT cuts target passes by up to 6.8x and reaches up to 3.2x the throughput of standard decoding, beating the calibrated and training-free drafting baselines in every task and budget at $T=0$ and $T=1$. In addition, we show that the learned drafter transfers across models, demonstrating successful transfer across six different DLMs. Project page: https://eliezer318.github.io/SPOT-dLLM/

</details>


### [129] [Deterministic Bounds on Accuracy and Stability of Metric-Space Regression and Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22564)
*Nina Maria Gottschling,Jakob Gawlikowski,Michele Caprio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种几何框架，通过平均核大小量化输入-目标关系的歧义性，给出了有限构造数据集上最佳可达经验误差和准确率的可计算、确定性、非渐近界，且与模型类无关，适用于回归和分类任务。实验表明该界在表格回归、图像回归和分类数据集上均给出了非平凡的参考基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动方法常面临数据集歧义，源于传感器或目标噪声、信息不足及输入数据噪声。现有最佳拟合机器学习方法虽可通过不确定性估计容纳噪声，但易过拟合，且难以量化输入与目标关系的歧义性。

Method: 引入几何框架，利用最坏情况输入和目标噪声导出的数据依赖（模型无关）可行集，计算平均核大小以量化歧义性。该框架提供有限构造数据集上最佳可达经验误差和准确率的可计算、确定性、非渐近界，适用于伪度量输入空间和度量目标空间的回归与分类。

Result: 歧义性导致三个后果：经验误差下限、准确率-稳定性权衡、在标准概率假设下训练误差低于准确率界时分布-训练误差gap严格为正。在欧氏范数的标准Borel假设下，平均核大小的分布类比等于最优分布均方根误差乘以根号2。实验在表格回归、图像回归和分类数据集上给出了非平凡的最佳可达经验误差和准确率界，并与原始数据集上的模型性能进行了描述性比较。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一个可计算、模型无关的构造数据集参考尺度，为理解数据固有歧义性及其对学习性能的影响提供了理论基础和实用工具。

Abstract: Data-driven approaches often face ambiguity in datasets due to sensor or target noise, insufficient information, and noise in the input data. While best-fit machine learning methods can accommodate such noise through uncertainty estimates, they are prone to overfitting and it is challenging to quantify ambiguity of the relation between inputs and targets. We introduce a geometric framework that quantifies this ambiguity through the average kernel size, computed with data-dependent, i.e. model-independent, feasible sets derived from worst-case input and target noise. The framework provides computable, deterministic, non-asymptotic bounds on the best achievable empirical error and accuracy for a finite, constructed dataset, independent of the predictive model class, and applies to regression and classification with pseudo-metric input spaces and metric target spaces. The ambiguity of the input-target relation implies three consequences: an empirical error floor, an accuracy-stability trade-off, and, under standard probabilistic assumptions, a strictly positive distribution-training error gap when training below the accuracy bounds. Under standard Borel assumptions for Euclidean norms, the distributional analogue of the average kernel size is related to Bayes risk as it reduces to the optimal distribution root-mean-squared error times the square root of two. Experiments spanning tabular regression, image regression and classification datasets yield nontrivial bounds on the best achievable empirical error and accuracy on a constructed dataset, alongside descriptive comparisons with model performance on the original datasets. The bounds provide a computable, model-independent reference scale on the constructed dataset and comparisons with model performance on the original dataset are descriptive.

</details>


### [130] [Coverage Cliffs in Off-Policy Quantile Certification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22565)
*Pauline Bourigault,Emmanuelle Bourigault*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何利用记录的情境老虎机数据，为从同一样本中选择的策略认证上尾分位数。提出了TailCert方法，基于PAC-Bayes Bernstein下置信包络，同时覆盖预声明阈值网格和所有数据依赖后验，给出所选后验混合p分位数的原子安全上置信界。理论上建立了有限类认证边界，在显式稀有动作类上证明样本复杂度随重叠因子线性增长、随策略搜索和置信复杂度对数增长、随逆CDF安全边际平方增长，且上下界在常数因子下匹配。实验验证了搜索复杂度转移、朴素重用的赢家诅咒失效以及分割的功率代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在离线情境老虎机设置中，需要从历史日志数据中选择策略并同时认证其尾部性能（如p分位数），但现有方法难以同时处理策略选择带来的数据依赖性和尾部分位数认证的严格覆盖要求。特别是需要解决：1）如何对所有数据依赖后验提供同时有效的CDF下置信包络；2）如何避免赢家诅咒导致的朴素样本重用失效；3）如何量化样本复杂度对重叠因子、搜索复杂度和安全边际的精确依赖关系。

Method: 提出TailCert过程：1）构建PAC-Bayes Bernstein下置信包络，针对目标累积分布函数（CDF），在预声明阈值网格和所有数据依赖后验上同时有效；2）利用包络首次穿越水平p的位置，返回所选后验混合p分位数的原子安全上置信界（或判定为不确定）；3）可用于在认证尾部约束下选择策略。核心理论贡献是建立有限类认证边界，将完整日志与其在固定阈值处的联合加权CDF指示器进行比较。分析了显式稀有动作类上的样本复杂度，证明保留动作计数可恢复完整日志速率。

Result: 在显式稀有动作类上：所需样本量随重叠因子W线性增长，随策略搜索复杂度和置信复杂度对数增长，随逆CDF安全边际平方增长；上下界在所述范式下在常数因子下匹配。发现CDF约简隐藏了哪些动作超出预算的信息；保留动作计数可恢复完整日志的收敛速率。仿真验证了：预测的搜索复杂度转移、朴素重用的赢家诅咒失效、以及样本分割带来的功率损失。

Conclusion: TailCert提供了一个理论严谨且实用的框架，用于从离线日志数据中同时进行策略选择和尾部分位数认证。该方法通过PAC-Bayes Bernstein包络实现了对所有数据依赖后验的同时覆盖，避免了赢家诅咒，并给出了最优的样本复杂度表征。结果表明，保留完整的动作级信息而非仅使用CDF汇总统计量对于达到最优速率至关重要。这为高风险离线决策中的安全策略部署提供了可靠的统计保证。

Abstract: We study when logged contextual-bandit data can certify an upper tail quantile for a policy selected from the same sample. We construct a PAC-Bayes Bernstein lower-confidence envelope for the target cumulative distribution function (CDF), simultaneous over a predeclared threshold grid and all data-dependent posteriors. The resulting procedure, TailCert, uses the envelope's first crossing of level p to return an atom-safe upper confidence bound on the selected posterior's pooled p-quantile (or inconclusive). It can also be used to select a policy under a certified tail constraint. The concentration ingredients are standard; the main theoretical contribution is a matched finite-class certification boundary comparing full logs with their joint weighted CDF indicators at a fixed threshold. On an explicit rare-action class, the required sample size grows linearly with the overlap factor W, logarithmically with policy-search and confidence complexity, and quadratically with the inverse CDF safety margin; the upper and lower bounds agree up to constants in the stated regime. On this class, the CDF reduction hides which actions exceeded the budget; retaining action counts restores the full-log rate. Simulations verify the predicted search-complexity shift, winner's-curse failure of naive reuse, and the power cost of splitting.

</details>


### [131] [Structure-Aware Spatial Kernels for Gaussian Process Distributed Port-Hamiltonian Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22575)
*Tianyi Yang,Thomas Beckers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出结构感知核设计改进GP-dPHS，通过张量积协方差解耦状态与空间依赖，融入空间异质性定性知识，显著提升非均匀分布式系统的重构精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准平稳核无法利用输入不变性，也不能反映结构化空间依赖，导致在空间非均匀系统中建模效率低下。

Method: 设计结构感知核：移除无关状态坐标，采用张量积协方差解耦状态与空间依赖；模块化构造允许通过选择空间核融入异质性定性知识，无需解析剖面描述。

Result: 非线性弦实验（周期性、平滑变化、半分段刚度剖面）表明：结构解耦持续提升重构精度；匹配剖面的空间核在力场与轨迹重构上带来显著更大增益。

Conclusion: 结构感知核设计有效融合物理结构与数据驱动建模，为空间非均匀分布式系统提供灵活高效的建模框架。

Abstract: Gaussian process distributed port-Hamiltonian systems (GP-dPHS) enable data-driven modeling of distributed dynamical systems while preserving their physical structure. However, standard stationary kernels can be inefficient for spatially heterogeneous systems because they neither exploit known input invariances nor reflect structured spatial dependencies. We address this limitation with a structure-aware kernel design that removes irrelevant state coordinates and decouples state and spatial dependencies through a tensor-product covariance. This modular construction allows qualitative knowledge of spatial heterogeneity to be incorporated by selecting an appropriate spatial kernel without requiring an analytical description of the underlying profile. Numerical experiments on a nonlinear string with periodic, smoothly varying, and semi-piecewise stiffness profiles demonstrate that structural decoupling consistently improves reconstruction accuracy, while profile-matched spatial kernels yield substantially larger gains in force-field and trajectory reconstruction.

</details>


### [132] [Noise-Robust Forced Alignment for Spontaneous Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22583)
*Laurin Wagner,Mario Zusag*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的GMM-HMM强制对齐流水线，针对自发性、噪声真实语音中的填充词、笑声和不流畅现象导致的词边界误差问题。通过使用上采样WavLM特征替代MFCC、显式建模填充词/非词汇声事件单元、以及判别性投影增强鲁棒性，在无人工边界标注的149小时数据上自举训练。在Buckeye和FluencyBank测试集上分别达到20.6ms和76.9ms的词边界MAE，比最强基线降低19%和17%，灾难性错位（>200ms）比MFA最佳模型减少2.7倍，笑声邻近误差减半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强制对齐器在自发性、噪声真实语音上性能显著下降，填充词、笑声和不流畅现象会破坏词边界准确性，影响众多下游应用。传统方法如MFA在对话语音中存在大量剪枝伪影导致的错误，且缺乏对非词汇声事件的显式建模。

Method: 改进经典GMM-HMM对齐器：1）使用上采样WavLM特征替代传统MFCC；2）引入显式音素单元建模填充词和非词汇声事件（如笑声），基于逐字标注的自发性语音训练；3）设计判别性投影，在干净与噪声污染特征对上拟合以提升鲁棒性；4）从149小时精选音频自举训练，无需人工或外部边界目标。

Result: 在会话语音（Buckeye）和不流畅语音（FluencyBank）上，词边界MAE分别为20.6ms和76.9ms，比10个基线中最强者降低19%和17%。灾难性错位（>200ms）比MFA最佳模型少2.7倍，即使MFA扩大搜索波束也无法弥补。扩大MFA波束可移除其会话语音错误中的大量剪枝伪影；事件单元使笑声邻近误差减半，整体鲁棒性显著优于现有对齐器。

Conclusion: 提出的自举GMM-HMM流水线结合WavLM特征、显式事件建模和判别性鲁棒性投影，有效解决了自发性噪声语音中的强制对齐难题，在词边界精度和灾难性错误率上均显著超越现有最强基线，为下游语音处理应用提供了更可靠的对齐基础。

Abstract: We show that forced aligners degrade on spontaneous, noisy real-world speech, and that fillers, laughter, and disfluencies corrupt word boundaries, impacting many downstream applications. We revisit the classical GMM-HMM aligner with upsampled WavLM features instead of MFCCs, explicit phone units for fillers and non-lexical vocal events trained on verbatim-annotated spontaneous speech, and a discriminative projection fitted on clean and noise-corrupted feature pairs to improve robustness. The pipeline is self-bootstrapped from 149h of curated audio without manual or external boundary targets. On conversational (Buckeye) and disfluent (FluencyBank) speech it reaches word-boundary MAEs of 20.6 and 76.9ms, 19% and 17% lower than the strongest of ten baselines. Catastrophic misalignments (>200ms) are 2.7 times rarer than Montreal Forced Aligners (MFA) best model even after widening MFA's search beam, which we show removes a large pruning artifact from its conversational error; event units halve the error next to laughter, while being meaningfully more robust than existing aligners.

</details>


### [133] [Initiating Removal from Operational Symptoms in Federated Vehicular Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22584)
*Zihao Ding,Jun Huang,Qiang Duan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ATONE（聚合时操作证据效应中和），一种将遗忘学习集成到联邦学习聚合规则中的新方法，解决联邦车辆驾驶模型中延迟症状导致的故障贡献难以定位和移除的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习训练的联网车辆驾驶模型在路测中出现延迟症状时，现有联邦遗忘学习（FU）假设移除请求明确指出要遗忘的内容，但实际症状无法指明具体车辆或训练轮次，且聚合操作已将故障原因扩散至整个车队，导致传统 FU 失效。

Method: ATONE 将日常驾驶误差和报告症状投影到由近期全局增量张成的子空间，利用这些增量估计曲率反向传播每个贡献的存活效应，在一次反向传播中对所有贡献评分。采用两级修正：每轮抑制疑似效应，经一轮探测确认后彻底消除。

Result: 理论上证明反向评分与前向评分一致，且在子空间外训练平坦时消除精确。在三个驾驶数据集上，ATONE 在 9 个实验设置中的 8 个中比最强基线减少 43%–61% 的残余危害，效用距离完全重训练仅 0.36 分以内。

Conclusion: ATONE 通过将遗忘学习嵌入聚合规则，利用操作证据和子空间投影实现高效、精确的故障贡献中和，显著优于现有基线，且保持接近完全重训练的模型效用，为联邦学习中的延迟症状遗忘提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Connected vehicles train a driving model through federated learning (FL) and drive with it, so a faulty contribution often surfaces only as a delayed symptom on the road. Federated unlearning (FU) assumes that a removal request names what to forget. A symptom names no vehicle or round, and aggregation has already spread its cause across the fleet. We propose \underline{A}ggregation-\underline{T}ime \underline{O}perational-evidence \underline{N}eutralization of \underline{E}ffects (\method{}), which makes unlearning a term of the FL aggregation rule. \method{} projects everyday driving errors and reported symptoms onto a subspace spanned by recent global increments. It propagates the surviving effect of every contribution with curvature estimated from these increments and scores all contributions in one backward pass. A two-tier correction suppresses suspected effects in every round and eliminates confirmed ones after a one-round probe. We prove that backward scoring matches forward scoring and that the elimination is exact when training is flat outside the subspace. On three driving datasets, \method{} leaves 43\% to 61\% less residual harm than the strongest baseline in eight of nine settings while staying within 0.36 points of full retraining in utility.

</details>


### [134] [Flat Loss, Evolving Features: Grokking in Modular Addition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22593)
*Akash Kumar,Karthik Sankaranarayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现神经网络在训练交叉熵损失保持平坦的情况下仍能持续学习：在模加法任务上，使用特定更新规则的ReLU网络能维持100%训练准确率，同时隐藏特征演化、泛化准确率从0升至90%以上，证明了训练损失平台期不等于学习停滞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨训练交叉熵损失平坦（正值）时网络是否仍能学习。现有认知认为损失平台期意味着学习停滞，但作者假设正确分类不需要最小化交叉熵（后者还依赖预测置信度），旨在理论和实验上证明损失平坦与特征演化、泛化提升可以共存。

Method: 在模加法任务上训练ReLU网络，采用：supported-Polar隐藏层更新、归一化输出层更新、权重衰减。理论部分：构造初始化，证明单隐层网络的两个有限窗口定理（傅里叶分量增益常数、任意低正损失下重拟合读出层先于网络泛化）。实验部分：随机初始化固定窗口、深层网络事后窗口、Transformer实验（输出增益缓慢衰减）。

Result: 单隐层网络理论证明损失平坦与特征演化共存；实验显示随机初始化固定窗口内泛化准确率从0升至90%以上、训练准确率保持100%、交叉熵相对平坦。深层网络在p=47,61上满足事后窗口标准。一层Transformer每个检查点训练对全正确、交叉熵上升、泛化逼近1；两层Transformer泛化上升后训练对才开始翻转。

Conclusion: 训练损失平台期不等于学习停滞。正确分类不需要最小化交叉熵，网络可在损失平坦时持续演化隐藏特征并提升泛化。这一现象在理论构造、随机初始化、深层网络和Transformer中均得到验证，揭示了损失景观与学习动态的解耦。

Abstract: Can a network keep learning while its training cross-entropy stays nearly flat at a positive level? We show that it can. On modular addition, a standard testbed for grokking, ReLU networks trained with supported-Polar hidden updates, normalized output-layer updates and weight decay can keep 100% training accuracy while their hidden features change and held-out accuracy rises from zero to above 90%, at relatively flat training cross-entropy. Correct classification need not minimize cross-entropy, which still depends on prediction confidence. We prove this coexistence for one-hidden-layer networks in two finite-window theorems from constructed initializations: one gives a constant gain in the Fourier fraction of the hidden features, and the other holds at arbitrarily low positive loss, where a readout refitted on the training data generalizes before the network. Experiments from random initialization show the same coexistence on a window fixed in advance, and in deeper networks held-out accuracy also rises over post-hoc windows with a net increase in training cross-entropy. With a last-layer rate set per training fraction and head decay scaled with the prime, deeper networks at p=47 and 61 meet a criterion on post-hoc windows, fixed in advance, in nearly all fresh runs. A one-block transformer trained with the same updates and a slowly decaying output gain keeps every training pair correct at every logged checkpoint while its training cross-entropy rises and its held-out accuracy approaches one; with two blocks this holds through the held-out rise, after which training pairs begin to flip. A plateau in training loss need not be a plateau in learning. Code: https://github.com/akashkumar-d/flat-loss-evolving-features.

</details>


### [135] [Diagnosing GNN Failure Modes with Layer-Wise Probe Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22595)
*Shanzi Bao,Mar Gonzàlez I Català,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种事后诊断方法，通过冻结训练好的图神经网络（GNN），在每一层拟合独立的线性探针，并追踪探针准确率与归一化预测熵，从而揭示信息在消息传递过程中的演化。实验表明，层级探针熵轨迹能区分有益、中性、有害的平滑效应，且能将异质图下的结构错配（早期出现）与过深导致的过度平滑（随深度加深出现）两大失效模式分离，为自适应深度选择和早退出机制提供理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GNN评估通常只关注最后一层的输出，忽略了任务相关信息在消息传递过程中如何逐层演化。几何度量无法有效区分有益、中性和有害的平滑效应，缺乏一种能够量化层级信息流动的诊断工具。

Method: 1. 训练标准GNN并冻结参数；2. 对每一层的隐藏表示分别训练一个独立的线性探针（线性分类器）；3. 记录每层探针的分类准确率与归一化预测熵；4. 绘制探针准确率与熵随层数变化的轨迹，并在真实图与合成图上对比浅层与深层模型。

Result: 层级探针熵轨迹能清晰区分三类平滑效应：有益平滑（熵降低、准确率提升）、中性平滑（熵与准确率均稳定）、有害平滑（熵升高、准确率下降）。在异质图设置中，结构错配导致的性能下降从最早层即表现为熵升高，而过度平滑仅在传播深度增加时才出现熵退化。两种失效模式的熵退化起始层显著不同。

Conclusion: 探针熵轨迹是一种有效的事后分析工具，能够量化GNN中任务相关信息的层级流动，揭示两大核心失效模式的根本差异。该方法为未来设计自适应深度选择策略、早退出机制以及改进异质图下的消息传递提供了实证指导。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) are typically evaluated only at their final layer, obscuring how task-relevant information evolves during message passing. We introduce a post-hoc diagnostic that freezes a trained GNN, fits an independent linear probe to each layer, and tracks probe accuracy alongside normalized predictive entropy. Across real and synthetic graph settings, layer-wise probe entropy distinguishes beneficial, neutral, and harmful smoothing, a capability not demonstrated by existing geometric measures. Moreover, by comparing shallow and deep models, we show that the onset of entropy degradation separates the two main GNN failure modes: structural mismatch under heterophily, which appears from the earliest layers even in shallow models, and excessive-depth oversmoothing, which emerges only as propagation depth increases. These results establish probe-entropy trajectories as a tool for analyzing information flow in GNNs and motivate future work on adaptive depth selection and early-exit mechanisms.

</details>


### [136] [Can LLMs predict the outcomes of machine learning experiments?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22599)
*Ayush Bansal,Aldo Pareja,Sean Klein,Serge Didenko,Artem Lukoianov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了前沿大语言模型（LLM）预测其自身生成的机器学习实验想法影响的能力，发现LLM具有一定的预测直觉但准确性有限，最佳模型Claude Opus 5.5仅能解释50%的结果方差（R²=0.49），且所有模型系统性地低估变化幅度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI技术进步，递归自我改进（RSI）变得越来越可行，LLM已能提出、选择并运行ML实验。然而ML设计空间巨大，LLM在决定是否运行实验前需要可靠的直觉来评估想法价值，本文旨在量化测量当前前沿LLM的这种预测直觉。

Method: 构建包含288个LLM生成想法的数据集，覆盖八个MLE-bench任务。提示五个前沿LLM（GPT-6 Astra、Claude Opus 5.5、Claude Fable 5.1、Kimi K3、GLM-5.3）预测每个想法的影响，并将预测与实际训练结果对比。同时测试不同编码框架、推理设置和上下文信息的影响。

Result: 主要发现：1）LLM预测优于随机但远未达准确，最佳模型R²=0.49；2）所有模型系统性低估变化幅度；3）预测质量随任务差异巨大；4）编码框架选择对结果影响小，无自我偏好证据；5）更高推理设置和额外上下文带来适度提升。

Conclusion: 当前LLM对哪些ML想法有效具有有用但有限的直觉，无法可靠地指导递归自我改进中的实验选择。发布数据集以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Recent advances in AI make Recursive Self-Improvement (RSI) increasingly plausible. Large Language Models (LLMs) can already propose, select, and run Machine Learning (ML) experiments. However, the space of possible design choices in ML is vast. An LLM that proposes an experiment needs a reliable intuition of whether it is worth running given the timing constraints of long training runs. In this work, we measure such intuition in current frontier LLMs by evaluating how accurately they predict the impact of self-generated ML experiment ideas before these are put into training runs. To this end, we construct a dataset of 288 LLM-proposed ideas on eight MLE-bench tasks. We then prompt five frontier LLMs (GPT-6 Astra, Claude Opus 5.5, Claude Fable 5.1, Kimi K3, and GLM-5.3) to predict the impact of each idea and compare their predictions with the actual outcomes after training. We report four main findings. First, LLM predictions are substantially better than random chance but far from accurate: even the best model, Claude Opus 5.5, explains only half of the variance in outcomes (R^2 = 0.49). Second, we notice that all tested models systematically underestimate the magnitude of the changes. Third, prediction quality varies widely across tasks. Finally, the choice of the coding harness that actually implements the ideas has little effect on outcomes, and we find no evidence of self-preference. Higher reasoning settings and additional context lead to modest gains. Overall, our results show that current LLMs have useful but limited intuition about which ML ideas will work. We release our dataset to support further study.

</details>


### [137] [Rarity of Singletons Matters & Supplementary Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22604)
*Pragatheeswaran Vipulanandan,Kamal Premaratne,Dilip Sarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出脆弱性感知（FA）框架，通过量化单例统计量的脆弱性并基于熵-KL优化自适应正则化经典缺失质量与未见物种估计器，在小样本、长尾分布下显著提升估计稳定性与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在小样本 regime 下，稀有事件占用统计（特别是单例）高度可变，导致经典估计器（如 Good-Turing、Good-Toulmin）对采样波动敏感且不可靠，亟需一种能量化并修正这种脆弱性的方法。

Method: 构建基于单例的脆弱性度量（结合概率质量与占用计数），制定熵-Kullback-Leibler 优化目标，根据脆弱性自适应调整对基准估计器的依赖程度。目标函数严格凹，拥有唯一闭式解，无需泊松化或渐近近似，可作为轻量级修正直接应用于经典估计器。

Result: 跨越 360 种实验配置（合成分布与真实语言语料），该方法在稳定 regime 保持竞争力，在碎片化、长尾及小样本设置中显著提升估计准确性与稳定性。

Conclusion: 显式建模并校正稀有观测的脆弱性，能有效改善对未观测物种及其概率质量的推断可靠性，为小样本下的缺失质量与未见物种估计提供了鲁棒的实用框架。

Abstract: Estimating missing-mass and unseen-species counts from finite samples relies heavily on rare-event occupancy statistics, particularly singletons. In small-sample regimes, these statistics can be highly variable, making classical estimators sensitive to sampling fluctuations and potentially unreliable. We propose a fragility-aware (FA) framework that explicitly quantifies this sensitivity and adaptively regularizes conventional missing-mass and unseen-species estimators. Using singleton-based fragility measures derived from probability mass and occupancy counts, we formulate an entropy--Kullback--Leibler (KL) optimization that adjusts the reliance on base estimates according to their fragility. The resulting objectives are strictly concave, admit unique closed-form solutions, and require no Poissonization or asymptotic approximations. The framework provides a lightweight correction to classical estimators, including Good--Turing and Good--Toulmin, while reducing reliance on potentially unstable rare-event statistics. Across 360 experimental configurations spanning synthetic distributions and real-world language corpora, our method remains competitive in stable regimes while improving estimation accuracy and stability in fragmented, long-tailed, and small-sample settings. The findings demonstrate that accounting for the fragility of rare observations can improve the reliability of inference about unobserved species and their associated probability mass.

</details>


### [138] [Noise-shaped Sampling and Annealing for Scalable On-the-edge Training of Binary and Ternary AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22621)
*Zihao Chen,Zhili Xiao,Rui-Jie Zhu,Yipeng Wang,Jason Eshraghian,Shantanu Chakrabartty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ΣΔ-Sampler优化器，利用二值/三值神经网络训练损失与高阶Ising模型能量的结构等价性，通过一阶ΣΔ噪声整形循环嵌入随机采样退火步骤，将量化噪声、高阶挫折和mini-batch采样噪声推出低频自适应带，使离散权重更新沿平滑下降轨迹演化。在QUBO、CIFAR-10、ImageNet-1k、类增量持续学习及高达2.7B参数的无MatMul语言模型上验证，三值模型精度逼近全精度基线，且训练内存占用降低50%以上、速度提升一个数量级、权重更新内存流量降低四个数量级，适合大规模二值/三值模型的端侧训练与微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二值与三值神经网络的训练损失可展开为离散权重上的多线性多项式，结构上等价于分层高阶Ising模型的能量函数。现有Ising求解器旨在寻找固定能量函数的基态，而神经网络训练需寻找能泛化到未见数据的解，两者目标存在根本差异。此外，标准量化感知训练（QAT）在大模型上内存占用大、计算开销高，难以部署于资源受限设备。因此需要一种能协调Ising求解与泛化目标、同时大幅降低资源消耗的离散权重优化方法。

Method: 核心贡献是ΣΔ-Sampler优化器：将随机采样退火步骤作为噪声量化器嵌入一阶ΣΔ噪声整形反馈回路中。该回路将权重量化噪声、高阶挫折效应及mini-batch采样噪声从低频自适应频带推向高频，使离散权重更新能沿着崎岖损失景观中嵌入的平滑下降轨迹演化。此方法利用Ising模型等价性引导离散优化，同时通过噪声整形保证训练动态的平滑性与泛化能力。

Result: 在植入式QUBO基准（已知最小值）上验证正确性；CIFAR-10上1300万参数三值模型精度距全精度仅1%；ImageNet-1k上三值ResNet-18的Top-5准确率较三值基线仅差0.4%；类增量持续学习中保持旧任务准确率；2.7B参数无MatMul语言模型上超越直通估计器基线。与标准QAT相比：训练内存占用减少>50%，训练速度提升一个数量级，权重更新内存流量降低四个数量级。

Conclusion: ΣΔ-Sampler成功弥合了Ising基态搜索与神经网络泛化训练之间的目标鸿沟，实现了高效、低内存、低带宽的二值/三值大模型训练。其噪声整形机制使离散优化轨迹平滑化，在多种任务与规模上均达到接近全精度的精度，同时大幅降低资源消耗，为端侧大规模离散权重模型的训练与微调提供了切实可行的路径。

Abstract: The training loss of binary and ternary neural networks, expanded as a multilinear polynomial over the discrete weights, is structurally equivalent to the energy of a hierarchical high-order Ising model. We exploit this equivalence to apply Ising solvers to discrete-weight network training, while accounting for a fundamental difference in objectives: an Ising solver seeks the ground state of a fixed energy function, whereas training seeks solutions that generalize to unseen data. Our key contribution is $ΣΔ$-Sampler, an optimizer that reconciles these objectives by embedding a stochastic sampling-and-annealing step as a noisy quantizer inside a first-order $ΣΔ$ noise-shaping loop. The loop pushes noise from weight quantization, high-order frustration, and mini-batch sampling out of the low-frequency adaptation band, so that discrete weight updates follow the smooth descent trajectories embedded in the rugged loss landscape. We validate the framework on a planted quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) benchmark with a known minimizer; on CIFAR-10, where a $13$M-parameter ternary model comes within $1\%$ of full-precision accuracy; on ImageNet-1k, where a ternary ResNet-18 comes within $0.4\%$ Top-5 accuracy of a ternary baseline; on class-incremental continual learning, where prior-task accuracy is preserved; and on MatMul-free language models up to $2.7$B parameters, where it outperforms a straight-through-estimator baseline under matched continued pre-training. Compared with standard quantization-aware training, $ΣΔ$-Sampler reduces the training memory footprint by over $50\%$, delivers an order-of-magnitude speed-up, and reduces weight-update memory traffic by four orders of magnitude, making it attractive for on-device training and fine-tuning of large binary and ternary models.

</details>


### [139] [Understanding Stability of Outer Nesterov Momentum on Stochastic Pseudo-Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22623)
*Nishant Sharma,Laura Gomezjurado Gonzalez,Ganesh Talluri,Sarath Chandar,Hiroki Naganuma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究大规模分布式神经网络训练中，外层动量、本地步数和工作者平均化对训练动态及稳定性的影响。通过随机二次模型推导出任意维度下的精确均方稳定性判据，发现内部批次大小决定增加本地步数是稳定还是破坏外层迭代；工作者平均化虽允许更大动量，但稳定性所需工作者数量随本地步数呈指数增长。理论发现可通过共享小批量扰动在中间检查点直接探测，并在多种网络上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模神经网络常在多工作者间并行训练，通信开销是主要瓶颈。工作者执行多步本地优化后再发送伪梯度给应用 Nesterov 动量的外层优化器。理解外层动量、本地步数、工作者平均化如何控制训练动态及稳定性，对高效分布式训练至关重要。

Method: 1. 理论分析：以随机二次模型为代理，推导任意维度下的精确均方稳定性判据。2. 实验验证：提出共享小批量扰动方法，在神经网络中间检查点直接探测均方动态。3. 实证研究：跨多种网络架构测试理论预测，量化对训练损失的影响。

Result: 1. 内部批次大小决定增加本地步数最终是稳定还是破坏外层迭代。2. 独立工作者的伪梯度平均化可允许更大的外层动量值。3. 稳定性所需的工作者数量随本地步数呈指数级增长。4. 均方动态可通过共享小批量扰动在中间检查点直接探测。5. 理论发现在多种网络上得到验证，并量化了对训练损失的影响。

Conclusion: 本文为带有本地步数和动量的分布式训练稳定性提供了理论基础，揭示了本地计算、通信频率与稳定性之间的关键权衡。本地步数增加导致所需工作者数量指数增长的现象，指出了通信高效训练的根本限制。提出的探测方法可直接在实际神经网络训练中监测稳定性动态。

Abstract: Large neural networks are commonly trained across many workers, and communication between them is a major cost. Workers therefore take several local optimization steps before sending their net parameter displacements, or pseudo-gradients, to an outer optimizer, which often applies Nesterov momentum. We study how the outer momentum, the number of local steps and the averaging over workers control the training dynamics, and in particular their stability. For random quadratics, we derive an exact mean-square stability criterion in any dimension, which reveals that the inner batch size determines whether increasing the number of local steps eventually stabilizes or destabilizes the outer iteration. Moreover, while averaging the pseudo-gradients of independent workers can make larger outer momentum values admissible, the number of workers required for stability can grow exponentially with the number of local steps. Finally, we show that the mean-square dynamics governing stability can be probed directly at intermediate neural-network checkpoints using shared-minibatch perturbations. We test our theoretical findings across networks, and measure their consequences for training loss.

</details>


### [140] [Network-Wide Freeway Speed Nowcasting and Forecasting from Sparse Fixed Sensors with a Probe-Supervised Graph Convolutional Recurrent Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22630)
*Ran Tu,Zihao Li,Arash Mirzaei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 GCRN+，一种图卷积循环网络，用于在仅 15.6% 链路有固定传感器的情况下，联合实时估测与未来一小时预测达拉斯-沃斯堡地区全部 4,127 条定向高速链路的小时级车速。训练阶段利用覆盖全网的探针车历史数据监督，推理阶段仅使用传感器读数，这种设定称为“探针监督稀疏感知”。


<details>
