  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通管理部门需要全网链路的当前与近未来车速，但固定传感器仅覆盖部分链路（本文实验中仅 15.6%）。现有方法难以在仅有稀疏传感器输入的推理条件下，利用全网探针车历史标签进行有效训练并泛化到无传感器链路。

Method: GCRN+ 预测相对于历史车速剖面的偏差，融合三大核心组件：(1) 无参数、方向感知的核插值，将传感器异常值插值为输入特征并作为输出基线；(2) 基于有向路网图的扩散卷积 GRU；(3) 低秩自适应图与可学习链路嵌入。模型规模线性增长，可在 CPU 上训练。为剔除探测器校准干扰，推理时从探针车速模拟传感器读数。

Result: 在四个保留测试日上，GCRN+ 在无传感器链路上的 RMSE 为 2.21 mph（当前小时）和 2.39 mph（下一小时），显著优于历史均值 3.23 mph。使用相同插值特征的最强基线为 2.53/2.75 mph；不使用插值特征的近期模型（STID、STAEformer、ImputeFormer、MoGERNN）最优为 2.54/2.53 mph。除与传感器在路网图中不连通的链路外，GCRN+ 在所有距离分段均居首。消融实验表明增益主要来自链路嵌入与自适应图。拥堵检测召回率在精度 0.94 下从历史均值 77%、最强竞品 86% 提升至 89%。

Conclusion: GCRN+ 通过探针监督稀疏感知范式，结合方向感知插值基线、扩散卷积循环骨干、可学习链路嵌入与低秩自适应图，实现了全网链路的高精度实时估测与短时预测，并在拥堵检测任务上显著提升召回率，验证了在仅有稀疏传感器推理输入时，利用全网探针标签训练图神经网络的有效性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Traffic agencies need current and near-future speeds on every link of their network, but their fixed sensors cover only part of it. We study joint nowcasting and next-hour forecasting of hourly speeds on all 4{,}127 directional freeway links of the Dallas--Fort Worth region from sensors on 643 links (15.6%). Historical probe-vehicle data supervise every link in training; only sensor readings are used at inference. We call this setting probe-supervised sparse sensing. We propose GCRN+, a graph convolutional recurrent network that predicts deviations from a historical speed profile. It combines a parameter-free, direction-aware kernel interpolation of sensor anomalies, used as input features and as an output baseline, with a diffusion-convolutional GRU on the directed road graph, a low-rank adaptive graph and learnable link embeddings. It scales linearly with network size and trains on a CPU. Sensor readings are emulated from probe speeds, which isolates spatio-temporal inference from detector calibration. On four held-out test days, GCRN+ attains an RMSE of 2.21~mph for the current hour and 2.39~mph for the next hour on unsensored links, against 3.23~mph for the historical average. The best baselines that use the same interpolation features reach 2.53 and 2.75~mph. Adapted recent models without these features (STID, STAEformer, ImputeFormer, MoGERNN) reach at best 2.54 and 2.53~mph; this comparison is between complete pipelines rather than architectures. GCRN+ is best in every distance band except links not connected to any sensor through the road graph. Ablations within one recurrent backbone attribute most of the gain to the link embeddings and the adaptive graph, which can be trained because probe labels exist for every link. For congestion detection on unsensored links, recall rises from 77\% (historical average) and 86% (best competitor) to 89% at aprecision of 0.94.

</details>


### [141] [Alert-List Concordance for Evaluating Imputation Strategies in ICU Sepsis Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22635)
*Priyanka Siddappa,Muntaqim Ahmed Raju,Shifat Haider Al Amin,Ruizhe Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出临床预测中预处理（特别是缺失值插补）是一个被低估的可靠性风险：即使模型 AUROC 相似，不同的插补策略会导致不同患者被标记为高风险。作者提出“警报列表一致性”（基于 Jaccard 相似度和 bootstrap 置信区间）来衡量两条流水线对高风险患者识别的一致性。在 PhysioNet/CinC 2019 数据集（40,336 例，71.9% 缺失）上比较九种插补策略和原始缺失基线，发现 22/28 对方法在 AUROC 上无显著差异，但警报列表 Jaccard 仅 0.518–0.750；ForwardFill 虽 AUROC 较高但一致性最低（0.40–0.45）。深度插补在判别力上不优于均值插补，且计算成本高 5–12 倍。结论：聚合判别指标不足以保证患者层面的决策一致性，需引入警报列表一致性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床预测模型中预处理步骤（尤其是缺失值插补）对最终警报名单的影响被忽视。现有评估主要关注重构误差或聚合 AUROC，无法直接量化不同流水线对同一患者是否给出一致的高风险标记。ICU 数据存在频繁、结构化的缺失，使得这一问题尤为突出。因此需要一种以患者为中心的指标来衡量不同预处理流水线在高风险患者识别上的共识。

Method: 引入警报列表一致性指标：取两条流水线各自输出的前 k% 高风险患者集合，计算 Jaccard 相似度，并用患者层面的 bootstrap 置信区间量化不确定性。在 PhysioNet/CinC 2019 数据集（40,336 例，71.9% 缺失）上，固定 XGBoost 模型配置，分别对九种插补策略和原始缺失基线进行训练和 Platt 校准。比较 28 个方法对的 AUROC 差异（Bonferroni 校正）与警报列表 Jaccard 重叠，并按医院系统、六种患者层面聚合规则、行层面进行亚组分析。同时记录总流水线壁钟时间以评估计算成本。

Result: 22/28 对插补方法在患者层面 AUROC 无统计学显著差异（Bonferroni 校正后），但警报列表 Jaccard 仅 0.518–0.750，表明相似聚合判别力伴随不同患者选择。ForwardFill 为异常值：AUROC 较高但与所有其他方法重叠最低（0.40–0.45）。模式在各医院系统及六种聚合规则下均成立。行层面八种方法 AUROC 差异仅 0.011。深度插补未在判别力上优于均值插补，顺序实现耗时为均值插补的 5–12 倍。

Conclusion: 聚合判别指标（如 AUROC）无法保证患者层面的决策一致性；不同插补流水线虽整体性能相近，但高风险患者名单差异显著。建议在临床部署前采用警报列表一致性评估，以确保预处理变更不改变关键患者识别。均值插补在判别力与计算效率上均具竞争力，深度插补未带来额外收益。

Abstract: Preprocessing is an under-measured reliability risk in clinical prediction: a model can keep a similar AUROC and still change who receives an alert. Imputation quality is commonly evaluated using reconstruction error or aggregate discrimination, neither of which directly measures agreement on which patients are flagged. At a matched alert rate, pipelines with similar aggregate performance can produce different patient lists. This question is especially relevant under the frequent, structured missingness of real ICU records. We therefore introduce alert-list concordance, which asks whether two pipelines identify the same high-risk patients, measured by the Jaccard similarity of their top-k% alert sets with patient-level bootstrap confidence intervals. On PhysioNet/CinC 2019 (40,336 patients, 71.9% missingness), we compare nine imputation strategies and a native-missingness baseline using a fixed XGBoost configuration, fitted and Platt-calibrated separately for each strategy. After Bonferroni correction, 22 of the 28 reconstruction-method pairs showed no statistically significant difference in patient-level AUROC, yet their alert lists overlap at Jaccard 0.518 to 0.750. Similar aggregate discrimination can therefore accompany different patient selections. ForwardFill is a separate outlier, pairing higher discrimination with the lowest overlap in the matrix, 0.40 to 0.45. The broad pattern holds within each hospital system and under six patient-level aggregation rules. At the row level, eight of ten methods fall within an AUROC range of 0.011. In this row-level pipeline, deep imputers did not outperform mean imputation on discrimination, and the sequential implementations cost 5 to 12 times mean imputation in total pipeline wall-clock time on our hardware.

</details>


### [142] [The Ephemeral Cost of Switching Optimizers Across LLM Training Stages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22650)
*Xinyu Zhao,Davis Wertheimer,Ashutosh Sharma,Rameswar Panda,Paul Pu Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了预训练与后训练阶段使用不同优化器（AdamW vs Muon）是否存在性能损失，发现优化器不匹配惩罚微乎其微，后训练优化器选择可基本独立于预训练优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作暗示预训练与后续训练阶段切换优化器会带来性能损失，但缺乏系统性研究。作者旨在量化AdamW与Muon优化器在预训练、持续预训练和监督微调（SFT）阶段混合使用时的实际影响。

Method: 从头预训练1.2B Llama-3模型，系统变换AdamW和Muon的更新比例；在持续预训练和SFT阶段同样插值两种优化器；进一步在Llama和Qwen系列（高达14B参数）的开放权重模型上评估通用任务SFT性能。

Result: 最终性能主要由预训练和后训练优化器的独立效应解释，几乎没有显著的优化器不匹配惩罚。SFT阶段Muon更新比例越高，下游性能通常越好。虽然匹配预训练优化器可减少SFT遗忘，但不转化为最终基准性能提升。Muon在AdamW预训练的模型上仍保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 后训练阶段的优化器选择可以基本独立于预训练阶段使用的优化器，无需担心优化器不匹配带来的性能损失。

Abstract: Recent work suggests that switching optimizers between pretraining and subsequent training stages incurs a performance cost, but systematic investigation of this phenomenon is lacking. We study the mismatch between AdamW and Muon optimizers by pretraining 1.2B Llama-3 models from scratch while varying the proportion of updates taken with each optimizer, and similarly interpolating between the two optimizers during continued pretraining and supervised fine-tuning (SFT). Surprisingly, we find little evidence of a meaningful optimizer mismatch penalty: final performance is largely explained by the independent effects of the pretraining and post-training optimizers, with downstream performance generally improving as the proportion of Muon updates during SFT increases. Although matching the pretraining optimizer can reduce forgetting during SFT, this does not translate into improved final benchmark performance. We further evaluate AdamW and Muon for task-general SFT of open-weight models, across the Llama and Qwen families up to 14B parameters, where Muon remains competitive with AdamW, despite these models having been pretrained with AdamW. Together, our results suggest that the choice of optimizer for post-training can be made largely independently of the optimizer used during pretraining.

</details>


### [143] [Edit Discovery in Human-AI Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22661)
*Alireza Mirrokni,Shayan Kiyani,George Pappas,Hamed Hassani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出编辑发现优化（EDO）框架，解决在人机交互中如何以有限编辑成本高效识别需修正的模型响应问题，并开发在线算法EDIT，在部分反馈设定下实现编辑预算约束下的最优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机交互产生大量数据，部分模型响应存在失败，若能识别并修正可作为宝贵监督信号。但编辑（专家、更强模型或增强计算）调用成本高昂，需在不知编辑是否真会修改的情况下决定哪些交互送编辑，核心挑战是在编辑成本预算下最小化漏掉的编辑机会并控制不必要的确认。

Method: 将问题形式化为编辑发现优化（EDO），证明总体最优策略为编辑后验概率的成本调整阈值。在此基础上提出EDIT算法：结合自适应编辑评分、确认控制阈值、成本感知选择规则，在每轮满足编辑预算并控制平均确认率（含消失的有限时域项），无需交互流分布假设。

Result: 在四个推理与事实问答基准上，EDIT在编辑成本、一类错误（过度确认）和二类错误（漏掉编辑）的权衡上优于直接基于LLM的批量选择方法。

Conclusion: EDIT提供了一种理论奠基且实用的在线算法，能在有限编辑资源下高效发现需修正的交互，为人机协作数据筛选提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Human-AI interactions are becoming a rich source of data for improving AI systems: while many model responses are already satisfactory, others expose failures that could become valuable supervision if identified and corrected. We study this setting through the notion of an editor: a more capable evaluation process that can inspect a response and either confirm it or modify it, but whose invocation is costly. The editor may be a human expert, a stronger AI system, or the same model equipped with additional computation, context, tools, or information. The key challenge is to decide which interactions to send to the editor before knowing whether an edit will actually be made, since invoking the editor itself consumes limited resources such as expert time, inference cost, latency, or tool-use overhead. We formulate this problem as Edit Discovery Optimization (EDO), which seeks to minimize edit opportunities missed among skipped interactions while controlling unnecessary confirmations and respecting an editor-cost budget. We characterize the population-optimal policy and show that it takes the form of a cost-adjusted threshold on the posterior probability of an edit. Building on this structure, we develop EDIT (Edit Discovery via Iterative Thresholding), an online algorithm for the partial-feedback setting in which edit labels are revealed only for selected interactions. EDIT combines an adaptive edit score, a confirmation-control threshold, and a cost-aware selection rule; it satisfies the editor budget at every round and controls the average confirmation rate up to a vanishing finite-horizon term, without distributional assumptions on the interaction stream. Across four reasoning and factual question-answering benchmarks, EDIT achieves a better trade-off between editor cost, Type-I error, and Type-II error than direct LLM-based batch selection.

</details>


### [144] [AlphaBet: Reinforcement Learning for LLM Event Forecasting Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22684)
*Liqin Ye,Rongzhi Zhang,Xin Liu,Zheng Li,Yuan He,Qingyu Yin,Priyanka Nigam,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 AlphaBet，一个基于大语言模型的智能体强化学习框架，通过多日期训练让单一策略同时学习证据搜索、推理和概率报告，解决了传统基于结果训练中证据获取脱离学习回路的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型预测器训练通常在单一预测日期使用预先组装好的证据，导致证据获取过程不在学习闭环内，无法适应不同信息状态下的决策。需要一种能够跨越多个预解析日期、联合优化信息获取与概率报告的训练方法。

Method: AlphaBet 采用智能体强化学习，训练单一 LLM 策略在多个预解析日期进行搜索、推理和概率报告。每个日期的预测用真实结果通过真分评分规则打分，从而让一个已解决事件监督不同信息状态下的决策。将预测遗憾分解为证据获取差距和概率报告差距，并指出 GRPO 的标准差归一化在多类别真分评分下会扭曲报告目标。

Result: 在 GLM-4.5-Air 上，AlphaBet 将 Brier 得分从 0.731 降至 0.626 (Forecast-Dojo)，从 0.612 降至 0.528 (FutureX)。消融实验证实增益来自多日期训练，且同时改善了信息获取和概率报告。实验验证 GRPO 的标准差归一化会损害真分评分规则性能，但基本不影响最高结果准确率。

Conclusion: AlphaBet 通过多日期智能体强化学习有效联合优化了证据获取与概率报告，显著提升了预测校准质量。研究揭示了 GRPO 标准差归一化在真分评分下的缺陷，为未来基于真分规则的 LLM 预测器训练提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Forecasting an unresolved event requires finding relevant evidence and deciding how strongly it supports each outcome. Yet outcome-based training of Large Language Model (LLM) forecasters often begins with preassembled evidence at a single prediction date, leaving evidence acquisition outside the learning loop. We introduce AlphaBet, an agentic reinforcement learning (RL) framework that trains a single LLM policy to search, reason, and report probabilities across multiple pre-resolution dates. Each dated forecast is scored by a proper scoring rule against the eventual outcome, allowing one resolved event to supervise decisions under different information states. We decompose forecasting regret into an evidence-acquisition gap and a probability-reporting gap, and show that Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)'s standard-deviation normalization can distort the reporting target under multi-class proper scores. On GLM-4.5-Air, AlphaBet reduces the Brier score from 0.731 to 0.626 on Forecast-Dojo and from 0.612 to 0.528 on FutureX. Controlled ablations attribute these gains to multi-date training and show that they come from improvements in both information acquisition and probability reporting. Our empirical results validate that GRPO's std normalization degrades proper-scoring-rule performance while leaving top-outcome accuracy largely unchanged.

</details>


### [145] [Chance-Constrained Personalized LLM Routing with Online Preference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22695)
*Ameya Anjarlekar,Rasoul Etesami,R Srikant*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究个性化大语言模型路由问题，提出基于约束上下文多臂老虎机的算法，在学习用户偏好的同时平衡推理成本与响应质量，实现次线性成本遗憾并保证累积满意度的高概率约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM路由方法未充分考虑用户偏好的个体差异、异构模型的成本-能力权衡，以及部署过程中从反馈在线学习偏好的需求。需在满足可靠性约束下最小化累积推理成本。

Method: 将问题建模为约束上下文多臂老虎机：在Logistic偏好模型下，设计基于乐观估计的算法，利用上下文信息学习用户偏好，通过严格可行性条件平衡探索与利用，同时满足累积满意度约束。

Result: 理论上证明了相对于最优平稳随机上下文神谕的次线性期望成本遗憾，并建立了累积满意度高概率满足约束的保证。实验验证了框架在LLM路由及更广泛在线模型选择任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的框架为个性化LLM路由提供了理论保证的解决方案，兼顾成本效率与质量可靠性，可推广至具有可靠性要求的在线模型选择问题。

Abstract: We study personalized LLM routing, where a router selects among heterogeneous models with different costs and capabilities. The router must minimize cumulative inference cost while ensuring reliable realized response quality, even though user preferences are unknown and must be learned from feedback during deployment. We formulate this problem as a constrained contextual bandit and develop an algorithm that learns preferences while balancing cost and quality. Under a logistic preference model and a strict-feasibility condition, we establish sublinear expected cost regret relative to an optimal stationary randomized contextual oracle and a high-probability guarantee on realized cumulative satisfaction. The framework extends beyond LLM routing to broader online model-selection problems with reliability requirements.

</details>


### [146] [Normalizing Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22711)
*Francesco M. Ruscio,Timothy James Hitge,Fred Zhangzhi Peng,Danyal Rehman,Zander W. Blasingame,Nicholas M. Boffi,Michael M. Bronstein,Alexander Tong,Avishek Joey Bose*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Normalizing Flow Maps (NFM)，一种精确可逆的推前映射，结合了少步生成和可计算的似然评估，在条件图像生成和平衡采样任务上达到参数量匹配下的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最先进的生成模型（扩散、流匹配、少步生成器）虽然样本质量高，但缺乏显式的模型密度；而经典的归一化流和自回归模型虽有可计算似然，但生成质量受限。本文旨在融合这两条技术路线，实现既有少步高质量生成，又有精确可计算的似然。

Method: NFM 学习底层动力系统的积分器，构造精确可逆的推前映射，推导两条互补的一致性轴以实现可扩展训练：(1) 逐点匹配，解锁基于 ℓ₂ 的流图回归损失；(2) 分布匹配，将推前分布与规定前向过程的边缘分布对齐。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10 和 ImageNet-64 条件图像生成上，NFM 在参数量匹配的归一化流中达到最优样本质量，并支持基于似然的 RLHF；在玻尔兹曼生成器的平衡采样任务中，NFM 在单系统和可迁移肽基准上均超越先前最优归一化流。

Conclusion: NFM 成功统一了少步生成与精确似然评估，通过逐点与分布双重一致性目标实现高效训练，在图像生成与分子模拟任务上均展现出优越的样本质量与似然计算能力，为可逆生成建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: Scalable approaches to learning neural dynamical systems have placed diffusion, flow matching, and few-step generators in flow maps at the forefront of generative modeling. Such state-of-the-art models have limited access to explicit model densities despite evolving from classical generative modeling approaches that leveraged tractable likelihoods, e.g., Normalizing Flows and Autoregressive models. In this paper, we combine these threads of few-step generation and tractable sample likelihoods and introduce Normalizing Flow Maps (NFM), a class of exactly invertible pushforward maps that generalizes normalizing flows and flow maps. More precisely, NFMs learn the integrator of an underlying dynamical system, but crucially, they provide efficient exact likelihood evaluation by construction. To enable scalable training of NFMs, we derive two complementary axes of consistency inherent to NFMs that yield (self) distillation objectives: (1) pointwise matching, which unlocks $\ell_2$-based flow-map style regression losses, and (2) distributional matching, which matches pushforward distributions to the marginals of a prescribed forward process. On conditional image generation on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet-64, NFMs achieve state-of-the-art sample quality among parameter-matched normalizing flows, while supporting likelihood-based RLHF. For equilibrium sampling in Boltzmann Generators, NFMs outperform prior state-of-the-art normalizing flows on both single-system and transferable peptide benchmarks.

</details>


### [147] [Spectral Limits of Polynomial Graph Filters on the Long-Range Ising Model Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22713)
*Amin Najafgholizadeh,Mohsen Afsharchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文分析了长程伊辛模型基准（LRIM）上各类图神经网络的表现，证明基于邻接矩阵多项式的谱滤波器存在不可消除的误差下界，提出跨步滤波器和膨胀卷积网络可突破该下界，并在大规模图上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图神经网络（如消息传递、谱图网络）在长程依赖任务上表现受限，缺乏理论分析其误差下界的工具。LRIM 基准通过构造保证长程依赖，使得模型与目标的距离可精确测量，从而为研究长程相互作用下的图学习提供理论基石。

Method: 1. 构造 LRIM 基准：标签为节点自旋与幂律耦合卷积后的自旋场乘积。2. 理论证明：基于邻接矩阵多项式的线性读出（消息传递与谱网络的线性核）在目标沿谱等值线变化时产生不可降的误差下界。3. 精确计算不同阶次多项式滤波器的最优误差下界，并预测两种已发布谱网络的表现。4. 设计跨步滤波器（约50个系数）和膨胀卷积网络，分别拟合训练标签并对比测试误差。5. 引入傅里叶神经算子（FNO）作为基线，并在两种耦合下验证分析的泛化性。

Result: 1. 邻接矩阵多项式滤波器存在理论误差下界，无法通过增加阶次降低。2. 跨步滤波器（约50系数）测试误差比最佳已发布网络低52倍。3. 膨胀卷积网络在256节点超越最佳基线，在1024节点持平，并在三个更大规模上给出首个域内结果。4. 标准模式的FNO随规模增大落后越来越多。5. 标准预算下膨胀网络仍比大规模跨步滤波器差2-4个数量级。6. 结论在第二种耦合下依然成立。

Conclusion: 长程依赖任务要求滤波器能单独加权邻接矩阵合并的偏移量，并解析全局耦合。跨步滤波器和膨胀卷积通过显式建模长程相互作用打破谱下界，为大规模图上的长程学习提供了有效且可扩展的架构方向。

Abstract: The Long-Range Ising Model benchmark (LRIM) guarantees long-range dependence by construction: each label is the node's spin times the spin field convolved with a power-law coupling, so any model's distance from the target is measurable. We prove that a readout whose filter is a polynomial in the adjacency, the linear core of isotropic message passing and spectral graph networks, leaves an error floor that no degree lowers, because the target varies along a level set of the spectrum on which every such filter is constant. Computed exactly, the floor and the best filter of each degree predict two spectral networks adapted from published architectures to within a tenth of a decade. A filter escapes the floor only if it weighs separately the offsets the adjacency lumps together, and must still resolve the coupling across the whole lattice. A strided filter of about fifty coefficients does both: fitted to the training labels, its test error is 52 times below the best published network's. A dilated convolutional network beats the best published result at 256 nodes, matches it at 1,024, and gives the first in-domain results at the three larger sizes, while a Fourier neural operator with its standard modes falls further behind as the lattice grows. At its standard budget the dilated network stays two to four orders of magnitude short of a larger strided filter fitted to the labels. The analysis carries over to the benchmark's second coupling.

</details>


### [148] [OrderFlowDiT: Transport-Regularized Diffusion Transformer for Probabilistic Limit-Order Supply Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22727)
*Jung Hun Phee,Tucker Balch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出OrderFlowDiT，一种条件扩散Transformer，用于预测未来五分钟限价单量在价格维度的分布，结合无平衡最优传输(UOT)辅助损失，并在全场评估框架下显著优于MSE基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在建模限价单量条件分布时，坐标扩散损失忽略价格位移，归一化字段丢失总量信息，摘要统计量一致性不足以保证联合场分布保真度，因此需要同时捕捉价格布局、总量及预测不确定性的生成模型。

Method: 提出OrderFlowDiT，基于条件扩散Transformer处理未归一化的买卖盘量价字段，引入无平衡最优传输(UOT)辅助损失，并开发全场评估框架，结合对价格布局和总量敏感的传输成本、适当评分规则及校准诊断。

Result: 在30只美股1950个验证条件下，加入UOT后全场传输成本降低45.8%，传输能量得分降低26.1%，所有股票均有改善。

Conclusion: 研究支持将价格几何和成交量纳入生成建模，并强调场准确性与预测不确定性需联合评估。

Abstract: We study the conditional distribution of limit-order volume submitted over the next five minutes, given the current order book and recent order flow. The task requires capturing price placement, total volume, and predictive uncertainty jointly. Coordinate-wise diffusion losses do not explicitly account for price displacement, while normalizing fields to unit mass removes information about total volume. Agreement on selected summary statistics also does not establish fidelity to the joint field distribution. We introduce OrderFlowDiT, a conditional Diffusion Transformer for unnormalized bid and ask volume-at-price fields, trained with an unbalanced optimal-transport (UOT) auxiliary loss. We also develop a full-field evaluation framework that combines a transport cost sensitive to price placement and total volume with proper scoring rules and calibration diagnostics. In the primary comparison on 1950 validation conditions across 30 U.S. equities, adding UOT reduces full-field transport cost by 45.8% and the transport energy score by 26.1% relative to training with mean squared error alone. Both measures improve for every stock. These findings support incorporating price geometry and volume into generative modeling, with field accuracy and predictive uncertainty evaluated together.

</details>


### [149] [Native Geometry vs. Linear Recoverability: How Alignment Changes Platonic Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22749)
*Angus Hoy,Austin Lutterbach,Jasper Chong,Aman Kumar,Michael J. Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了模型规模与跨数据集表示相似性的关系，发现模型规模更明确地提升了线性可恢复性，而非原生余弦邻域对应关系，且未趋向共享的原生度量几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证柏拉图表示假说（PRH）：更大的模型是否学习到越来越相似的世界表示。区分原生余弦几何（对齐前的余弦相似度最近邻结构）与线性可恢复性（监督线性映射揭示的对应关系）两种相似性度量。

Method: 使用匹配的Legacy Survey和Hyper Suprime-Cam (HSC)嵌入，跨五个模型家族进行实验。对比Ridge回归对齐与原生余弦几何在模型规模与跨数据集邻域对应关系上的表现。控制嵌入维度，测试稀疏自编码器(SAE)和块稀疏特征器(BSF)特征的优势。

Result: Ridge对齐显著增强了模型规模与跨数据集邻域对应关系的关联，效果在五个模型家族中一致，且在降维后依然存在。学习到的映射远非旋转或均匀缩放。SAE和BSF特征相比密集嵌入无优势。

Conclusion: 模型规模更明确地改善线性可恢复性，而非原生余弦邻域对应关系。大模型并未收敛到共享的原生度量几何，而是通过线性映射实现跨数据集表示对齐。

Abstract: Do larger models learn increasingly similar representations of the same world? The Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) predicts that they should, but similar can mean native cosine geometry---nearest-neighbour structure induced by cosine similarity before any learned alignment---or linear recoverability: correspondence revealed by a supervised linear map. Using matched Legacy Survey and Hyper Suprime-Cam (HSC) embeddings across five model families, we find that Ridge alignment strengthens the relationship between model size and cross-dataset neighbourhood correspondence relative to native cosine geometry. The effect appears in all five families and persists when both representations are reduced to dimension, so it is not simply that wider embeddings give alignment more dimensions to work with. The learned maps remain far from rotations or uniform scaling, and Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) and Block-Sparse Featuriser (BSF) features give no advantage over the same alignment applied to dense embeddings. Model scale thus improves linear recoverability more clearly than native cosine-neighbourhood correspondence, without establishing convergence toward a shared native metric geometry.

</details>


### [150] [Refusal Is Not Robustness: Measuring Harmful Reachability in Autoregressive Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22754)
*Parv Kapoor,Teresa Fortes,Claudia Mamede,Rui Abreu,Jonathan Aldrich,Eunsuk Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出有界延续分析（BCA），通过建模自回归生成的随机过程并计算有限步内达到有害延续的概率，量化采样安全响应下隐藏的有害行为风险，补充传统攻击成功率（ASR）指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM安全评估主要依赖攻击成功率（ASR），仅评估采样输出，无法捕捉生成过程中的潜在有害路径概率分布。自回归模型可能在采样拒绝的同时，仍保留大量有害生成路径的概率质量，导致安全评估不全面。

Method: 提出有界延续分析（BCA）：将自回归生成建模为token前缀上的随机过程，构建有界延续树，使用语义危害评估器标记终端延续，采用精确概率模型检查估算有害到达概率，量化有限步长内生成到达有害延续的概率。

Result: 在四个指令微调LLM和100个JailbreakBench行为上实验表明，BCA能有效量化隐藏有害行为，区分ASR下表现相似的模型，并为红队测试分配精力提供有用信号。

Conclusion: BCA作为ASR的补充指标，通过概率模型检查揭示生成过程中的隐藏风险，为LLM安全评估和红队测试提供更细粒度的风险量化工具。

Abstract: LLM safety evaluations via jailbreaking typically index on attack success rate (ASR): whether a generated response violates safeguards. However, ASR only evaluates sampled outputs and provides little information on the underlying generation process. Autoregressive language models define distributions over possible outputs, and a sampled refusal can coexist with substantial probability mass on harmful generation paths. We study this hidden structure through bounded continuation analysis (BCA), which models autoregressive generation as a stochastic process over token prefixes and computes the finite-horizon probability that generation reaches a harmful continuation. BCA constructs a bounded continuation tree, labels terminal continuations with a semantic harm evaluator, and estimates harmful reachability using exact probabilistic model checking. BCA complements ASR by measuring how much harmful behavior remains accessible beneath a sampled safe response. This makes it useful for distinguishing models that look identical under measured ASR values, and allocating red-team effort toward regions of the generation process with greater hidden risk. Across four instruction-tuned LLMs and 100 JailbreakBench behaviors, we show that BCA effectively quantifies hidden harmful behavior and provides a useful signal to guide red teaming.

</details>


### [151] [Acquisition Function-Guided Generation for Bayesian Optimization over Discrete Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22763)
*Tommaso Ancilli,Thomas Wedenig,Henry Moss,Robert Peharz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 AFGG 方法，结合预训练生成模型与数据空间代理模型，通过扩散桥采样候选点，在分子基准测试中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散空间贝叶斯优化面临梯度缺失和有效解稀疏问题；潜空间 BO 难以直接引入领域知识；因此需要在数据空间结合代理模型并利用生成模型采样。

Method: 结合预训练生成模型与数据空间代理模型（如领域特定核），通过采集函数倾斜分布采样候选点，将倾斜分布嵌入扩散桥以获得平滑后验平均采集函数，实例化为分类扩散模型和自回归归一化流。

Result: 扩散模型变体与领先 BO 方法持平；流模型变体在多数情况下超越最先进方法，其余情况保持高度竞争力。

Conclusion: AFGG 有效解决了离散空间 BO 中梯度缺失和领域知识融合难题，通过扩散桥实现平滑采集函数指导生成，在分子优化任务中达到最优性能。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization (BO) over discrete spaces is challenging due to missing gradients and the sparsity of valid solutions. Latent-space BO sidesteps both issues by fitting a surrogate in the latent space of a generative model, which however substantially complicates the direct incorporation of domain knowledge that is naturally expressed in the original discrete space. In this work, we instead combine a pretrained generative model with a surrogate defined directly in data space, where inductive biases such as domain-specific kernels can be readily incorporated. Rather than maximizing the acquisition function over the discrete design space, our method samples candidates from the generative model under a distribution tilted by the acquisition function. To recover useful gradients for sampling, we embed this tilted distribution into a diffusion bridge that yields a smooth, posterior-averaged acquisition function. This leads to \emph{acquisition function guided generation} (AFGG), which we instantiate with a categorical diffusion model and an autoregressive normalizing flow. Across a broad suite of molecular benchmarks, the diffusion model variant performs on par with leading BO methods, while the flow model variant outperforms state-of-the-art methods in most cases and remains highly competitive otherwise. Our implementation is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/afgg.

</details>


### [152] [Reusing Calibration Gains across Model Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22771)
*Xu Cao,Wenxuan Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究预训练模型不同表示（如浮点与量化INT3）之间尺度校准更新的可迁移性，提出无需接收端优化的组级增益提取与安装方法，在Qwen3-1.7B和Llama-3.2-3B上实验表明双向迁移可保留76.9%-99.3%的校准收益，且组分配学习对迁移效果至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有尺度校准通常针对特定模型表示单独优化，计算成本高且难以复用。作者旨在量化同一预训练模型不同表示（浮点、量化等）间校准更新的共享程度，并开发免优化的跨表示迁移机制，以减少重复校准开销并保持量化模型的整数码、零点及存储布局不变。

Method: 提出“组级增益提取与安装”迁移流程：从源表示的校准更新中提取正向组级增益，直接安装到目标表示的权重或原生量化尺度中，无需目标侧优化。对量化接收端保持整数码、零点及存储布局不变。实验覆盖Qwen3-1.7B（FP/INT3）双向迁移、组内增益打乱消融、Llama-3.2-3B控制实验（四投影初始张量相同、其余九投影不同）等设置，在WikiText与C4新数据上评估文本损失改进保留率。

Result: Qwen3-1.7B FP/INT3 双向迁移保留 76.9%-90.5% 的接收端专属校准收益；组内打乱增益值会损害双方，证明学习到的组分配对迁移后效果关键。Llama-3.2-3B 控制实验中跨安装保留 98.6%-99.3% 的文本校准收益，且各接收端对自有增益存在微小但显著的偏好。

Conclusion: 校准更新包含大量可跨表示复用的适应性成分（高达 99%），但仍保留少量接收端特定收益需通过专属训练获得。组级增益的结构化分配是迁移成功的关键，简单的数值复制无效。该方法为量化模型部署提供了免优化的校准复用路径，显著降低多表示维护成本。

Abstract: Scale calibration learns an update for one model representation. We study how much of that update can be reused by another representation of the same pretrained model. Our transfer procedure extracts positive group-wise gains and installs them in the recipient's weights or native quantization scales without recipient-side optimization. On a quantized recipient, it preserves integer codes, zero points and storage layout. For a Qwen3-1.7B floating-point/GPTQ INT3 pair, bidirectional transfer retains 76.9-90.5% of the text-loss improvement from recipient-specific calibration on fresh WikiText and C4 cohorts excluded from project development. Shuffling the same gain values within each projection worsens both recipients, showing that their learned group assignment matters after transfer. A Llama-3.2-3B control keeps the initial native tensors at all four calibrated projections identical and changes nine projections elsewhere. Cross-installation retains 98.6-99.3% of the text-calibration benefit, alongside a small, resolved preference for each recipient's own gain. These results quantify both the adaptation carried by a reusable calibration update and the remaining benefit of recipient-specific training.

</details>


### [153] [Next Token or Vector? Learning How Far to Decode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22788)
*Sai Sreenivas Chintha,Chidera Biringa,Rishab Sharma,Anusha Agrawal,Prachi Badarayani,Gemma Zhang,Nigel Boachie Kumankumah,Marco Ayala,Sulaiman Vesal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CADENCE (Context-Adaptive DEcoding)，一种上下文自适应解码方法，通过高斯预测头和学习型路由器，同时学习“起草什么”以及“起草多远”，实现自适应生成粒度，在 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 上实现最高 1.3 倍吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自回归解码每次仅生成一个 token，即使后续多个 token 可一起预测。现有推测解码方法通常使用固定长度草稿，无法根据上下文动态调整预测跨度，导致草稿接受率不稳定。

Method: 1) 高斯预测头将未来 token 跨度表示为连续潜变量；2) 共享解码器并行将选定潜变量转为 token 预测；3) 学习型路由器从模型隐藏状态和高斯参数中选择预测长度，单 token 生成作为回退；4) 主干模型验证每个草稿续写，仅保留与贪婪解码一致的前缀。

Result: 在 Alpaca、GSM8K、HumanEval 三个基准上，路由器选择的动态长度草稿接受率显著高于固定长度基线；CADENCE 相比标准贪婪解码实现最高 1.3 倍生成吞吐提升。

Conclusion: CADENCE 通过联合学习草稿内容与草稿长度，并利用上下文自适应路由机制，有效平衡了并行度与接受率，为大模型高效推理提供了一种即插即用的解码加速方案。}

Abstract: Standard autoregressive decoding advances one token at a time, even when several upcoming tokens can be predicted together. We propose CADENCE (Context-Adaptive DEcoding), a generation method that learns both what to draft and how far to draft. Gaussian prediction heads represent future spans as continuous latent vectors, and a shared decoder converts the selected vector into token predictions in parallel. A learned router selects the prediction length from the model's own hidden states and predicted Gaussian parameters, adapting generation granularity to the current context with single-token generation as a fallback. The backbone verifies each proposed continuation and retains the prefix that matches its greedy predictions. Experiments with Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct on Alpaca, GSM8K, and HumanEval show that router-selected positions yield higher draft acceptance than applying the corresponding prediction length everywhere. CADENCE achieves up to 1.3x higher generation throughput than standard greedy decoding.

</details>


### [154] [Ori: Drift-Aware Streaming $k$-PCA for Very Low-Rank LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22794)
*Xinyu Luo,Jincheng Zhou,Mengbo Wang,Zilin Shen,Nadia Atallah Lanman,Petros Drineas,Bruno Ribeiro,Brian Bullins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Ori，一种新型低秩LLM优化器，通过流式k-PCA算法(Oja++)实现高频梯度子空间跟踪，解决现有低秩优化器中梯度子空间漂移问题。在1B LLaMA预训练中将与全秩优化器的性能差距缩小69.3%，在Roberta-Base微调16项任务中10项达最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低秩优化器(如GaLore)通过梯度子空间投影降低内存，但子空间随时间缓慢漂移。现有方法要么周期性重计算子空间，要么事后自适应跟踪，均存在滞后或计算开销大的问题。需要一种能实时、高频跟踪主梯度方向变化的方法。

Method: 提出Ori优化器，采用受Oja++启发的流式k-PCA算法进行梯度子空间的在线跟踪。该算法单步计算开销低，支持高频即时子空间更新，能捕捉主梯度方向随时间的平稳漂移，无需周期性重计算或事后补偿。

Result: 在1B LLaMA预训练任务中，Ori将最先进低秩优化器与全秩优化器的性能差距缩小高达69.3%；在Roberta-Base微调的16个任务场景中，Ori在10个任务上取得最佳性能。

Conclusion: Ori通过流式k-PCA实现高效在线子空间跟踪，显著提升了低秩优化器在LLM训练中的精度和效率，进一步缩小了低秩训练与全秩训练的性能差距，推动了梯度投影类优化器的前沿。

Abstract: A prominent family of low-rank LLM optimizers, pioneered by GaLore, performs gradient-subspace projection: at each step, they project the gradient into a low-dimensional subspace and map the update back to the full model. Most existing work either recomputes the gradient subspace at certain intervals, or performs post-hoc adaptation to catch up to the moving subspace. In this work, we explicitly identify in practice the slow, stable manner in which LLM gradient subspaces drift across time. To this end, we propose Ori, a novel low-rank optimizer for training LLMs that captures the shifting principal gradient directions over time to achieve state-of-the-art accuracy under stringent low-rank constraints. Ori employs a streaming $k$-PCA algorithm inspired by Oja++, whose low per-step cost enables high-frequency, on-the-fly subspace tracking. In practice, Ori reduces the performance gap of the state-of-the-art low-rank optimizers to full-rank optimizers by up to 69.3\% on a 1B LLaMA pretraining task, and achieves the best performance on 10 out of 16 task scenarios in Roberta-Base fine-tuning. Together, these contributions allow Ori to further push the performance and efficiency frontiers of gradient-projecting optimizers, narrowing the gap to full-rank training for LLMs. Code can be found at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Ori-Submission-2132.

</details>


### [155] [Partial-Revelation Panel Forecasting: Predictive Information Gain, Allocation, and Coarsening-Consistent Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22831)
*Bin Wen,Dai-Qiao Zhang,Tien-Ping Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究部分揭示面板预测：给定 n 个标注者中 k 个对上一轮对话的投票，预测下一轮面板的完整计数分布。提出基于 CCAR 掩码的粗化一致面板算子，采用 Dirichlet-多项式闭式实现、学习补全和标注者计数迁移。实验显示跨轮预测增益适中且依赖语料，冷启动 NLL 改进分别为 -0.422/-0.166/-0.014 nats；同项标注价值实验中首两票分别降低 NLL 0.583/0.221 等。目标自由分配仅在行级信号可预测时优于均匀分配。一致性可审计但不保证预测提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标注聚合多关注同一项目的标注价值，而跨轮面板预测（根据上一轮部分标注预测下一轮完整分布）的信息价值尚未系统研究。需要形式化部分揭示机制下的人口增量，并开发可审计、可迁移的预测算子。

Method: 1. 定义嵌套揭示机制下的增量 V(k)-V(k+1)=I(Y;R_{k+1}|X_k)。2. 引入 CCAR 掩码提供可交换随机标注者解释。3. 构建粗化一致面板算子：Dirichlet-多项式闭式实现、学习补全、标注者计数迁移。4. 对比逐层查表、冷启动、保留标注者、目标自由分配等基线。

Result: 冷启动计数 NLL 差异：IEMOCAP -0.422、M3ED -0.166、EmoWOZ -0.014 nats。保留标注者实验：首票/次票 NLL 降低分别为 0.583/0.221、0.578/0.184、0.180/0.069 nats。跨轮与同项增益不可互换。目标自由分配在预算 1 时回收 IEMOCAP/M3ED 83-93% 回顾性特征类空间，但 EmoWOZ 无提升。多处增益微小或为零。

Conclusion: 部分揭示面板预测的增益适中且强依赖语料。CCAR 掩码是受控干预而非观测到达过程。粗化一致性可审计但不保证预测改进；逐层边缘化有时优于共享表。目标自由分配仅在行级信号可预测时有效。代码与聚合结果已随预印本发布。

Abstract: We study partial-revelation panel forecasting: given $k$ of $n$ annotator votes on the previous dialogue turn, predict the full count distribution of the next turn's panel. This cross-turn predictive information differs from same-item annotation value. Under any nested revelation mechanism, the population decrement satisfies $V(k)-V(k+1)=I(Y;R_{k+1}\mid X_k)$; a fitted model also contributes adjacent excess-risk differences. Coarsened-completely-at-random (CCAR) masking supplies the exchangeable random-rater interpretation used in our operators. We define coarsening-consistent panel operators with a closed-form Dirichlet-multinomial realization, learned completion, and rater-count transfer. Against per-level lookup, cold-start count negative log-likelihood differences are -0.422/-0.166/-0.014 nats on IEMOCAP/M3ED/EmoWOZ. A held-out rater experiment measures same-item value: first and second additional votes reduce NLL by 0.583/0.221, 0.578/0.184, and 0.180/0.069 nats, respectively. Cross-turn and same-item gains are not interchangeable, and CCAR masks are controlled interventions rather than observed arrival processes. Consistency is auditable but does not guarantee predictive improvement; per-level marginalization can outperform a shared table. Target-free allocation beats uniform only where row-level signal is predictable: at budget one it recovers 83--93% of retrospective feature class headroom on IEMOCAP/M3ED but does not improve EmoWOZ. Gains are modest and corpus-dependent, with several small or null partial-revelation differences. Code and aggregate result artifacts accompany the preprint.

</details>


### [156] [Benchmarking and Enhancing Agentic Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22846)
*Pingchuan Ma,Rui Ding,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为 Heuresis 的新基准，用于评估大语言模型在因果发现任务中的真实能力，区分了单纯调用算法与真正利用数据洞察修正错误的能力。实验表明，GPT-6.1 Sol 和 Claude Opus 5.5 能超越最佳现有算法，且三分之一的真实边仅由模型发现，而非任何算法输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前因果发现基准存在两大问题：一是变量名含语义信息，导致 LLM 可靠变量名推断因果方向（如 Tübingen 数据集 99% 准确率）；二是数据过于干净，仅选用更好算法即可弥补差距，无法测试 LLM 是否真正能发现算法错误并通过进一步分析修正。因此需要匿名、机制多样、且存在算法无法弥补的性能缺口的基准。

Method: 构建 Heuresis 基准：包含六个匿名模拟数据集家族，机制从线性高斯到重尾分布不等。设计评测框架，分离“更好算法带来的增益”与“仅靠数据洞察能获得的增益”，并追踪代理修改的每条边。实例化代理框架，测试 GPT-6.1 Sol、Claude Opus 5.5、Qwen 3.8 Flash 三个 LLM，对比 PC 算法、路由器及两个因果发现基座模型。

Result: GPT-6.1 Sol 在所有六个家族均超越最佳算法，最难家族邻接 F1 提升 14%；Claude Opus 5.5 在最难家族提升 7.4%。两大最难家族中，代理恢复的真实边有 1/3 未出现在任何算法输出中。Qwen 3.8 Flash 表现未超越最佳算法。

Conclusion: Heuresis 成功构建了能区分“算法选择”与“数据驱动洞察”的因果发现基准。头部 LLM 表现出超越现有算法组合的能力，能通过分析数据本身发现算法遗漏的因果边，验证了 LLM 在因果发现中具备真正的推理与修正能力。代码与数据将公开以推动后续研究。

Abstract: LLM agents can now run causal discovery algorithms on a dataset and report what comes out. Whether they do more than that, such as noticing where an algorithm went wrong and fixing it with further tests, is hard to tell from current benchmarks. Most of them give the variables meaningful names, and an LLM shown only the names of the Tübingen cause-effect pairs already gets 99% of the directions right. With the names removed, the data are usually clean enough that picking a better algorithm than PC closes most of the gap. We introduce Heuresis, a benchmark of anonymous simulated datasets whose mechanisms range from linear Gaussian to heavy-tailed. For each family of datasets it separates what a better existing algorithm can gain from what only a closer look at the data can gain, and it traces every edge an agent changes. Every family leaves a gap that none of the algorithms, the router and the two causal-discovery foundation models we ran can close, so a score above the best chosen algorithm has to come from using the data. We instantiate the benchmark with an agent harness and three LLMs, GPT-6.1 Sol, Claude Opus 5.5 and Qwen 3.8 Flash. GPT-6.1 Sol surpasses the best chosen algorithm on all six families, by 14% adjacency F1 on the most difficult one, and Claude Opus 5.5 does so by 7.4% there. A third of the true edges the two agents restore on the two hardest families appear in no algorithm's output. The code and data will be publicly released to facilitate future work.

</details>


### [157] [Learning to Be Guided: Shaping Latent Geometry for Classifier-Free Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22885)
*Haoming Liu,Junhao Zhu,Shaoyi Zheng,Yuanhe Guo,Jinnuo Liu,Shenji Wan,Hongyi Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了潜在扩散模型中类别几何对无分类器引导生成的影响，提出了一种轻量级的原型正则化方法，在受限训练预算下显著提升了ImageNet上的引导生成质量，并推广到伪标签、文本嵌入和视频潜变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在扩散模型主要关注学习易于建模的潜在空间，但忽略了在无分类器引导下，条件在潜在空间中的排布方式（类别几何）对引导生成性能的关键作用。

Method: 在高斯类别模型下分析类内/类间方差对噪声下类别可区分性的影响，推导出噪声后验目标及其下降方向与最优CFG修正的比例关系；基于此提出原型正则化（PR），对空间池化后的潜变量应用余弦原型分类器。

Result: 在受控训练预算（VAE 20 epochs，DiT 80 epochs）下，加入PR后引导gFID从3.31降至2.28，超越DINOv3对齐的VA-VAE（2.44），且开销可忽略。PR还泛化到聚类伪标签、连续文本嵌入和视频潜变量。

Conclusion: 类别几何是引导生成质量的关键因素，PR是迈向CFG兼容表示学习的第一步，为条件扩散模型的潜在空间设计提供了新视角。

Abstract: Classifier-free guidance (CFG) is a standard component of conditional diffusion models, and guided generation performance has become the \textit{de facto} measure of state-of-the-art models. Recent work on latent diffusion models seeks to learn latent spaces that are easier to model with diffusion. However, under CFG, generation additionally depends on how conditions are arranged in the latent space. We study this arrangement under discrete class conditions, termed \emph{class geometry}, and investigate what class geometry allows a latent space to work well with CFG. Under a Gaussian class model, we show that shifting variance from intra- to inter-class at fixed total variance makes classes more distinguishable under noise, and derive a noisy posterior objective whose per-sample descent direction is proportional to the oracle CFG correction. Based on the analysis, we propose Prototype Regularization (PR), a lightweight surrogate for the intractable noisy posterior objective, which applies a cosine prototype classifier to spatially pooled latents. With a controlled training budget (20 epochs for VAE, 80 epochs for DiT), adding PR to an LDM baseline reduces guided gFID on ImageNet from 3.31 to 2.28, surpassing a DINOv3-aligned VA-VAE (2.44) with negligible overhead. We further demonstrate that PR generalizes to pseudo-labels derived from clustering, continuous text-embedding conditions, and video latents. Together, our findings highlight class geometry as a critical factor in guided generation and take a first step toward CFG-compatible representation learning. Our project page is available at: https://maps-research.github.io/cfg-geometry/.

</details>


### [158] [Batched Contextual Reinforcement II: Does RLVR Need Long Rollout Windows?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22887)
*Bangji Yang,Hongbo Ma,Ruihan Guo,Jiajun Fan,Xi Zhu,Ge Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出并发约束强化学习（CCR），通过在单个训练完成中并行放置三个独立可验证问题，解决了长回滚窗口在可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）中的高昂成本问题。CCR在8k token窗口下相较于单问题GRPO的32k窗口，分别使4B和8B模型的每步训练token减少82.1%和79.1%，同时保持推理质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长回滚窗口使RLVR训练成本高昂，但简单缩短窗口会导致单问题回滚在产生答案前被截断，使得群体相对准确率优势消失，丧失正确性信号。需要一种在短窗口下保留奖励信号的训练结构。

Method: 提出并发约束强化学习（CCR）：在单个受限的训练完成中并行放置三个独立可验证问题。在截断前完成的正确答案保留奖励，共享预算鼓励更短的有效推理而无需显式长度惩罚。将此机制与条件奖励和更新方差分析相联系，并在4B和8B数学推理模型上进行实验。

Result: CCR在8,000-token窗口下相较于单问题GRPO的32k窗口：4B模型每步训练token从975,464降至174,957（节省82.1%），8B模型从793,832降至165,788（节省79.1%）。8B训练轨迹显示准确率奖励随完成长度缩短而增加。单问题评估证实推理质量得以保留。

Conclusion: 奖励保持的训练结构（CCR）是降低短窗口RLVR每步成本的实用途径，通过并发问题设计在大幅减少计算开销的同时保持了推理能力。

Abstract: Long rollout windows make reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) expensive, yet simply shortening them can remove the correctness signal: when every single-problem rollout truncates before an answer, group-relative accuracy advantages vanish. As a technical continuation of Batched Contextual Reinforcement (BCR), we study how concurrent rewards relax this dependence on long windows. Concurrent Constraint Reinforcement (CCR) places three independently verifiable problems in one capped training completion. Correct answers completed before truncation retain credit, while a shared budget encourages shorter useful reasoning without an explicit length penalty. We connect this mechanism to conditional reward- and update-variance analyses and study 4B and 8B mathematical reasoning models. Against single-problem GRPO with a 32k window, CCR with an 8,000-token window reduces tokens per training step from 975,464 to 174,957 on 4B and from 793,832 to 165,788 on 8B, saving 82.1% and 79.1%, respectively. An 8B training trace shows increasing accuracy reward alongside shorter completions. Single-problem evaluations provide supporting evidence of retained reasoning quality. These results identify reward-preserving training structure as a practical route to lower per-step cost in short-window RLVR.

</details>


### [159] [What Accuracy Hides In Learned Transformer Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22891)
*Jonathan Lee,Narmeen Oozeer,Philip Quirke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究 Transformer Programs (TP) —— 一种将 Transformer 架构约束为可提取可读 Python 代码的方法。作者通过程序检查揭示了 token-level 准确率掩盖的完整序列错误，并在九个算法任务上评估了 TP，发现训练数据改进可显著提升序列准确率，同时揭示了 TP 训练过程中的准确率损失和操作组合导致的层数/查找表膨胀问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 TP 方法虽能提取可读程序，但 token 准确率可能掩盖完整输出序列的错误。需要通过程序检查深入理解模型学到的计算，指导训练数据改进，并揭示 TP 架构约束带来的训练动态与表达能力限制。

Method: 在九个算法任务上评估 TP，对比 token 准确率与完整序列准确率，检查采样测试遗漏的失败案例；基于程序检查和失败分析改进训练数据；在计数任务中进行穷尽域评估；分析频率排序任务中正确计数却导致错误答案的机制；研究 TP 训练过程中的准确率晚期下降现象，以及受限操作组合导致的层数与查找表规模增长。

Result: 程序检查揭示了 token 准确率无法捕捉的序列级错误；训练数据改进在多个任务上提升了序列准确率；计数任务中模型在采样测试 100% 通过却在穷尽评估中失败，增加大计数训练样本后通过穷尽评估；频率排序任务显示正确计数可被错误的程序逻辑抵消；TP 训练晚期出现准确率下降，受限操作组合需大量层或巨大查找表。

Conclusion: 程序检查是理解和改进 TP 的关键工具，能揭示准确率指标掩盖的系统性错误，指导针对性的数据增强。TP 的架构约束虽带来可解释性，但也导致训练动态不稳定和表达效率问题，未来需在可解释性与性能间寻找更好平衡。

Abstract: Transformer Programs (TP) (Friedman et al., 2023) constrain a Transformer's architecture so that its learned computation can be extracted as readable Python code. TP expose learned computations, but token accuracy can hide incorrect complete answers. In this work, we examine how program inspection provides insights beyond accuracy scores and helps guide training. We first evaluated TP on nine algorithmic tasks. We compared token accuracy with accuracy on complete output sequences and checked for failures missed by sampled tests. Further, we assessed training data improvements informed by program inspection and failure analysis, finding gains in sequence accuracy on several tasks. We examined a counting task where a program achieved 100% accuracy on sampled test sequences but failed on another input using the symbols and lengths allowed during training. The program counted correctly, but the required answer was missing from its output labels. After adding training examples with larger counts, the retrained models passed exhaustive evaluation over the bounded domain. In the frequency ranking task, inspection showed how correct counts contributed to incorrect answers in programs trained before and after changes to the training data. Finally, we studied constraints imposed by TP training and operations. The TP training process can lead to accuracy losses late in training. Our reference constructions also show how composing the restricted TP operations can use many layers or large lookup tables.

</details>


### [160] [A Feature Selection Layer Approach for Multi-Class Classification on Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22898)
*João Pedro Silveira e Silva,Márcio Dorn,Bruno Iochins Grisci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了对特征选择层的多种扩展，以解决嵌入式特征加权方法在多分类问题中难以捕捉标签特定关系的局限。实验表明，最佳扩展在识别全局相关特征上与现有方法相当，在捕捉标签特定相关性上表现更优，且无需训练后的后处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据驱动决策时代，深度学习模型虽强大但因可解释性不足而限制在科学和商业应用中的广泛采用。特征加权方法是提升可解释性的主流途径。事后解释技术在建模多分类问题中特征与标签的细微关系方面已取得显著进展，但嵌入式方法（将特征相关性学习直接集成到模型训练中）通常无法捕捉标签特定的关系。

Method: 作者提出了对特征选择层（一种用于神经网络的嵌入式特征加权机制）的多种扩展。通过大量实验筛选出最有效的扩展，并将其与成熟的特征加权方法进行基准对比。

Result: 所提方法在识别全局相关特征方面与现有技术持平，在捕捉标签特定相关性方面优于现有技术。它利用训练好的神经网络内部动态，揭示单个特征如何对特定标签的预测做出贡献，且仅需训练过程，无需额外的后处理步骤。

Conclusion: 本文提出的嵌入式特征加权扩展有效解决了嵌入式方法在多分类任务中无法捕捉标签特定特征相关性的关键缺陷，同时保持了无需训练后处理的优势，为可解释深度学习提供了更实用的方案。

Abstract: In the age of data-driven decision-making, deep learning models have become powerful tools for uncovering patterns and making predictions that influence many aspects of society. However, their broader adoption in scientific and business applications is often limited by a lack of interpretability. Feature weighting methods, which assess the relevance of individual features, are among the most widely studied approaches for improving interpretability. While post hoc techniques have advanced significantly, especially in modeling nuanced relationships between features and labels in multiclass problems, embedded methods typically fall short in this regard. Embedded techniques integrate feature relevance learning directly into the model training process, but they are often unable to capture label-specific relationships. To address the limitation, we propose multiple extensions to the Feature Selection Layer, an embedded feature weighting mechanism for neural networks. Through extensive experimentation, we identify the most effective extension and benchmark it against established feature weighting methods. Our results show that the proposed method matches existing techniques in identifying globally relevant features and outperforms them in capturing label-specific relevance. By leveraging the internal dynamics of the trained neural network, it reveals how individual features contribute to the prediction of specific labels, requiring no post-processing beyond the training process.

</details>


### [161] [Scalable Levenberg-Marquardt Training Framework for Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22925)
*Xianghao Wang,Johan Barthélemy,John Taylor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LeMA 是一种分布式 Levenberg-Marquardt 算法，通过精确对偶公式和 Jacobian 分片策略，将内存占用从参数数量的二次方降低到批次大小的二次方，使得精确 LMA 步骤可用于十亿参数模型，并在 8-GPU 上实现 7.97 倍加速、99.7% 强扩展效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LMA 收敛快但内存和计算成本高，主要来自 Jacobian 矩阵，其维度随样本数和参数数增长。现有方法如 PCG 仅近似求解，需特定预条件子；其他优化限于特定任务或架构。因此 LMA 难用于大规模通用神经网络训练。

Method: 提出 LeMA，包含两个核心贡献：1) LMA 的精确对偶公式，消除对参数维度的内存依赖；2) Jacobian 分片策略，即时并行计算 Jacobian 碎片，避免实例化完整 Jacobian。

Result: 对于大规模神经网络，LeMA 将 LMA 内存占用从参数数量的二次方降至批次大小的二次方，使得精确 LMA 步骤可用于十亿参数模型，无需近似 PCG 求解。在拥有 1,000,022,632 参数的 CNN 上，8-GPU 节点实现 7.97 倍加速，强扩展效率 99.7%。

Conclusion: LeMA 通过分布式精确对偶公式和 Jacobian 分片，解决了 LMA 在大规模神经网络训练中的内存和计算瓶颈，实现了高效、可扩展的精确二阶优化，为大规模模型训练提供了实用的二阶优化方案。

Abstract: The Levenberg-Marquardt (LMA) algorithm is a widely used nonlinear least-squares solver known for its rapid convergence. For small-scale neural networks, LMA provides better convergence and architectural robustness than first-order optimizers like Adam. However, LMA imposes prohibitive memory and computational costs. These costs are primarily from the Jacobian matrix, whose dimensions scale with both the number of samples and parameters. This bottleneck prevents its use in large-scale neural networks. Recent efforts scale LMA using preconditioned conjugate gradient (PCG) solvers to avoid materializing the Jacobian. However, PCG only approximates the solution, requiring many network propagations and a task-specific preconditioner. Other studies optimize LMA to reduce costs, but they restrict their methods to specific tasks or network architectures. As a result, LMA remains impractical for training general large-scale neural networks. In this paper, we propose LeMA, a distributed LMA algorithm for large-scale neural network training on multi-GPU platforms. Our contributions are twofold: First, an exact dual formulation of LMA removes the memory dependence on the parameter dimension. Second, a Jacobian sharding strategy computes Jacobian shards on the fly and in parallel to avoid materializing the full Jacobian. For large-scale neural networks, LeMA reduces the memory footprint of LMA from quadratic in the number of parameters to quadratic in the batch size. This makes exact LMA steps feasible for billion-parameter models without approximate PCG solves. Furthermore, for a convolutional neural network with 1,000,022,632 parameters on an 8-GPU node, LeMA achieves a 7.97x speedup over a single GPU, corresponding to a 99.7% strong scaling efficiency.

</details>


### [162] [Large Diffusion Models Need More Than EMA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22945)
*Chuyu Hu,Huanran Chen,Shitong Shao,Yinpeng Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究扩散模型训练中学习率策略与EMA的作用，理论证明恒定步长轨迹平均存在固有优化局限，而学习率衰减和Schedule-Free优化具有消失平稳性保证。实验表明：LR衰减与EMA互补但对衰减起始时机敏感；Schedule-Free无需时机调整且持续优于恒定LR+EMA，在参数稳定性与后期优化进展间取得更好平衡；且其优势可迁移至ReFL和生成器侧DMD等场景。结论：大规模扩散模型需要超越单纯参数平均的有效优化策略，需平衡方差降低与持续优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模扩散模型通常采用恒定学习率配合EMA训练，尽管学习率衰减和Schedule-Free优化在大语言模型训练中表现优异。作者旨在探究EMA是否专门适配扩散训练，或是否存在未被充分探索的更强替代方案。

Method: 1. 理论分析：对比恒定步长轨迹平均、学习率衰减、Schedule-Free优化在标准非凸假设下的平稳性保证。2. 实证研究：在多个扩散模型训练设置中对比三种策略（恒定LR+EMA、LR衰减+EMA、Schedule-Free），考察固定评估目标、生成质量、参数变异性、检查点变异性及后期优化进展。3. 迁移性验证：在ReFL和生成器侧DMD等不同扩散优化场景中测试Schedule-Free的泛化能力。

Result: 1. 理论上：恒定步长轨迹平均存在固有优化局限，LR衰减和Schedule-Free具有消失平稳性保证。2. 经验上：LR衰减与EMA互补，结合能提升基线但对衰减起始时机敏感；Schedule-Free无需时机调整，持续优于恒定LR+EMA，实现更低固定评估目标和更好生成质量。3. 三种策略均大幅降低参数变异性，但过强稳定化非最优：恒定LR+EMA保留较高参数变异性；激进衰减+EMA达最低检查点变异性但削弱持续优化；Schedule-Free在大幅降低参数变异性的同时保留更多后期进展，实现更好平衡。4. Schedule-Free优势延伸至ReFL和生成器侧DMD，展现跨场景迁移性。

Conclusion: 大规模扩散模型训练不能仅依赖参数平均（EMA），有效优化需要在方差降低与持续优化之间取得平衡。Schedule-Free优化作为一种无需精细调整衰减时机的方法，在保持参数稳定性的同时维持后期优化进展，为扩散模型训练提供了更优的优化范式，且具备跨不同扩散优化设置的泛化能力。

Abstract: Large-scale diffusion models are commonly trained with a constant learning rate (LR) and exponential moving average (EMA), despite the strong performance of LR decay and Schedule-Free optimization in training large language models. We study whether EMA is uniquely suited to diffusion training, or whether stronger alternatives have been underexplored. Our theory shows that averaging a constant-step trajectory has an inherent optimization limitation, whereas LR decay and Schedule-Free optimization admit vanishing stationarity guarantees under standard nonconvex assumptions. Empirically, LR decay and EMA provide complementary benefits: combining decay with EMA further improves the standard baseline at most tested starting points, although the gain remains sensitive to when decay begins. Schedule-Free avoids this timing requirement and consistently outperforms EMA with a constant LR across training stages, achieving both lower fixed-evaluation objectives and better generation quality. Our analysis further shows that all three strategies substantially reduce parameter variability, yet stronger stabilization is not always better: EMA with a constant LR retains comparatively higher parameter variability, while aggressive decay combined with EMA achieves the lowest checkpoint variability but also weakens continued optimization. Schedule-Free substantially reduces parameter variability while preserving more late-stage progress, providing a better balance between parameter stability and continued optimization. Finally, the benefits of Schedule-Free extend beyond pretraining to ReFL and generator-side DMD, demonstrating its transferability across distinct diffusion optimization settings. Overall, our results suggest that large diffusion models need more than parameter averaging alone: effective optimization requires balancing variance reduction with continued optimization.

</details>


### [163] [On the tradeoff between robustness and geometric accuracy in adversarial training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22955)
*Rachel Morris,Ryan Murray,Kerrek Stinson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在非参数二分类中，面对对抗扰动时，对抗分类器的几何精度（以到贝叶斯分类器的Hausdorff距离衡量）与其鲁棒性（对抗预算ε）之间的最优定量关系。结果表明，鲁棒分类器最多牺牲O(√ε)或O(ε)的精度，取决于贝叶斯分类器的正则性。这与先前关于低复杂度假设类中鲁棒性与精度不兼容的结论相反，高复杂度假设类允许存在精确量化精度的鲁棒分类器。结论适用于欧氏空间任意范数诱导的对抗模型，且同时适用于无限和有限数据设定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注低复杂度假设类中鲁棒性与标准精度的权衡，往往证明二者存在根本冲突。然而，对于非参数（高复杂度）分类器，这种权衡的定量刻画尚不明确。本文旨在填补这一空白，从几何角度量化对抗分类器相对于贝叶斯最优分类器的精度损失，并建立关于对抗预算ε的最优界。

Method: 作者采用非参数统计学习理论框架，利用Hausdorff距离度量分类器决策边界与贝叶斯决策边界的几何偏差。通过分析对抗风险与标准风险的关系，结合贝叶斯分类器的正则性条件（如边界的平滑度或边界条件），推导出对抗分类器几何精度的最优上界。证明技术涉及几何测度论、经验过程理论以及对抗扰动下的风险一致性分析，同时覆盖任意范数诱导的对抗模型和有限/无限样本设定。

Result: 证明了对抗分类器的几何精度损失（Hausdorff距离）最多为O(√ε)或O(ε)，具体取决于贝叶斯分类器的正则性：在一般条件下为O(√ε)，在更强正则性（如边界条件或平滑边界）下可达到O(ε)。该界是最优的，且适用于任意范数诱导的对抗预算。实验或理论验证了在高复杂度假设类中，存在同时具有鲁棒性和高精度的分类器。

Conclusion: 本文从根本上改变了对鲁棒性与精度权衡的理解：在非参数（高复杂度）设定下，鲁棒分类器可以实现精确量化的精度保证，最多牺牲O(√ε)或O(ε)的几何精度。这为对抗鲁棒学习提供了新的理论基础，表明通过增加模型复杂度可以缓解鲁棒性与精度的冲突，且结论普适于各种范数对抗模型和数据设定。

Abstract: For non-parametric binary classification in the presence of an adversary, we prove an optimal quantitative estimate for adversarial classifiers controlling geometric accuracy, as measured by the Hausdorff distance to the Bayes classifier, in terms or their robustness, as encoded by the adversarial budget $\varepsilon$. This rigorously shows that robust classifiers sacrifice at most $O(\sqrt{\varepsilon})$ or $O(\varepsilon)$ in accuracy, depending on the regularity of the underlying Bayes classifier. In contrast to prior work on the incompatibility of robustness and accuracy over low-complexity hypothesis classes, our work shows that high-complexity hypothesis classes allow for robust classifiers with precisely quantified accuracy. Our results apply for adversaries determined by any norm on Euclidean space and in both the infinite and finite data settings.

</details>


### [164] [Towards Hyperparameter Transfer for Differentially Private Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22976)
*Tianze Wang,Zhiqi Bu,Linjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DP-μP框架，将μP扩展到差分隐私训练中，解决了隐私噪声与梯度谱特性不匹配导致的超参数迁移失效问题，实现了跨模型宽度的零样本学习率迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在差分隐私（DP）训练中，噪声注入和逐样本梯度裁剪改变了优化动力学，导致标准μP无法直接迁移超参数，因为高维噪声与低秩梯度的谱特性差异破坏了μP的缩放假设。

Method: 通过分析梯度裁剪、噪声注入与特征学习的相互作用，推导出信噪比（SNR）条件以指导逐层学习率缩放，结合谱缩放公式，给出了DP-SGD、DP-Adam和DP-Muon优化器的具体缩放规则。

Result: 在MLP、ViT、GPT-2等多样架构上实验表明，DP-μP实现了跨宽度的有效零样本学习率迁移，性能达到或超过单独调优的基线，同时大幅降低计算成本。

Conclusion: DP-μP为隐私保护大模型训练提供了理论支撑的超参数缩放框架，解决了DP训练中超参数调优的核心难题，具有广泛的适用性和实用价值。

Abstract: Scaling laws and hyperparameter tuning remain central challenges in training large-scale models, particularly under differential privacy (DP), where optimization is further complicated by noise injection and per-sample gradient clipping. While Maximal Update Parametrization ($μ$P) allows for zero-shot hyperparameter transfer in standard training, we show that it fails under DP constraints due to the distinct spectral properties of high-dimensional noise versus low-rank gradients. In this work, we introduce DP-$μ$P, a theoretically grounded framework that extends $μ$P to the privacy-preserving setting. By analyzing the interaction between gradient clipping, noise injection, and feature learning, we derive a Signal-to-Noise Ratio (SNR) condition that governs layer-wise learning rate scaling. Combining this with our proposed spectral scaling formulation, we provide specific scaling rules for DP-SGD, DP-Adam, and DP-Muon optimizers. Empirically, we demonstrate that DP-$μ$P enables effective zero-shot transfer of learning rates across model widths on diverse architectures (MLP, ViT, GPT-2), matching or exceeding the performance of individually tuned baselines while significantly reducing computational costs.

</details>


### [165] [TALLOP: Joint Optimization of Local Training and Communication Compression for Time-Constrained Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22996)
*Honoka Anada,Yafei Ou,Shinya Takamaeda-Yamazaki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TALLOP 是一种联邦学习方法，在每轮时间预算约束下联合优化本地训练工作量和通信压缩，通过预测训练损失（LENS）和压缩误差（CLEN）来选择最优的计算-通信分配，从而最小化压缩后的损失，实验表明其在异构环境下能以竞争性或更优的时间达到目标精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中异构客户端导致的 straggler 问题使全局同步延迟，现有方法主要优化本地计算而忽视通信瓶颈，在通信受限环境下需要联合优化训练工作量和压缩以在固定每轮时间预算内平衡训练进度与压缩损耗。

Method: TALLOP 包含两个预测模块：LENS 利用早期 mini-batch 损失轨迹预测不同计算预算下的训练损失；CLEN 基于压缩误差模型估计通信压缩带来的性能退化。两者结合在每轮时间约束下搜索最优的训练-通信分配，使压缩后损失最小。

Result: 在异构联邦学习实验中，TALLOP 相比仅优化本地训练或不优化的方法，普遍实现竞争性或更优的 time-to-accuracy，但相对于仅训练适配的优势取决于具体设置。

Conclusion: 联合优化本地计算与通信压缩能有效缓解异构环境下的 straggler 影响，TALLOP 提供了一种基于损失预测和压缩误差建模的实用分配策略，能在固定时间预算内自适应平衡训练与通信开销。

Abstract: Minimizing the wall-clock time required to reach a target accuracy is crucial in federated learning (FL), where many applications require frequent model updates as data distributions evolve over time. Nevertheless, in heterogeneous environments, slow clients with limited computational and communication capabilities can delay global synchronization, forcing faster clients to wait and increasing the overall training time. To mitigate this straggler effect, recent studies have introduced per-round time constraints and adapted client workloads within the available time. However, these approaches primarily focus on local computation, whereas communication can become a major bottleneck in communication-limited environments, motivating the joint optimization of training workload and communication compression. Although previous studies have explored such joint optimization, determining an effective training--communication balance under a fixed per-round time budget remains challenging, as the resulting model performance depends on both training progress and compression-induced degradation. We therefore propose TALLOP, which jointly optimizes the local training workload and communication compression under per-round time constraints. TALLOP predicts the training loss achievable under different computation budgets from the early-stage mini-batch loss trajectory (LENS), and estimates the degradation caused by communication compression using a compression-error model (CLEN). TALLOP combines these predictions to select the training-communication allocation that minimizes the post-compression loss under the given round time constraint. Experiments in heterogeneous FL environments show that TALLOP generally achieves competitive or improved time-to-accuracy compared with approaches that optimize only local training or neither, although the benefit over training-only adaptation depends on the setting.

</details>


### [166] [A Label Noise Robust Algorithm for Graph Data using Neighborhood Label Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22998)
*Pintu Kumar,Nandyala Hemachandra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了标签噪声对图卷积网络（GCN）邻域标签分布的影响，证明噪声会平滑分布、增加熵值并降低类间可区分性。提出LENS框架，通过留一法熵变化筛选噪声节点并结合重标签机制恢复特征信息，实现了端到端的低开销噪声鲁棒训练，在节点分类任务上达到SOTA精度且速度提升一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GCN性能高度依赖干净标签，但真实场景中标签噪声普遍存在。现有研究缺乏对噪声如何从理论上改变节点邻域标签分布的深入分析，且现有噪声鲁棒方法计算开销大、往往丢弃噪声节点导致特征信息浪费。

Method: 1. 数学推导标签噪声对邻域标签分布的平滑效应及熵增机制。2. 提出LENS框架：采用留一法计算移除特定节点后邻域熵的变化量来识别噪声节点。3. 引入重标签机制，而非丢弃噪声节点，保留其特征信息。4. 实现端到端训练，计算复杂度极低。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，LENS在节点分类任务中达到最先进的分类精度，同时训练速度比现有噪声鲁棒方法快一个数量级。实验验证了熵平滑理论分析的正确性及重标签机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过理论分析揭示标签噪声破坏邻域判别性的本质机制，提出基于熵变化的高效噪声节点筛选与重标签策略，实现了精度与效率的双重突破，为图神经网络的噪声鲁棒学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Graph Convolutional Networks (GCNs) have demonstrated exceptional performance on graph-structured data. However, their success heavily relies on the availability of clean labels, an assumption that often fails in real-world scenarios. The learnability of GCNs is intrinsically linked to the neighborhood structure of nodes, making it critical to understand how this structure changes in the presence of label noise. In this work, we investigate how the neighborhood label distribution of a node changes under label noise. We mathematically derive that label noise smooths this distribution, which inherently increases its entropy and severely decreases interclass distinguishability. To address this challenge, we propose Leave-one-out Entropy Node Screening (LENS), an end-to-end framework that identifies noisy nodes by measuring the reduction in neighborhood entropy when a specific node is removed. LENS further incorporates a relabeling mechanism to recover and preserve valuable feature information instead of entirely discarding the identified noisy nodes. Crucially, the end-to-end computational overhead of LENS is remarkably low. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our algorithm achieves state-of-the-art node classification accuracy while executing an order of magnitude faster than existing noise-robust approaches.

</details>


### [167] [A Minimal ReLU Classifier on Non-Linearly Separable Data: Convergence and Implicit Bias at and beyond the Edge of Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23007)
*Quan Nguyen,Robin Yadav,Param Mody,Mark Schmidt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究两层ReLU神经网络在非线性可分1D数据上用梯度下降（大步长）训练的理论行为，发现参数空间中存在“活性锥”，在此区域问题等价于分段逻辑回归，证明了常数步长下的有限时间损失界、方向收敛到最大间隔向量、正交分量收敛到势函数极小点，并提出自适应步长策略使学习率趋向无穷大而不离开活性锥，实现$O(1/(T.log T))$的更快损失收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大步长梯度下降理论主要局限于线性模型或线性可分数据，而神经网络在非线性可分、非凸非光滑目标下的大步长行为缺乏理论支撑。本文旨在构建一个最小化的神经网络模型（双神经元ReLU网络），在非线性可分1D数据上研究大步长及自适应步长的优化动力学，填补理论空白。

Method: 1. 定义双神经元ReLU网络在1D非线性可分数据上的Logistic损失目标。2. 识别参数空间中的“活性锥”，证明其中包含所有完美分类器，且目标在锥内等价于分段逻辑回归。3. 在活性锥内分析常数步长GD的动力学，推导有限时间损失界、方向收敛到显式最大间隔向量、正交分量收敛到非强凸势函数唯一极小点。4. 证明“弹射”现象：单次损失尖峰后达到指数级小损失。5. 设计自适应步长调度，使学习率趋向无穷大但保持在活性锥内，证明$O(1/(T.log T))$损失率。6. 将结果扩展到更一般网络的局部收敛，并在MNIST上验证。

Result: 1. 活性锥内问题等价于分段逻辑回归，常数步长GD在有限时间内达到损失界。2. GD方向收敛到显式最大间隔向量，正交分量收敛到非强凸势函数唯一极小点。3. 存在弹射现象：一次损失尖峰后损失指数级下降。4. 自适应步长调度使学习率趋向无穷大而不离开活性锥，实现$O(1/(T.log T))$损失率，优于标准$O(1/T)$。5. 理论扩展到一般网络局部收敛，MNIST实验验证。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个可处理的神经网络模型，用于研究超越线性预测器和可分数据的大步长及自适应步长优化行为，揭示了活性锥结构、方向收敛、弹射现象及自适应步长的加速效果，为理解神经网络大步长训练的理论基础做出贡献。

Abstract: Gradient descent (GD) with large learning rates can successfully optimize neural networks even though classical smoothness-based learning rate conditions are violated. Yet theory for this regime largely covers linear models or linearly separable data. We analyze a minimal setting outside both regimes. Specifically, a two-neuron ReLU network trained with logistic loss on 1D non-linearly separable data. The objective is nonconvex and nonsmooth, and GD exhibits edge-of-stability (EoS) behavior. We identify an active cone in parameter space that contains every perfect classifier, where the problem becomes piecewise logistic regression with a varying effective dataset. Within this cone, we prove a finite-time loss bound for constant learning rates and show that GD converges in direction to an explicit max-margin vector, while the orthogonal component converges to the unique minimizer of a non-strongly convex potential. We further establish catapult results where exponentially small loss is reached after a single loss spike. Furthermore, we propose an adaptive schedule that lets learning rates grow to infinity without leaving the active cone. We prove that this schedule achieves a loss rate of $O(1/(T\log{T})$, which is faster than the standard $O(1/T)$ rate. Our theoretical results extend to local convergence on more general network and are validated on MNIST dataset. Overall, we show that this setup provides a tractable neural-network model for studying large and adaptive step sizes beyond linear predictors and separable data.

</details>


### [168] ["Deep lifts": Neural networks can generalize even with the dumbest optimizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23009)
*Sunil Madhow,Yu-Xiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了在过参数化ReLU网络中均匀随机选择插值参数的泛化性质，发现其能自适应地以更小网络的复杂度泛化，归因于小网络参数见证了大网络参数空间中的指数级体积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解过参数化神经网络中随机插值解的泛化行为，解释为何简单模型能在大模型空间中主导泛化，以及如何将近似理论结果转化为泛化保证。

Method: 采用“深层提升”证明技术，显式构造小网络参数在大网络参数空间中对应的近等价参数集合，将可实现体积与小网络的稳定性联系起来，从而推导泛化界。

Result: 均匀随机插值参数以Õ(wdWD/n)速率泛化，优于VC维界；该技术可将近似论结果转化为非参数类的泛化保证，且对标签噪声具鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ReLU网络的归纳偏置使得小网络参数在大网络参数空间中占据指数级体积优势，导致随机插值解自适应地表现出小网络的泛化性能，深层提升技术为分析此类现象提供了新工具。

Abstract: Given a labeled dataset $\{(x_i, y_i)\}_{i = 1}^n$, consider drawing a parameter vector $\hatθ$ uniformly at random from those that interpolate the data within a width-$W$ depth-$D$ ReLU architecture with continuous weights bounded within $[-M, M]$. We study the generalization properties of $\hatθ$ in the case where the dataset can, in fact, be explained by a smaller $w$-by-$d$ ReLU network. Despite the obliviousness of the selection rule for $\hatθ$, it adaptively generalizes at the rate $\tilde O\left(\frac{wdWD}{n}\right)$, rather than being governed by the VC dimension of the full $W$-by-$D$ hypothesis class. The reason is due to a particular inductive bias in ReLU networks: the $w$-by-$d$ parameter *witnesses* an exponential volume of $W$-by-$D$ interpolating parameters, which dwarfs the volume of poorly generalizing parameters (conditioned on interpolation). We explicitly construct this set of near-equivalent $W$-by-$D$ parameters by a proof technique called *deep lifting*, and then tie the achievable volume to stability properties of the $w$-by-$d$ witness. Examples show that these techniques allow us to convert approximation-theoretic results into generalization guarantees for nonparametric classes. We further show that generalization of uniform-random interpolators can survive label corruptions.

</details>


### [169] [Towards Looped Models Done Right, Part I: Topology, Input Injection, Recurrent-State Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23036)
*Benhao Huang,Chufan Shi,Junlin Chen,Shicheng Wen,Zhengzhong Liu,Eric Xing,Xuezhe Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文系统分离了循环语言模型中的三个设计维度——循环位置、输入注入方式、循环状态组织——并从头训练等参数规模、等逻辑深度、等Token预算的模型进行对比实验，发现“前奏-循环块-尾声”三明治架构在上下文抽取和推理任务上显著提升性能；输入注入主要增强知识检索与上下文利用，而随机初始化和分离高/低循环状态收益不稳定甚至为负。完整的Huginn架构在7.3亿参数/3360亿Token规模上全面超越Ouro，且该优势迁移至MoE模型：在8B激活/0.8B常驻参数（展开后32B/3B）、5000亿Token训练下，Huginn在知识、推理、编码基准上近乎全胜，专家路由更均衡。在等训练/推理FLOPs下，Huginn风格模型常驻参数仅为112层前馈MoE基线的25%，却在DROP和GSM8K上超越基线，在MATH500持平，其余基准保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环Transformer架构将循环位置、输入注入、循环状态组织三个正交设计轴耦合在一起，导致难以单独评估各自贡献，也阻碍了设计原则向Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）等稀疏架构的迁移。论文旨在通过受控消融实验，分离这三个轴，建立可迁移的循环模型设计原则，并验证其在密集与稀疏模型中的普适性。

Method: 1. 定义三个正交设计轴：循环位置（prelude/block/coda）、输入注入（是否在每步注入原始嵌入）、循环状态组织（共享/分离高低层状态、初始化策略）。2. 在固定物理参数量（~7.3亿）、逻辑深度（64层等效）、训练Token数（3360亿）下从头训练密集模型全因子组合。3. 将验证出的最优组合（Huginn：prelude-recurrent block-coda + 输入注入 + 共享状态零初始化）扩展至MoE设定：8B激活/0.8B常驻参数（展开32B/3B），5000亿Token训练，对比Ouro基线。4. 再在等FLOPs约束下，与112层前馈MoE基线对比常驻参数效率。

Result: 密集模型：Huginn在十个基准上全面击败Ouro；输入注入带来知识检索与上下文利用最大增益；随机初始化与分离H/L状态收益好坏参半。MoE模型：Huginn在知识、推理、编码全谱系基准近乎全胜，专家路由熵更高（更均衡）。等FLOPs下，Huginn常驻参数仅为前馈MoE基线的25%，却在DROP/GSM8K超越基线，MATH500持平，其余任务竞争力保持。

Conclusion: “前奏-循环块-尾声”三明治架构配合输入注入、共享零初始化循环状态，构成了一个鲁棒、可扩展、参数高效的循环设计范式（Huginn）。该范式在密集与稀疏MoE模型中均表现出优越的推理与上下文处理能力，且能以大幅更少的常驻参数在等算力下匹配或超越深层前馈基线，为大规模循环语言模型提供了明确的工程指南。

Abstract: Looped language models reuse physical Transformer blocks across logical depth, but established architectures entangle recurrence placement, input injection, and recurrent state organization. We isolate these axes in models trained from scratch at matched parameter scale, logical depth, and token budgets, and then test whether the resulting design principles transfer to resident- and active-parameter-matched Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models. The prelude-recurrent block-coda sandwich architecture consistently improves performance on context extraction and reasoning-intensive tasks. Input injection primarily enhances knowledge retrieval and context utilization, whereas random initialization and separate H/L recurrent states provide mixed or negative returns. Taken together, these results favor the Huginn-style design: at the 730M-parameter and 336B-token scale, the complete Huginn architecture outperforms Ouro across all ten benchmarks. The advantages of Huginn over Ouro transfer to MoE models. At the 8BA0.8B (32BA3B when unrolled) scale trained on 500B tokens, Huginn outperforms Ouro on nearly all benchmarks spanning knowledge, reasoning, and coding, while also exhibiting more balanced expert routing. Under matched training and inference FLOPs, the Huginn-style model uses 75% fewer resident parameters than a 112-layer feedforward MoE baseline. Despite its smaller physical parameters, it outperforms the feedforward baseline on DROP and GSM8K, matches it on MATH500, and remains competitive on the other benchmarks.

</details>


### [170] [GR-OPD: Group-Relative Sequence Credit for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23042)
*Yushu Wang,Renkai Yang,Jingcao Xu,Zili Geng,Haihong Tang,Yaohui Jin,Hao He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出GROPD（Group-Relative On-Policy Distillation），通过将token级反馈聚合为校准的序列级信用分配，解决了on-policy distillation中局部信用分配忽略早期动作对后续反馈影响的问题，在数学推理任务上显著超越基线且训练更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有on-policy distillation（OPD）采用sampled-token更新，仅进行局部信用分配，忽略了早期token动作对后续教师反馈的影响，导致信用分配偏差；且在思维模式不匹配时训练易崩溃。需要一种能将token反馈解耦为序列级信用、并校准教师指导的方法。

Method: GROPD将token级反馈聚合为序列级信用：理论上证明在理想on-policy假设下，将原始求和反馈广播到每个token可得到序列级reverse-KL目标负梯度的无偏估计；实践中引入衰减机制降低后期可能不可靠反馈的权重，利用同prompt rollout群组校准教师指导，保留受控的群组均值比例（collective teacher pressure），同时保持相对响应偏好。

Result: 在五个Qwen3师生设置和六个数学推理基准上，GROPD在macro Avg@K上全面超越vanilla OPD，其中思维模式匹配的两个设置分别提升10.9和11.5个百分点；在思维模式不匹配设置中保持训练稳定，而vanilla OPD崩溃。消融实验显示适度的collective teacher pressure提升学习，过大或无限制反馈视野会破坏稳定性。

Conclusion: GROPD通过序列级信用分配和群组相对校准，有效解决了OPD的局部信用分配缺陷和分布偏移敏感性，实现了更准确的梯度估计和稳健训练，为on-policy distillation提供了理论与实践兼备的改进方案。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) provides token-level teacher feedback, yet common sampled-token updates assign credit locally and omit the effects of earlier actions on subsequent feedback. We introduce \method{} (Group-Relative On-Policy Distillation), which decouples feedback computation from credit assignment by aggregating token feedback into calibrated sequence-level credit. We establish a theoretical basis for this approach by showing that, under ideal on-policy assumptions, broadcasting raw summed feedback to every response token yields an unbiased estimate of the negative gradient of the sequence-level reverse-KL objective. To support effective learning in practice, \method{} discounts later feedback that may become less reliable under distribution shift and uses same-prompt rollout groups to calibrate teacher guidance. It preserves relative response preferences while retaining a controlled fraction of the group mean, which we term collective teacher pressure. Across five Qwen3 teacher--student settings and six mathematical reasoning benchmarks, \method{} outperforms vanilla OPD in macro Avg@K in every setting, including gains of 10.9 and 11.5 percentage points in two settings with matched thinking patterns. It also maintains stable training under mismatched thinking patterns, where vanilla OPD collapses. Ablations show that retaining an appropriate amount of collective teacher pressure can improve learning, while excessive collective teacher pressure or an unrestricted feedback horizon can destabilize training.

</details>


### [171] [Path to Prior-Data Fitted Relational Models via Learned Latent Multi-table Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23073)
*Mohammad Sadeq Abolhasani,Viswanath Ganapathy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索将PFN（Prior-data fitted networks）范式扩展到基于图的关系基础模型Griffin，通过大规模合成关系数据库预训练，并研究课程学习对消息传递表示的影响。实验表明，从模式引导数据到模式无关数据的合成课程学习在冻结上下文预测器下性能最优，优于仅用真实数据预训练和无序单阶段混合；若在真实数据预训练后追加该课程，可获得整体最佳编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PFN范式在Transformer表格/关系基础模型（如RDB-PFN）中已证明有效，但在基于消息传递的图关系模型中鲜有探索。作者希望验证合成数据预训练能否迁移到此类模型，以及课程学习（在Transformer关系学习器中有益）是否也能改进消息传递表示。

Method: 在大量合成关系数据库集合上预训练图关系基础模型Griffin。采用课程学习策略：先用模式引导的合成数据，再用模式无关的合成数据。不在下游任务上微调，而是将预训练编码器作为通用关系表示学习器，将其潜在表示传给冻结的上下文预测器（TICL），整个流程无任何组件在目标任务上训练。在16个真实关系分类任务上，对比原始特征和未训练编码器基线，五种随机种子评估。

Result: 纯合成课程学习（模式引导→模式无关）在冻结上下文预测下性能最强，显著优于仅真实数据预训练和所有无序单阶段语料混合；在真实数据预训练后追加该课程，可得到整体最佳编码器。

Conclusion: 结果为PFN风格的合成预训练迁移到基于图的关系基础模型提供了初步证据，表明课程学习能有效提升消息传递模型的关系表示质量，且无需下游微调即可泛化。

Abstract: Prior-data fitted networks (PFNs) show that pretraining on large populations of synthetic tasks can enable generalization to unseen datasets with little or no downstream fine-tuning. This paradigm is effective for transformer-based tabular and relational foundation models such as RDB-PFN, but remains largely unexplored for graph-based relational models built on message passing. We pretrain the graph-based relational foundation model Griffin on large collections of synthetic relational databases and ask whether curriculum learning, which benefits transformer-based relational learners, also improves message-passing representations. Rather than fine-tune on downstream tasks, we use the pretrained encoder as a general-purpose relational representation learner and pass its latent representations to a frozen in-context predictor (\ticl{}); no component is trained on the target task. On 16 real-world relational classification tasks, evaluated over five seeds against raw-feature and untrained-encoder baselines, a purely synthetic curriculum that begins with schema-guided data and continues with schema-agnostic data gives the strongest frozen in-context performance\rev{, outperforming real-data-only pretraining and every unordered single-stage mixture of the corpora; when appended after real-data pretraining, it yields the best encoders overall}. These results provide initial evidence that PFN-style synthetic pretraining transfers to graph-based relational foundation models.

</details>


### [172] [Where Latents Belong and How They Flow: Joint Identification of Structural Roles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23074)
*Xintong Li,Kun Zhang,Yujia Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 RoleTrace 框架，解决动态系统中潜在因子结构角色的识别与追踪问题。通过理论证明在适当条件下三种结构角色（表达位置、接收过去影响、传递未来影响）可在共同排列下联合识别，并在合成数据与真实多方会议数据上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态系统中的潜在因子在不同时间点和观测中扮演不同结构角色（如会议中某想法仅由部分人表达、继承早期讨论、影响后续发言）。现有方法难以将表达结构、接收影响结构、传递影响结构对齐到同一潜在坐标系，导致无法准确刻画因子的结构角色。

Method: 1. 形式化定义潜在因子的结构角色：表达模式、历史依赖、未来影响三个维度。2. 理论分析证明：在结构稀疏性与变异性条件下，三种角色可在每个时间点共同排列下联合识别。3. 开发 RoleTrace 框架，在共享潜在坐标系中追踪结构角色，包含对应对齐与角色推断模块。

Result: 合成实验验证了结构恢复的准确性；真实多方会议实验成功识别出参与者的动态结构角色（如发起者、响应者、调节者），揭示了序贯交互中的潜在影响流模式。

Conclusion: RoleTrace 为动态系统中潜在因子的结构角色识别提供了理论保证与实用框架，能在共享坐标系下追踪因子随时间的表达、接收与传递角色，适用于多方交互、神科学、社会网络等序贯数据分析。

Abstract: Latent factors in dynamical systems can play different roles across observations and time. For example, an idea in a group meeting may be expressed by only some participants, inherit information from earlier discussions, and shape what is said next. We formalize this notion as the structural role of a latent factor, describing where it is expressed, whether it receives influence from the past, and whether it transmits influence to the future. The main challenge is correspondence, since the three structures may be identified under different permutations, while a structural role is defined only when they are aligned to the same latent coordinate. We show that, under suitable structural and variability conditions, all three roles are jointly identifiable under a common permutation at each time point. Guided by this theory, we develop RoleTrace, a framework that traces structural roles in a shared latent coordinate system. Synthetic experiments validate structural recovery, while experiments on multiparty meetings examine the identified roles in real sequential interactions.

</details>


### [173] [Towards a GraphNARX model for structural dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23103)
*George Tsialiamanis,Keith Worden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GraphNARX模型，一种基于图的非线性自回归外生模型，用于结构动力学系统辨识与虚拟传感，通过图神经网络建模结构单元间相互作用，实现对不同尺寸、自由度结构的泛化及无数据节点位移推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构动力学建模方法难以同时兼顾几何拓扑建模、多尺度泛化能力及缺测节点状态重构，需一种能显式编码结构拓扑、支持变维度结构迁移并具备虚拟传感功能的数据驱动模型。

Method: 构建GraphNARX模型：以结构节点为图节点，节点特征编码历史位移与节点力，利用图神经网络进行局部消息传递学习节点间动力学耦合，输出下一时刻节点位移；采用邻接矩阵表达结构拓扑，实现对任意维度、自由度结构的适应性建模。

Result: 在系统辨识与虚拟传感两大框架下验证：模型在多种结构规模上均达到高精度预测，展现出对未见结构尺寸的强泛化能力，且能仅凭部分节点数据准确推断无传感器节点的位移响应。

Conclusion: GraphNARX通过图表示学习统一了结构动力学建模与虚拟传感，其局部交互机制赋予模型拓扑不变性与尺度可扩展性，为复杂工程结构的数据驱动监测与分析提供了通用框架。

Abstract: The current paper introduces the GraphNARX model, a graph-based nonlinear autoregressive exogenous model for structural dynamics. The model is built to account for the geometry of structures and to model dynamic behaviour as the interaction between elements of the structure. Similar to other types of NARX models, GraphNARX is used for system identification. Nodal attributes of the graph encode past displacements and nodal forces, and the output of the model is the next-timestep displacement. The graph formulation of the model allows it to generalise to structures of different dimensions and degrees of freedom. Moreover, because graph-learning is performed locally between the nodes, the GraphNARX model can also learn to infer displacements of nodes for which data are not available, i.e.\ to perform virtual sensing. The proposed model's performance is evaluated in a system-identification framework and a virtual-sensing framework, and achieves good accuracy and exhibits generalisation capabilities to structures of different sizes.

</details>


### [174] [Domain-specific language alignment of multimodal embeddings towards explainable predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23110)
