We first show that the forward spectral law extends to random importance weights once one conditions on the weights together with the realized offspring counts, rather than on the weights alone. We then construct a regularized free-deconvolution estimator, prove its consistency with an explicit error budget at a fixed resolution scale, and certify model mismatch through a computable forward residual. The repaired spectrum enters the filter only as the preconditioner of a Metropolis-adjusted rejuvenation move, so that spectral error affects mixing efficiency but not the invariant target. For the full filtering cycle, we derive a geometrically summable error recursion under stable scalar dynamics and an explicit law for the cost of abstaining from repair. Pre-registered simulations show that the inversion reduces the spectral error by an order of magnitude, that the corrected preconditioner recovers about 90% of the oracle's sampling efficiency in an anisotropic regime, and that the cost of abstention depends on when abstentions occur rather than on how many. The repair recovers eigenvalue laws, not eigenvectors, and is certified only within an explicitly stated exogenous-weight regime.

</details>


### [209] [Predictable Scaling: Efficient Model Exploration via Scaling-Law Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23453)
*Aishwarya Wesanekar,Sheng-Tao Yang,Jiawei Zhang,Flavio Sales Truzzi,Chen Chen,Nader Beigiparast,Shengyuan Huang,Roshanak Zilouchian Moghaddam,Vitor Cid,Colin Clement,Vinayak Thapliyal,Sebastian Ament,Jihao Andreas Lin,Vincent Huang,Kai Han,Boyi Jiang,Wenlin Chen,Chaorong Chen,Ashwin Kumar,Maximilian Balandat,David Eriksson,Yifei Zhang,Pan Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Predictable Scaling (PS) 框架，利用小规模代理模型预测全规模推荐模型性能，在 79-88% 算力削减下仍能正确排序可行假设，最简单策略误差已达重复实验噪声底噪，高斯过程策略再提升 15% 相对 MAE 并提供校准不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐模型的候选改动（假设）评估需全规模训练，耗费数千 GPU 小时，严重限制工程师可探索的假设空间，亟需低成本、高保真的性能预测方法。

Method: PS 通过可组合的缩放函数在不同资源轴构建缩放变体，再用曲线拟合将指标投影到目标规模并给出校准不确定性；对比三种表达力递增的预测策略：原始代理、参数化曲线拟合、高斯过程回归。

Result: 最简单的原始代理策略预测误差已处于重复实验方差的噪声底噪内；最高级的高斯过程策略在相对 MAE 上再提升约 15% 并提供校准不确定性；在任务专用模型和基础模型两类排序模型上，PS 在 79-88% 算力节省下能正确排序多样假设。

Conclusion: Predictable Scaling 通过系统化的小规模代理与外推，以极小算力开销实现了对全规模模型性能的高保真排序，显著拓宽了推荐系统假设探索的可行边界。

Abstract: Evaluating candidate changes (hypotheses) to recommendation models requires full-scale training runs consuming thousands of GPU-hours, severely limiting the hypothesis space engineers can feasibly explore. We introduce Predictable Scaling (PS), a systematic framework that exploits empirically verified regularity conditions to predict full-scale model performance from small-scale proxies. PS constructs scaled-down variants via composable scaling functions along distinct resource axes, and projects metrics to the target scale via curve fitting with calibrated uncertainty estimates. We compare three prediction strategies of increasing expressiveness: even the simplest (raw proxy) achieves prediction error within the empirical noise floor of repeated-run variance; the most expressive (Gaussian Process) provides an indicative 15% relative MAE improvement and calibrated uncertainty. Evaluation on two ranking models of increasing complexity---a task-specific model and a foundation model---demonstrates that PS correctly ranks actionable hypotheses across diverse hypothesis types at 79-88% compute reductions.

</details>


### [210] [A Topological Signature of Learning under Noisy Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23491)
*Zhongtian Sun,Fan Mo,Prayag Tiwari,Kelin Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文利用持久同伦分析神经网络在噪声标签监督下的表示几何变化，发现噪声监督在训练后期留下可测量的几何特征，且该特征在归一化和尺度控制下依然存在，甚至当模型对噪声标签的拟合率极低时也能检测到。平均激活范数在噪声率校准中表现优于原始持久性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练准确率仅反映模型对训练标签的一致性，无法揭示表示空间的几何结构变化。作者旨在探究噪声标签如何影响神经网络学习到的表示几何，以及这种影响是否超越标签拟合程度。

Method: 采用持久同伦追踪神经激活中的环结构随距离阈值的变化。实验涵盖图像分类（CIFAR-10/100, ResNet-18）、文本分类和模运算任务。对比干净标签与噪声标签子集的持久性差异，并引入均匀尺度控制、归一化处理以及五种流水线下的噪声率校准实验。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/ResNet-18上，epoch 10时干净与噪声子集持久性无显著差异，但epoch 50时噪声子集持久性显著更高，且该差异在尺度控制下保持；此时模型对噪声标签的预测匹配率仅7-11%。归一化保留了CIFAR-10上的干净/噪声轨迹交叉现象，但CIFAR-100未观察到交叉。噪声率校准中，平均激活范数的样本内R²（0.936）高于原始持久性（0.908）。

Conclusion: 噪声监督在表示几何上留下可测量的特征，这种特征不依赖于模型对噪声标签的高拟合程度。结合尺度控制下的持久性与标签拟合度量，提供了超越训练准确率的表示响应噪声标签的研究范式。

Abstract: How does noisy supervision change the representations learned by a neural network? Training accuracy measures agreement with training labels but provides little information about representation geometry. We use persistent homology to track loop structure across distance thresholds in neural activations. Experiments span image classification, text classification, and modular arithmetic. On a fixed CIFAR-10/ResNet-18 training probe, equally sized clean-label and corrupted-label subsets show no detectable persistence difference at epoch 10, whereas corrupted-label subsets have higher persistence at epoch 50. The contrast survives uniform-scale controls, even though predictions match the assigned noisy labels for only 7-11% of corrupted probe examples at that checkpoint. Normalisation preserves the crossover between clean and noisy training trajectories on CIFAR-10/ResNet-18, whereas CIFAR-100/ResNet-18 shows no crossover under any of the five evaluated constructions. In a separate noise-rate calibration comparison across five pipelines, mean activation norm achieves a higher mean in-sample R2 than raw persistence (0.936 versus 0.908). These findings show that noisy supervision leaves a measurable geometric signature even when agreement with corrupted labels is low. Comparing persistence under scale controls with label-fitting measurements offers a way to study how representations respond to label corruption beyond what training accuracy reveals.

</details>


### [211] [Uncertainty-aware AI-enabled framework for predicting hydrogen dispersion in confined spaces to enhance operator situational awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23515)
*Niclas Flehmig,Mary Ann Lundteigen,Shen Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 一种两阶段AI框架，用于海事密闭空间氢气加注过程中的泄漏率估算与扩散预测，以增强操作员态势感知并支持预防性安全决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决海事密闭空间压缩氢气加注过程中氢气泄漏的安全隐患，现有手段缺乏早期预警能力，需要在危险浓度达到前部署预防性安全屏障，提升操作员态势感知并辅助决策。

Method: 构建两个独立AI模型组成的系统：第一阶段利用早期传感器读数估算未知泄漏率；第二阶段预测未来时间步氢气云团扩散情况，输出浓度分布图及置信区间。系统设计包含保障机制确保AI在能力边界内运行，作为决策支持工具增强传感器读数，独立的紧急停机系统作为最终安全层。

Result: 在密闭空间氢气释放开放数据集上训练测试：泄漏率估算平均绝对误差0.052 g/s，氢气浓度预测MAE约0.018，接近传感器精度界限±0.01；浓度预测提供良好校准的不确定性估计，使操作员能判断预测可靠性。

Conclusion: 该框架能提供危险浓度积累的早期信息，潜在支持操作员采取预防性行动。AI系统作为决策支持工具而非替代安全系统，配合保障机制和独立紧急停机层，构成分层安全架构。

Abstract: We present a two-stage AI-enabled framework for enhancing operator situational awareness during compressed hydrogen bunkering in maritime confined spaces. In the event of a leak, the framework first estimates the unknown leakage rate from early sensor readings and then forecasts how the hydrogen cloud will disperse over the next time steps, providing operators with a predicted concentration map together with confidence bounds. The framework is based on two independent AI models building one AI system. The AI system is designed as a decision-support tool. It augments sensor readings, and thus enhances operator situational awareness and supports decision-making. This enables preventive safety barriers to be deployed before hazardous concentrations are reached, while an independent emergency shutdown system remains the ultimate safety layer. The system design incorporates safeguards to ensure the AI system operates within its competence envelope. Trained and tested on an open dataset of confined-space hydrogen releases, the framework achieves a mean absolute error of $0.052$~g/s in leakage-rate estimation and $\approx0.018$ in hydrogen-concentration prediction, approaching the sensor precision bound of $\pm0.01$. The hydrogen-concentration prediction provides well-calibrated uncertainty estimates that allow operators to judge the reliability of each forecast. These results suggest that the framework may provide early information about hazardous concentration build-up, potentially supporting preventive actions by operators.

</details>


### [212] [Out of Support, Not Out of Reach: In-Context Learning with Unsupported Instructions and Composed Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23528)
*Xuhan Tong,Yuchen Zeng,Jiawei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个理论框架，分析上下文学习（ICL）在预训练分布之外的泛化能力，区分了目标任务分布偏移和指令分布偏移两种情况，并展示了演示如何帮助模型识别支持内任务、克服不支持的指令偏移，以及通过任务分解处理长程不支持任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论主要将ICL解释为识别预训练中已表示的潜在任务，但尚不清楚当指令或目标任务落在预训练支持之外时，ICL是否仍能成功。需要一种理论框架来分离这两种分布偏移并刻画ICL的泛化边界。

Method: 在温和假设下建立理论框架，将分布偏移分解为目标任务偏移和指令偏移两种形式。对于预训练支持内的任务，分析演示如何识别任务并降低对提示模板的敏感度；对于不支持的指令，在固定模型诱导的参考扩展下，分析演示证据如何主导指令偏移；将框架应用于可分解为预训练支持子任务的长程任务，逐步应用支持任务分析得到累积误差界。

Result: 1) 对于预训练支持内的任务，演示能识别任务并随着数量增加降低对预训练支持提示模板的敏感度；2) 对于不支持的指令，在固定参考扩展下，演示证据可主导N无关的指令偏移，同时保持指数级识别率；3) 对于不在预训练中但可分解为支持子任务的长程任务，逐步应用分析得到标准累积误差界，阐明了沿中间状态轨迹调用熟悉子任务如何避免直接选择不支持的完整任务。

Conclusion: 演示、指令和任务分解三者结合，可以使ICL超越相应的预训练支持范围。理论框架刻画了ICL在分布偏移下成功的条件，为理解ICL的泛化机制提供了统一视角。

Abstract: In-Context Learning (ICL) enables pretrained LLMs to adapt to downstream tasks from a small set of input-output demonstrations, without parameter updates. Yet existing theory primarily explains ICL as identifying a latent task already represented in pretraining, leaving unclear whether ICL can succeed when either the instruction or the target task falls outside the pretraining support. We develop a theoretical framework under mild assumptions that separates these two forms of distribution shift. For target tasks represented in pretraining, we show that demonstrations can identify the task and reduce sensitivity to pretrain-supported prompt templates as their number increases. For an unsupported instruction, we show that under a fixed model-induced reference extension, demonstration evidence can dominate an $N$-independent instruction shift while preserving the exponential identification rate. We then use the same ICL framework as an interpretive lens for long-horizon target tasks that are not represented in pretraining but admit a plan that decomposes them into pretraining-supported subtasks. Applying the supported-task analysis step by step yields a standard accumulated-error bound and clarifies how invoking familiar subtasks along an intermediate-state trajectory can avoid directly selecting the unsupported complete task. Together, our results characterize when demonstrations, instructions, and task decomposition can support ICL beyond the corresponding pretraining supports.

</details>


### [213] [Beyond Expected Values: Risk-Sensitive Planning with Distributional Monte-Carlo Tree Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23565)
*Quynh Trinh,Trung Nguyen,Nam Nguyen,Tuan Dam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出两种分布式蒙特卡洛树搜索算法（CATSO 和 PATSO），通过维护 Q 边的经验分布来优化 CVaR 等尾部风险目标，并证明了收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 MCTS 仅存储标量均值估计，不适合 CVaR 等尾部风险目标；需要分布式表示来捕捉风险敏感行为。

Method: CATSO 使用分类原子和狄利克雷汤普森采样表示 Q 边分布；PATSO 使用自适应粒子表示。两者均基于汤普森采样分布的 CVaR 加多项式乐观奖励选择动作，并通过访问加权平均子节点 CVaR 分数传播标量延续值。

Result: 对于有限深度嵌套 CVaR 规划目标，证明了 n 次模拟后根节点值误差的 O(n^{-1/2}) 界，上界风险敏感单纯遗憾。CATSO 引入固定网格离散化偏差；封顶 PATSO 引入可调 Wasserstein 压缩偏差。实验显示分布式 Q 边改善下尾收益并减少灾难性结果。

Conclusion: 分布式 MCTS 算法有效解决风险敏感规划问题，在均值最优与风险敏感行为不同时表现更优。

Abstract: Monte-Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) typically stores only scalar mean estimates, which makes it poorly suited for tail-risk objectives such as conditional value-at-risk (CVaR). We introduce Categorical Thompson Sampling with Optimism (CATSO) and Particle Thompson Sampling with Optimism (PATSO), two distributional MCTS algorithms that maintain finite-support empirical backup laws at Q-edges. CATSO represents Q-edge laws with categorical atoms and Dirichlet Thompson sampling, while PATSO represents them with adaptive particles. Both algorithms select actions using the CVaR of a Thompson-sampled Q-edge law plus a polynomial optimism bonus, and propagate scalar continuation values through visit-weighted averages of child Q-edge CVaR scores. For the finite-depth nested CVaR planning target, we prove an $O(n^{-1/2})$ bound on the root value error after $n$ rollouts, which upper-bounds the risk-sensitive simple regret. CATSO adds a fixed-grid discretization bias, while capped PATSO adds a tunable Wasserstein compression bias. Experiments on stochastic planning benchmarks show that distributional Q-edges improve lower-tail return and reduce catastrophic outcomes when mean-optimal and risk-sensitive behavior differ.

</details>


### [214] [BMuon: Budgeting Muon's Spectral Geometry for Structured Compressibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23571)
*Rida Assalouh,Tom Jacobs,Rebekka Burkholz,Yani Ioannou,Umut Şimşekli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Budget Muon (BMuon)，通过修改 Muon 的谱几何，引入全局通道分配预算，使隐藏通道在块间竞争资源，从而协调优化性能与结构化可压缩性。实验显示 BMuon 在 ViT-S/16 和 1B LLaMA 模型上均优于 Muon，且学习到的分配比权重幅度更有效的剪枝准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon 虽然是强优化器（可解释为谱范数几何下的最速下降），但训练的 Transformer 在剪枝 MLP 隐藏通道后性能急剧下降，缺乏结构化可压缩性。需在保持优化性能的同时实现结构化稀疏。

Method: BMuon 修改 Muon 的谱几何，引入共享的全局通道分配预算，使各块的隐藏通道竞争预算。其精确预言器是在一族插值 Muon 几何与结构化稀疏几何的范数下的归一化最速下降。推导了 Minorization-Maximization 代理算法求解分配问题。

Result: 理论上证明：线性分解模型中，Muon 允许冗余宽度的最小成本精确表示；BMuon 在预算稀缺时，所有最小成本表示均为宽度最小。实验上：ImageNet-1K ViT-S/16 渐进式结构化 MLP 剪枝下 BMuon 优于 Muon，分配比权重幅度更好；1B LLaMA 模型后训练剪枝同样优于 Muon。

Conclusion: BMuon 通过全局通道预算将优化几何与结构化稀疏统一，提供可解释的稀疏控制，在理论和实践上均显著提升 Transformer 的结构化可压缩性，同时保持优化性能。

Abstract: Muon has emerged as a strong optimizer for neural network training and admits an interpretation as steepest descent under a spectral-norm geometry. Yet we find that Muon-trained Transformers can degrade sharply when their MLP hidden channels are pruned after training. To reconcile Muon's optimization performance with structured compressibility, we introduce Budget Muon (BMuon), which modifies Muon's spectral geometry so that hidden channels across blocks compete for a shared allocation budget. The resulting oracle is exactly normalized steepest descent under a family of norms that interpolate between Muon's geometry and structured sparse geometries. Our principal sparsity control is an interpretable global channel-allocation budget, rather than a setting-dependent sparsity-penalty coefficient. For linear factorization models, we show that Muon admits minimum-cost exact representations with redundant hidden width, whereas under the BMuon oracle geometry and a sufficiently scarce budget, every minimum-cost representation is width-minimal. For practical training, we derive a minorization-maximization surrogate for the exact allocation problem. On ImageNet-1K with ViT-S/16, BMuon improves over Muon under gradual structured MLP pruning, and its learned allocations provide a more effective pruning criterion than weight magnitude. On a 1B-parameter LLaMA-style model trained on FineWeb, BMuon improves over Muon under post-training MLP channel pruning.

</details>


### [215] [Boundary Detection for High-Dimensional Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23590)
*Abigail Hickok,Valerio Galanti,Osama Al Dalahmah,Andrew J. Blumberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出两种针对高维点云数据边界检测的新方法，适用于非均匀密度、高环境维度和大量噪声的情况。第一种方法在局部降维后拟合分离超平面，第二种方法测量局部邻域的方向均匀性。两种方法均能输出边界点的法向量。在合成数据和真实数据（空间转录组学肿瘤边界检测、LLM激活几何）上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有边界检测方法在处理高维、非均匀密度、噪声大的点云数据时存在局限性，需要能够自适应处理这些挑战的新方法。

Method: 方法一：局部降维后拟合分离超平面，依赖内在维度估计，适用于可靠估计内在维度的情况。方法二：测量局部邻域的方向均匀性，无需维度估计，在合成数据上整体性能最优。两者均输出点级边界分数和法向量估计。

Result: 在合成数据上验证了两种方法的有效性，第二种方法整体性能最优。在空间转录组学数据上成功检测肿瘤边界，在LLM激活几何研究中也展示了适用性。

Conclusion: 提出的两种边界检测方法能有效处理高维、非均匀密度、噪声大的点云数据，提供边界分数和法向量，在实际应用中表现良好，为高维数据边界分析提供了新工具。}

Abstract: We introduce two new methods for boundary detection in high-dimensional point cloud data, intended to work in situations with non-uniform density, high ambient dimension, and substantial noise. Our first method fits a separating hyperplane on local neighborhoods after doing local dimension reduction---this method works best when one can reliably estimate the intrinsic dimension of the data. Our second method measures the directional uniformity of local neighborhoods, and has the best overall performance on synthetic data. In addition to pointwise numerical boundary scores, our algorithms yield an estimated normal vector at each boundary point. We validate our methodology on synthetic data as well as two real examples: we analyze spatial transcriptomics data, where we show that we can detect the boundary of a tumor, and we also study LLM activation geometry.

</details>


### [216] [When Magnitude Suffices: Reconstructing Time-Domain-Gated Microwave Signals Without Phase on a Thoracic Phantom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23608)
*Maciej Cichoń,Patrycja Gołaś,Maksymilian Bartosik,Angelika Walczak,Agata Bielecka-Dąbrowa,Maciej Ślot*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文展示如何仅使用频谱仪的幅度扫描数据，通过神经网络重建矢量网络分析仪（VNA）的时域门控幅度响应，实现无需相位测量的标量仪器门控测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时域门控需要VNA测量复数S参数，成本高且复杂。研究旨在利用廉价标量仪器（频谱仪+跟踪源）仅测量幅度，通过学习幅度与相位的依赖关系重建门控响应，降低测量系统成本和复杂度。

Method: 在单一校准配置下，选择门控中心以分离测量状态，对每个仪器进行一次每频点减去参考态的校正，使用全连接神经网络在VNA和频谱仪的扫描数据上训练，测试对象为具有十一介电态的躯干模体，每个态轮流留出验证。

Result: 在S11上，从频谱仪输入重建的形状误差为0.08 dB²，状态排序相关性0.81，与VNA输入重建相当；在S21上形状误差0.82 dB²并保持状态有序分组。显式相位预测效果差，因门控需要约0.2 rad RMS相位精度。跨十一配置显示反射模式下相位强依赖幅度，透射模式下弱依赖，解释S11/S21差异。在UM-BMID乳腺数据集上，网络形状误差比逐频点线性映射低30%。

Conclusion: 当幅度携带足够相位信息时（如反射测量），标量仪器经一次校准和神经网络训练后可直接给出门控响应，无需后续相位测量，为低成本微波成像提供可行路径。

Abstract: Time-domain gating separates the arrivals that form a microwave S-parameter measurement. It requires the measurement of complex values, so this application almost always uses a vector network analyzer (VNA). We show that, on one calibrated measurement configuration, the gated magnitude the VNA would give can be reconstructed from a magnitude-only sweep of a spectrum analyzer with a tracking generator (SA). We select the gate center for separability of the measured states, correct each instrument once by a per-bin subtraction of a reference state, and train a fully connected network on sweeps from both instruments. The test object is a torso phantom with lung-shaped compartments whose filling takes eleven dielectric states, each held out in turn. On $S_{11}$ the reconstruction from SA input reaches a shape error of 0.08 dB$^2$ with a state ordering of 0.81. This matches the reconstruction from VNA input. On $S_{21}$ it reaches 0.82 dB$^2$ and keeps the ordered groups of the states. An explicit phase prediction performs much worse, because gating needs the phase to about 0.2 rad RMS. Across eleven measurement configurations the dependence of phase on magnitude is strong for reflection and weak for transmission. This explains the difference between $S_{11}$ and $S_{21}$. Where the magnitude carries enough of the phase, a scalar instrument gives the gated response without any phase measurement after installation. On the open UM-BMID breast data set, with whole phantoms held out, the shape error of the network is 30 percent lower than that of a per-bin linear map.

</details>


### [217] [How Many, but How Sure? Class Competition as a Confidence Proxy in Multi-Class Density-Based Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23611)
*Villanelle O'Reilly,Georgios Leontidis,Marc Hanheide*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种无需训练的置信度代理指标——显著性测度，基于类别间的局部竞争来评估多类别密度估计器中密度质量分配的可靠性，并在推理时衰减有争议的密度，从而在Hicks等人数据集上将DSACA预测的总体MAE从0.920降至0.846。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多类别密度估计器虽能计数拥挤场景中的目标，但其类别输出无法指示密度质量分配的可靠性，缺乏对跨类别泄漏的诊断手段。

Method: 基于预测密度图中最强类别通道间的局部竞争，构建无需重训练、无需访问模型内部的显著性测度；在推理阶段衰减竞争激烈区域的密度，以此检验残余竞争是否能诊断跨类别泄漏。

Result: 在Hicks等人数据集上的DSACA预测中，该方法将总体MAE从0.920降低至0.846，表明类别竞争包含关于计数可靠性的有用信息。

Conclusion: 类别间的竞争信息可作为免训练的置信度代理，通过抑制有争议的密度分配能有效提升多类别拥挤计数的准确性。

Abstract: Multi-class density estimators can count objects in crowded scenes, but their class-wise outputs offer no indication of how reliably density mass has been allocated. We introduce a training-free confidence proxy based on local competition between the strongest class channels. The resulting prominence measure requires only the predicted density maps, with no retraining or access to model internals. We test whether residual competition is diagnostic of cross-class leakage by attenuating contested density at inference time. On DSACA predictions for the Hicks et al. dataset, this reduces overall MAE from 0.920 to 0.846, suggesting that class competition contains useful information about count reliability.

</details>


### [218] [The Sign Code: The Hidden Binary Nature of Deep Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23616)
*Niclas Göring,Shuofeng Zhang,Roi Holtzman,Yoonsoo Nam,Ard A. Louis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了过参数化视觉分类器中神经元符号（sign）与幅度（magnitude）对分类性能的贡献，发现符号编码（sign code）是决定性因素：仅使用二进制符号编码的 k-NN 分类器即可达到与完整模型相当的精度；将所有层的幅度替换为随机噪声几乎不影响 ResNet 性能，而翻转仅 20% 的符号就会使准确率降至随机水平。泛化能力取决于同类样本是否共享相似的符号编码，边际分解显示符号对 logit 的贡献随宽度按 √d 增长，主导幅度残差。这表明这些网络的自然分析单元可能是离散的符号编码，而非连续的权重空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在过参数化视觉分类器中，神经元输出的符号与幅度哪一个对分类决策起主导作用，以及符号编码如何影响模型的泛化能力。

Method: 1. 将神经元输出分解为符号（正/负）和幅度（绝对值），定义全体神经元符号集合为符号编码。2. 使用仅基于二进制符号编码的 k-最近邻分类器评估分类性能。3. 在保持原始符号不变的前提下，用随机噪声替换所有层的幅度，观察对 ResNet 性能的影响。4. 翻转部分符号（如 20%）测试鲁棒性。5. 引入正则化器破坏同类样本符号编码的重叠，考察训练与测试准确率的变化。6. 通过边际分解分析符号与幅度对 logit 的贡献随网络宽度的缩放规律。

Result: 1. 仅使用符号编码的 k-NN 分类器在多种架构、激活函数和数据集上均能达到与完整模型相当的准确率。2. 将所有层幅度替换为随机噪声且保留原始符号，ResNet 性能几乎不变。3. 翻转仅 20% 的符号即可使准确率降至随机水平。4. 同类样本共享相似符号编码时模型泛化良好；正则化破坏这种重叠会导致训练准确率保持高位但测试性能崩溃。5. 边际分解表明符号对每个 logit 的贡献随网络宽度按 √d 增长，主导幅度残差。

Conclusion: 对于具有符号保持激活函数的过参数化视觉分类器，分类决策主要依赖于离散的符号编码而非连续的幅度。符号编码是这些网络的自然分析单元，其主导地位随网络宽度增长而增强。这挑战了基于连续权重空间的传统分析范式，提示未来研究应关注离散符号动态。

Abstract: Every neuron in a trained network outputs a number that can be separated into its sign (positive or negative) and its magnitude (absolute size). We call the set of all neuron signs the \textit{sign code}. This raises a key question: does classification depend more on the sign code or on the magnitudes? For overparameterized vision classifiers with sign-preserving activations, the answer is overwhelmingly the sign code. A $k$-nearest neighbor classifier operating only on this binary sign code achieves accuracy comparable to the full model across different architectures, activation functions, and datasets. Moreover, replacing all magnitudes in every layer with random noise while keeping the original signs barely changes residual network performance. In contrast, flipping just 20\% of the signs reduces accuracy to chance. Models generalize well when same-class inputs share similar sign codes. When this overlap is disrupted by a regularizer, training accuracy remains high, but test performance collapses. A margin decomposition helps explain these results: the sign-only contribution to each logit grows as $\sqrt{d}$ in network width, dominating the magnitude residual. The natural unit of analysis for these networks may not be through their continuous weight space but instead through their discrete sign codes.

</details>


### [219] [ThetreStageAI: Multi-View Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Structured Bengali Theatre Production Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23618)
*Md. Mehedi Hasan Naeem,Mst. Kamrunnahar Ruma,Shakila Sultana,Nafiza Anjum,Nasreen Jannatun Nemu,Md. Sujan Ali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出TheatreStageAI，一个用于结构化孟加拉语戏剧制作规划的检索增强框架，采用多视角检索（场景、演员调度、灯光）并结合Gemini生成受模式约束的JSON输出，构建了合成数据集并进行了对比评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 戏剧制作需要协调对话、演员动作、舞台指示、灯光焦点、提示时序和设备约束，现有方法可能缺乏结构化、多视角的检索与生成能力，尤其针对孟加拉语资源稀缺。

Method: 构建三个视角的独立检索索引（场景、演员调度、灯光），使用BGE-M3嵌入孟加拉语文本并存入Qdrant集合；检索结果与制作简报结合输入Gemini，生成包含角色、场景、对话、舞台指示、调度、灯光、音效提示的schema-constrained JSON；应用结构和语义检查，最多一次受约束修复；构建人工指导、LLM辅助的合成数据集（500条记录扩展为1500个检索文档）。

Result: 数据集诊断显示标识符一致、灯光字段有效，但存在大量对话重复；配对摄像机就绪评估对比了单一合并检索与完整多视角检索，验证了多视角方法的优势。

Conclusion: TheatreStageAI通过多视角检索增强实现了结构化孟加拉语戏剧制作规划，公开了代码、数据集和评估材料，为低资源语言戏剧制作提供了可复现的基准。

Abstract: Staging a theatre performance requires coordinating dialogue, actor movement, stage directions, lighting focus, cue timing, and equipment constraints within the same production plan. This work presents ThetreStageAI, a retrieval augmented framework for structured Bengali theatre production planning. Rather than retrieving from one mixed corpus, the system maintains separate scene, actor-blocking, and lighting views. Bengali text is embedded with BGE-M3 and indexed in view-specific Qdrant collections. Retrieved references are combined with a production brief and passed to Gemini, which generates schema-constrained JSON containing characters, scenes, dialogue, stage directions, blocking, lighting, and optional sound cues. Structural and semantic checks are applied before acceptance, with at most one constrained repair attempt. We also construct a human-guided, LLM-assisted synthetic dataset of 500 short Bengali theatre records that expands to 1,500 retrieval documents. Dataset diagnostics confirm consistent identifiers and valid lighting fields while also revealing substantial dialogue repetition. A paired camera-ready evaluation compares single-combined and full multi-view retrieval under matched settings. The source code, dataset, and evaluation materials are publicly available.

</details>


### [220] [GRAFT-EEG: Gauge-Aware Physical Field Transport for Cross-Dataset Motor-Imagery EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23620)
*Ying Zhou,Mengting Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种跨数据集EEG解码方法，在无目标统计信息的情况下，通过移除主动集公共参考基准并利用模板运动导场传输原始掩码，结合源拟合频谱分类器和有限状态空间模型残差，实现了显著优于基线的二分类运动想象解码性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨数据集EEG解码通常将导联布局、参考电极和有源传感器变化视为表征偏移，但这些变化也改变了观测算子。现有方法依赖目标域统计信息，限制了零样本迁移能力。因此需要研究目标统计盲迁移，仅利用电极坐标实例化固定预测算子。

Method: 方法移除主动集公共参考基准，将各原始掩码通过模板运动导场传输；采用源拟合频谱分类器作为主要决策锚点，有限状态空间模型（SSM）残差作为次要补充；建立受限可识别性和算子扰动界。

Result: 在五个留一数据集实验（253名被试）中，动态方法达60.865%平衡准确率，优于BIOT基线52.684%（差异+8.182，95%CI +6.382至+10.196，所有目标均为正）。四个EEGPT-clean目标上描述性均值为61.520%对59.057%。四个事后同源测试中，FULL分别比坐标基、匹配频谱、行打乱、错误解剖控制高出9.959、9.698、11.241、3.985点（所有95%CI排除零，Holm校正P<0.001）。独立60被试同范式评估保留物理算子对比+4.029点（+2.338至+5.773），动态-静态对比无结论；SSM贡献+1.059点（+0.013至+1.892）。

Conclusion: 该方法在五个二分类运动想象领域及测试算子下实现了显著的零样本跨数据集解码提升，但结论仅限于这些特定条件，预注册实验仅提供部分确证而非无限制外部验证。

Abstract: Cross-dataset EEG decoding often treats montage, reference, and active-sensor changes as representation shift, although they also alter the observation operator. We study target-statistics-blind transfer: fitting receives no target signal, label, or empirical statistic, and electrode coordinates only instantiate a fixed prediction operator. method removes the active-set common-reference gauge and transports each native mask through a template motor leadfield. A source-fitted spectral classifier anchors the decision; a bounded state-space-model (SSM) residual is secondary. We establish restricted identifiability and an operator-perturbation bound. Across five leave-one-dataset-out targets (253 participants), dynamic method achieved 60.865% equal-target balanced accuracy versus 52.684% for strict-clean 5/5 BIOT (hierarchical difference +8.182 points; 95% CI +6.382 to +10.196; positive in every target). On four EEGPT-clean targets, the descriptive means were 61.520% and 59.057%. In four post-hoc exact same-parent tests, FULL exceeded coordinate-basis, matched-spectrum, row-shuffled, and wrong-anatomy controls by 9.959, 9.698, 11.241, and 3.985 points, respectively (all 95% CIs excluded zero; Holm-adjusted P<0.001). A separate 60-participant same-paradigm evaluation retained a positive physical-operator contrast (+4.029 points; +2.338 to +5.773), whereas dynamic--static was inconclusive; its preregistered status is partial confirmation rather than unrestricted external validation. The SSM added +1.059 points (+0.013 to +1.892). Claims remain bounded to five binary motor-imagery domains and the tested operators.

</details>


### [221] [Deconstructing Multi-Task Active Learning: The Paradox of Gradient Conflict and Orthogonal Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23623)
*Juho Jung,Seongjae Kang,Jinyoung Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多任务主动学习（MTL-AL）新框架，摒弃传统的线性标量化方法，通过三个正交维度重新设计获取函数：1）冲突悖论——不在获取函数中评分梯度冲突，而是通过训练时的梯度手术解决；2）正交分解——将获取分为信息量与探索两阶段，先筛选信息量样本再最大化覆盖，避免多样性稀释；3）跨任务互信息（CTMI）——基于MC-dropout任务熵的协方差统计量捕捉跨任务依赖。在COCO、Cityscapes、NYUv2上实验显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MTL-AL方法主要依赖不确定性、多样性、梯度冲突等异构目标的线性标量化，但线性组合无法捕捉冲突目标下的帕累托最优权衡，导致获取策略次优。作者认为需从组合转向解构，结构性地重新思考获取函数设计。

Method: 提出三大创新：1）冲突悖论：识别出在候选排序中惩罚梯度冲突反而有害，于是将冲突从获取函数中解耦，改由训练阶段的梯度手术（如PCGrad）处理干扰。2）正交分解：将获取分解为信息量（不确定性）和探索（多样性）两个正交阶段，先按信息量过滤候选，再在信息量高的子集中最大化覆盖，防止无信息离群点稀释多样性。3）跨任务互信息（CTMI）：利用MC-dropout估计各任务预测熵，计算跨任务熵的协方差矩阵，作为跨任务依赖的统计量，指导信息量评分。

Result: 在COCO（目标检测+分割）、Cityscapes（语义分割+深度估计）、NYUv2（语义分割+深度估计+法线预测）三个基准上，对比最新MTL-AL基线（如MTL-AL、CIMG、GradMatch）和数据选择基线（如CoreSet、BADGE），本方法在各标注预算下均取得一致性能提升，验证了结构性重新设计优于增量式启发式改进。

Conclusion: 通过将多任务主动学习的获取函数从线性标量化转向正交解构，分离冲突处理、信息量与探索、跨任务依赖建模，可显著提升标注效率。这表明获取策略的结构性重思考比启发式微调能带来更大收益，为MTL-AL提供了新的原则性范式。

Abstract: Existing multi-task active learning (MTL-AL) methods predominantly rely on linear scalarization of heterogeneous objectives such as uncertainty, diversity, and gradient conflict. We argue that this entangled formulation is fundamentally limited, as linear combination fails to capture Pareto-optimal trade-offs under conflicting objectives. We propose a shift from combination to deconstruction and introduce a principled framework that rethinks MTL-AL along three orthogonal dimensions. First, we identify the Conflict Paradox: although the optimization literature treats gradient conflict as interference to be suppressed, penalizing conflict when ranking candidates is measurably worse than not scoring it at all. We therefore decouple the two concerns: the acquisition function does not score conflict, and the resulting interference is resolved by gradient surgery during training. Second, we introduce Orthogonal Decomposition, separating acquisition into Informativeness and Exploration. This two-stage process, informativeness filtering followed by coverage maximization, prevents "diversity dilution" by excluding uninformative outliers before enforcing diversity. Third, we propose Cross-Task Mutual Information (CTMI), a covariance-based statistic of cross-task dependency computed from MC-dropout task entropies. Extensive experiments on COCO, Cityscapes, and NYUv2, including comparisons against recent MTL-AL and data-selection baselines, demonstrate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art methods, highlighting that structural rethinking of acquisition yields larger gains than incremental heuristic design. Code is available at https://github.com/Juho-Jung/multitask_active_learning_official.

</details>


### [222] [Deep LLM layers expose passage-level context in EEG during silent reading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23638)
*Ingo Marquardt,Anthilia Alchanat,Priyanka Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探究了EEG可解码结构在大语言模型（Llama-3.1-8B）各层中的分布，发现词级可解码性在早期层峰值后随深度下降，而语境追踪成分随深度上升；单个EEG窗口即携带篇章级信息，词身份定位于早期层，话语状态定位于深层，目标层选择成为EEG语言解码器的设计旋钮。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作仅对比了语言模型的两层（无上下文输入嵌入层和一中间层），尚不清楚EEG可解码结构在模型全深度上的分布情况，特别是词级信息与语境追踪信息如何随层深变化。

Method: 使用Llama-3.1-8B的33层，分别训练每层的解码器，包含带因果Transformer和不带因果Transformer两种设置，将短EEG窗口与各层隐藏状态嵌入对齐；引入目标几何控制（合成预测器：真实目标加噪声，噪声校准至各解码器词级准确率）和跨运行/跨会话预测置换控制，剖析语境追踪成分来源。

Result: 词级可解码性在早期层达到峰值并随层深稳步下降；语境追踪成分的检索增益随层深上升，无论是否使用因果Transformer；单个0.5秒EEG窗口即可追踪文本篇章；目标几何控制表明语境追踪非词级解码与目标几何的副产品；置换控制显示语境追踪主要跟随文本而非EEG记录会话特征。

Conclusion: 单个EEG窗口包含篇章级信息，可被语境目标吸收；词身份定位于语言模型早期层，话语状态定位于深层；目标层的选择成为EEG语言解码器的重要设计参数。

Abstract: In a previous report we showed that a decoder can recover open-vocabulary word-level information from 19-channel EEG recorded during silent reading, by aligning short EEG windows with hidden-state embeddings of the presented word taken from a large language model (LLM). That analysis contrasted two of the model's 33 layers: the context-free input embedding and one mid-depth layer. Here we ask how EEG-decodable structure is distributed over the full depth of the language model. We trained one decoder per layer of Llama-3.1-8B, with and without a causal transformer over the sequence of EEG windows. We distinguish word-level decodability from a context-tracking component. Word-level decodability peaks in early layers and declines steadily towards the last layer. In contrast, the context-tracking component of the retrieval gain rises with depth, both with and without a causal transformer. Unexpectedly, a decoder that sees a single 0.5-s window at a time tracks the text passage too. Two new controls dissect this component. A target-geometry control, a synthetic predictor made of the true target plus noise calibrated to each decoder's word-level accuracy, shows that context tracking is not a by-product of word decoding and target geometry. A control that permutes predictions across runs from either the same or from different sessions shows that most of the observed context-tracking is not caused by an EEG recording session signature. Instead, context tracking mostly follows the text. A single EEG window therefore carries passage-level information that a contextual target can absorb. The results locate word identity in the early layers of the language model, discourse state in the deeper layers, and make the choice of target layer a design dial for EEG language decoders.

</details>


### [223] [No-Regret Language Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23641)
*Jiaxun Li,Vinod Raman,Ambuj Tewari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出无悔生成框架，引入基于树的错误强迫维度精确刻画有限时域极大极小复杂度，建立可实现和不可知设定下的必要充分条件，并揭示生成速率的丰富谱系；同时将生成复杂度与在线预测联系起来，给出最优的错误界和遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决生成器在面对演示序列和目标语言时如何实现无悔学习，即在最坏情况下错误数次线性增长；扩展到不可知设定允许任意演示，统一表征生成复杂度。

Method: 引入树基错误强迫维度来刻画有限时域极大极小复杂度，建立无悔生成的充要条件；利用Littlestone维度连接在线预测复杂度，推导可实现和不可知设定下的错误界和遗憾界。

Result: 证明每个可接受的非递减生成速率均可在常数缩放下达到；对Littlestone维度为d的语言类，可实现错误界为O(d log(1+T/d))，不可知遗憾界为O(√(dT log(1+T/d)))，前者在常数因子下最优。

Conclusion: 无悔生成框架通过树基维度精确刻画了生成复杂度，揭示了生成速率的完全谱系，并与在线预测理论建立了紧密联系，为生成任务的理论分析提供了统一工具。

Abstract: We introduce no-regret generation, which requires a generator to make a sublinear number of mistakes uniformly over all demonstration sequences and compatible target languages. We also formulate an agnostic variant that allows arbitrary demonstrations that may not be compatible with any languages. For both settings, we introduce tree-based mistake-forcing dimensions that exactly characterize finite-horizon minimax complexity and establish necessary and sufficient conditions for no-regret generation. Our characterizations reveal a rich spectrum of generation rates: up to constant rescaling, every admissible nondecreasing profile is attainable in both the realizable and agnostic settings. Finally, we connect generation complexity to online prediction: for language classes of finite Littlestone dimension \(d\), we obtain realizable mistake bounds of \(O(d\log(1+T/d))\) and agnostic regret bounds of \(O(\sqrt{dT\log(1+T/d)})\). The realizable bound is optimal up to universal constants.

</details>


### [224] [JAXtari: High-Throughput and Easy-to-Modify Arcade Learning Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23650)
*Quentin Delfosse,Raban Emunds,Paul Seitz,Sebastian Wette,Jannis Blüml,Daniel Kirn,Dominik Mandok,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: JAXtari 是一个开源的、高性能的 Atari 2600 环境重新实现，利用大规模 GPU 并行化，显著加速训练（PPO 在单张 A100 上 1 亿步不到一小时），并提供面向对象的观测和灵活的环境修改系统，支持神经符号方法和快速实验迭代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Arcade Learning Environment (ALE) 缺乏原生的对象提取和环境修改支持，限制了对智能体是否学习鲁棒因果模型还是依赖脆弱视觉启发式的研究；同时 ALE 依赖 CPU 仿真，成为现代 GPU 加速训练流水线的瓶颈，制约实验规模和迭代频率。

Method: 重新实现 Atari 2600 环境，利用 JAX 实现大规模 GPU 并行化；提供面向对象的观测表示和灵活的环境修改系统，允许研究人员进行神经符号方法开发、发现新的捷径并快速训练和适配智能体。

Result: PPO 智能体在单张 A100 GPU 上完成 1 亿环境步训练耗时不到一小时；环境修改和快速实验迭代可在几分钟内完成，显著提升了实验规模和迭代速度。

Conclusion: JAXtari 解决了 ALE 的性能瓶颈和功能局限，为深度强化学习研究提供了高性能、可扩展的基准环境，推动因果模型学习、神经符号方法及快速原型开发的研究。

Abstract: The Arcade Learning Environment (ALE) has been a cornerstone of Deep Reinforcement Learning research, providing a standardized benchmark for evaluating general agents. However, its lack of native support for object-extraction and environment modification restricts research into evaluating whether agents learn robust causal models or rely on brittle visual heuristics. Furthermore, the ALE's dependence on CPU-based emulation creates a significant bottleneck in modern GPU-accelerated training pipelines, limiting both experimental scale and iteration frequency. In this work, we introduce JAXtari, an open-source, high-performance reimplementation of Atari 2600 environments. By leveraging massive GPU parallelization, JAXtari allows PPO agents to finish training on 100M environment steps on a single A100-GPU in under an hour. Beyond speed, it provides object-centric observations and a flexible modification system, allowing researchers to, e.g. develop neuro-symbolic methods or discover novel shortcuts and subsequently train and adapt their agents rapidly, sometimes in only minutes.

</details>


### [225] [TriRouter: Sparse-to-Dense Electrode-Space Routing for Robust EEG Motor Imagery Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23665)
*Ying Zhou,Yaoxin,Mengting Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出TriRouter框架，解决运动想象脑机接口中多通道EEG电极缺失或损坏的问题，通过三路由路径和验证校准路由器提升解码鲁棒性，在多种缺失率下显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动想象脑机接口需要从多通道EEG可靠解码，但实际采集中常出现稀疏或损坏的电极布局，导致解码性能下降，需要一种能处理电极缺失的鲁棒框架。

Method: 提出TriRouter电极空间路由框架，包含判别保持路径、掩码鲁棒路径和潜在补偿路径三条并行路径。验证校准路由器根据可观测通道可用性动态分配路径贡献，在保持完整通道判别能力的同时，在严重缺失条件下增强表征恢复。

Result: 在BNCI2014-001数据集上，TriRouter优于标准公开主干网络和动态空间滤波损坏EEG基线。在25%、50%、75%缺失设置下，准确率分别比最强损坏EEG基线提升3.68、4.02、6.46个百分点。在结构化区域缺失和低通道数据集评估中仍保持有效性。

Conclusion: TriRouter通过多路径路由和自适应分配机制，有效解决了EEG电极缺失/损坏问题，显著提升了运动想象BCI在实际噪声环境下的解码鲁棒性和准确性，为实际应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Motor imagery brain-computer interfaces require reliable decoding from multichannel EEG, but practical acquisition often yields sparse or corrupted electrode montages. We propose TriRouter, an electrode-space routing framework with discriminative-preservation, mask-robustness, and latent-compensation paths. A validation-calibrated router uses observable channel availability to allocate path contributions, preserving complete-channel discrimination while increasing representation recovery under severe missingness. In BNCI2014-001, TriRouter outperforms standard public backbones and a dynamic-spatial-filtering corrupted-EEG baseline. It improves accuracy over the strongest corrupted-EEG baseline by 3.68, 4.02, and 6.46 percentage points at nominal 25%, 50%, and 75% missing settings, and remains effective under structured regional missingness and additional low-channel dataset evaluation.

</details>


### [226] [Equivalence testing and run-count design for stochastic machine-learning experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23700)
*Dmytro Knopov,Nadiya Shvai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种严格的统计协议，用于基于单次运行指标（如AUROC）认证两种训练方法的实际等价性。该协议采用两层（种子×项目）方差模型、TOST等价性检验、两层bootstrap置信区间，并提供了将等价边界和目标功效转化为所需运行次数的闭式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中声称两种训练方法性能相当的结论，往往仅基于每种方法2-3次运行之间的非显著差异，缺乏严格的统计功效保证。本文旨在解决这一问题，建立一套具有理论保证的等价性认证框架。

Method: 1. 两层（种子×项目）方差模型，分离运行间离散度与项目层面噪声；2. 基于预设边界的TOST（双单侧检验）等价性检验，等价于置信区间落在边界内；3. 两层bootstrap处理复合统计量；4. 闭式公式将边界和目标功效转化为每种方法所需运行次数，并提供shifted-t修正；5. 两阶段设计（先导实验+公式），利用先导方差的上置信界减少欠功效。

Result: 在270格模拟网格和非高斯种子分布下，公式在设计相关范围内（≥5次运行）得到验证。案例研究1：某联合能量模型4次已发表运行无法认证等价性，主要是设计问题——按该方差校准，每组2次运行认证概率仅约3%，而以80%功效认证0.01 AUROC需12次运行。案例研究2：千种子CIFAR-10动物园和MultiBERTs验证公式对真实种子分布的准确性（误差<0.05），并解决了2-3次运行无法判定的比较。两阶段设计中，使用先导方差的上置信界显著减少了点估计导致的欠功效。

Conclusion: 该协议为机器学习实验中的等价性认证提供了理论严谨且实用的框架，包含经验证的功效公式和bootstrap方法，适用于现实的运行次数（≥5）和非高斯分布，填补了当前实践中仅凭少量运行声称等价的统计空白。

Abstract: Claims that two training methods perform equally well often rest on a non-significant difference between two or three runs per method. We develop a protocol, with its power theory, for certifying practical equivalence in per-run metrics such as the AUROC (area under the receiver operating characteristic curve). It rests on a two-level (seed x item) variance model separating between-run dispersion from item-level noise. Equivalence within a pre-specified margin is tested by two one-sided tests (TOST), equivalently by a confidence interval inside the margin; a two-level bootstrap covers composite statistics. A closed-form formula converts a margin and a target power into runs per method; its shifted-t refinement is calibrated against the realised two-level process. Simulations on a 270-cell grid and under non-Gaussian seed laws confirm the formula within its design-relevant range, from five runs per method. For four published runs of a joint energy-based model, the failure to certify equivalence was largely decided by design: with the variance calibrated to those runs, two per method certify with probability near three per cent, whereas certifying 0.01 AUROC at 80 per cent power needs twelve even under exact equality. A second case study on a thousand-seed CIFAR-10 zoo and the MultiBERTs checks the formula against real seed laws (within 0.05 in that range) and resolves comparisons that two or three runs leave undecided. In a two-stage design (pilot, then formula), our experiments suggest that an upper confidence bound on the pilot variance substantially reduces the undersizing seen with its point estimate.

</details>


### [227] [Blitz: Accelerating the Diffusion Modules of AlphaFold3 and Boltz-2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23703)
*Vinay Saji Mathew,Soundar Kumara,Gretta Kellogg,William K. M. Lai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Blitz是一个少步蒸馏框架，将扩散蛋白结构预测模型从200步降至8步，同时保持教师级精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型预测蛋白结构需要约200步去噪，计算成本高，推动了少步采样器的发展，但现有方法基准不一，需统一评估。

Method: 使用几何噪声调度均匀分布步长，通过多步一致性训练学习更新；推理时采用物理引导和无目标候选选择减少几何缺陷；应用于AlphaFold 3和Boltz-2。

Result: 将去噪步数从200降至8；在统一评估协议下，Blitz保持教师级结构精度，AF3-Blitz超越所有少步基线并在多项指标上超过教师模型。

Conclusion: Blitz实现了高效的少步蛋白结构预测，显著降低计算成本同时不损失精度，甚至超越教师模型。

Abstract: Diffusion-based protein structure prediction models produce all-atom structures in about 200 denoising steps, and that cost has driven a number of few-step samplers. We present Blitz, a few-step distillation framework that uses a geometric noise schedule to distribute steps more evenly across noise scales, then learns those updates through multistep consistency training. Physical steering and target-free candidate selection help reduce the remaining geometric defects at inference. We apply Blitz to AlphaFold 3 and Boltz-2, reducing denoising from 200 steps to eight. Because published few-step results use different benchmarks and scoring procedures, we compare both distilled models with their teachers and other few-step methods under a common evaluation protocol covering structural accuracy, interface quality, ligand placement, and stereochemical validity. Blitz retains teacher-level structural accuracy, with AF3-Blitz outperforming all evaluated few-step baselines and exceeding its teacher on several accuracy metrics.

</details>


### [228] [Neural Expansion: A Unified Mechanism for How Deep Neural Networks Generalize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23716)
*Chashi Mahiul Islam,Samuel Jacob Chacko,Mao Nishino,Canlin Zhang,Xiuwen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了“神经扩展”这一统一几何框架，通过输入空间中方向选择性射线覆盖的扩展来解释深度神经网络的泛化机制，并引入泛化区间量化方向特定的预测稳定区域，实证表明训练过程中沿低雅可比奇异值方向的射线覆盖逐步扩展，能解释大量正确分类、对抗样本及分布外样本的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于边界、PAC-贝叶斯、神经切线核等全局理论虽然提供了泛化的宏观解释，但缺乏对训练动力学如何产生泛化的机制性洞察，且难以解释过参数化网络为何仍能良好泛化。

Method: 提出神经扩展框架，利用泛化区间刻画训练样本周围预测保持稳定的方向特定区域；在多种架构和数据集上进行大量实证研究，分析训练过程中射线覆盖的扩展动态，特别是沿雅可比矩阵低奇异值方向的扩展。

Result: 训练导致射线覆盖沿低雅可比奇异值方向逐步扩展，该机制能解释大部分正确分类的测试输入、对抗样本、错误标签及分布外样本的行为，揭示了泛化源于输入空间曲率与预测行为的几何关联而非实例级记忆。

Conclusion: 神经扩展框架为深度学习泛化提供了机制性基础，将输入空间几何与预测行为联系起来，补充了现有全局理论，为理解过参数化网络的泛化提供了新视角。

Abstract: Deep neural networks demonstrate remarkable generalization despite being highly over-parameterized, challenging traditional statistical learning theories. While several theoretical paradigms such as margin-based bounds, PAC-Bayesian analyses, and neural tangent kernels provide global explanations, they often lack mechanistic insight into how generalization emerges from training dynamics. We introduce Neural Expansion, a unified geometric framework that characterizes generalization through the expansion of directionally-selective ray coverage in the input space. Using Generalization Intervals (GIs), we quantify direction-specific regions around training samples where the model's predictions remain stable. Our extensive empirical studies across diverse architectures and datasets reveal that training leads to progressive expansion of ray coverage, particularly along directions with low Jacobian singular values. This mechanism helps explain a large fraction of correctly classified test inputs, adversarial examples, and even mislabeled or out-of-distribution samples through the geometry of ray coverage rather than instance-level memorization. By connecting input-space curvature to predictive behavior, our framework provides a mechanistic foundation that complements existing global theories of deep learning.

</details>


### [229] [Escaping Parameter Space: Tight Generalization Bounds via Representation Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23729)
*Niclas Göring,Shuofeng Zhang,Branton DeMoss,Ard A. Louis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对确定性神经网络提出了一种基于最后一层隐藏表示的紧泛化界，利用无参数的 k-近邻分类器评估表示质量，避免了传统参数空间复杂度度量、KL散度、权重范数和压缩等手段，实现了非空洞的泛化界认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的泛化界往往依赖参数空间复杂度度量（如权重范数、压缩、PAC-Bayes KL散度等），导致界线松散甚至空洞。作者旨在开发一种直接基于学习到的表示质量的紧泛化界，并将其分解为可独立测量的经验量，以揭示泛化的因果机制。

Method: 基于最后一层隐藏表示，使用无参数的 k-近邻分类器评估表示质量，推导出一个加性分解为三项经验量的泛化界：局部类几何（k-NN解码器误差）、解码器-网络一致性、训练稳定性。该界不依赖参数空间复杂度、KL散度、权重范数或压缩。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10 (ResNet-18) 上，从头训练的界与测试误差相差 8.0 个百分点；在 CIFAR-100 上给出了首个非空洞认证；使用冻结的 DINOv2 表示，在 CIFAR-10 上认证误差 1.4%，在 1000 类 ImageNet 上认证误差 23.1%。主动破坏局部类几何会消除泛化但保留数据集记忆，证实类几何是泛化的因果相关机制。学习到的表示对权重噪声的敏感度显著低于线性读出，解释了为何参数空间证书难以保持紧致。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了首个基于表示质量的紧泛化界，不仅提供了非空洞的认证，还通过分解诊断揭示了局部类几何是泛化的关键因果机制，并指出了参数空间证书松散的根源在于表示对权重噪声的鲁棒性远高于线性读出层。

Abstract: We derive a tight generalization bound for deterministic neural We derive a tight generalization bound for deterministic neural networks based on their last hidden layer representations. By evaluating representation quality with a parameter-free $k$-nearest-neighbor ($k$-NN) classifier, our certificate avoids traditional parameter-space complexity measures, KL divergences to a posterior, weight norms, and compression. The bound is tight: trained from scratch, it reaches an 8.0 percentage point gap to test error on CIFAR-10 (ResNet-18) and yields the first non-vacuous certificates we are aware of on CIFAR-100; with frozen DINOv2 representations, it certifies at 1.4\% on CIFAR-10 and 23.1\% on 1,000-class ImageNet. It decomposes additively into three empirical, independently measurable quantities: local class geometry ($k$-NN decoder error), decoder-network agreement, and training stability. This allows the bound to act as a diagnostic tool: we demonstrate that actively destroying local class geometry eliminates generalization while leaving dataset memorization intact, identifying class geometry as a causally relevant mechanism for generalization. Finally, we show that learned representations are significantly less sensitive to weight noise than linear readouts, suggesting why parameter-space certificates often struggle to remain tight.

</details>


### [230] [LagNet: Lag-Structured Operators for Long-Term Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23735)
*Zhenan Yu,Guangxin Jiang,Jiuyun Jiang,Jin Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于滞后结构时序算子的直接多步预测框架，核心构建模块LagLayer通过Toeplitz矩阵绑定相同时间滞后的历史-未来连接，并引入水平和历史维度的缩放系数放宽精确共享，可替代现有模型中的时序线性层。堆叠LagLayer构成的LagNet在长期时序预测基准上表现具竞争力，且在有限数据下展现出更高的样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期时序预测模型主要关注输入表示和模型结构设计，较少关注历史到未来预测算子本身的结构形式。研究者直接分析该算子，发现不同模型族的敏感度矩阵均呈现对角线模式，对角线对应相对时间滞后，这揭示了预测算子内在的滞后结构特性。

Method: 提出基于滞后结构时序算子的直接多步预测框架。基本模块LagLayer构建Toeplitz矩阵，将具有相同相对滞后的历史-未来连接绑定共享权重，并引入针对预测水平和历史步长的缩放系数以放宽精确权重共享。该设计使LagLayer可直接替代现有模型中的时序线性层。将LagLayer与激活函数和多特征图堆叠形成LagNet架构。

Result: 在长期时序预测基准实验中，该方法与强基线表现具竞争力。敏感度矩阵诊断和有限数据实验表明，LagLayer作为线性层的替代品，具有有效性和样本高效性优势。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模预测算子的滞后结构，LagLayer提供了一种结构化、参数高效且样本高效的时序建模方式，为长期时序预测提供了新的基础组件，且易于集成到现有架构中。

Abstract: Long-term time series forecasting maps a finite history window to an extended future horizon. Many existing models focus primarily on the design of input representations and model structures, while paying less attention to the structural form of the history-to-future forecasting operator itself. We examine this operator directly and find that, across model families, sensitivity matrices exhibit recurring diagonal patterns whose diagonals correspond to relative temporal lags. Motivated by this observation, we develop a framework for direct multi-step forecasting based on lag-structured temporal operators. Its basic building block, LagLayer, constructs a Toeplitz matrix by tying history-to-future connections with the same relative lag and incorporates horizon-wise and history-wise scaling coefficients to relax exact sharing. This design enables LagLayer to serve as an effective replacement for temporal linear layers in existing forecasting models. Stacking LagLayer modules with activations and multiple feature maps yields LagNet. Experiments on long-term time series forecasting benchmarks show that our method is competitive with strong baselines, while sensitivity matrix diagnostics and limited-data experiments demonstrate that LagLayer provides an effective and sample-efficient alternative to a linear layer.

</details>


### [231] [Universal Semantic Enhancement for Weakly Supervised Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23765)
*Siyu Zhou,Yuqin Xie,Hao Yuan,Yingjie Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于通用语义增强的弱监督表格数据异常检测方法，融合预训练表格基础模型的通用知识与目标数据集的领域特定知识，通过原型条件调制机制有效整合两类特征，在仅有8个标注异常样本的情况下优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有弱监督表格异常检测方法主要学习目标领域内在分布模式的特征，但难以完整描述可能的异常特征，且极易过拟合极度稀缺的标签。

Method: 提出基于通用语义增强的弱监督异常检测方法，融合预训练表格基础模型的通用语义知识与目标数据集学习的领域特定特征，设计原型条件调制机制有效整合两类特征，使其相互增强。

Result: 在公开数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在训练过程中仅有8个标注异常样本时，性能优于竞争方法。

Conclusion: 通用语义与领域特定知识互补融合，通过原型条件调制机制可有效提升弱监督表格异常检测性能，解决标签稀缺导致的过拟合和特征不完整问题。

Abstract: Weakly supervised anomaly detection on tabular data is an essential task in many label-scarce applications, since tabular data is widely encountered and it is commonly observed in practice that only a limited number of labeled anomalies are available alongside abundant unlabeled samples. Most existing methods try to learn features that capture the inherent distribution patterns in the target domain. However, such features often struggle to provide a complete description of possible abnormal characteristics and are highly susceptible to overfitting the extremely scarce labels. To address this challenge, we propose a weakly supervised anomaly detection method based on universal semantic enhancement for tabular data, which fuses the universal knowledge from a pre-trained tabular foundation model with the domain-specific knowledge learned on the target dataset. The two types of knowledge are naturally complementary: universal semantics describe the common patterns of tabular data across domains, while the domain-specific features capture the inherent distributions of normal and anomalous behaviors in the target dataset. A prototype-conditioned modulation mechanism is then designed to effectively integrate the two types of features, allowing them to reinforce each other for improved anomaly detection. Extensive experiments on public datasets show that the proposed method outperforms competitive methods when only eight labeled anomalies are available in the training procedure.

</details>


### [232] [Logic-Guided Graph Denoising Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23766)
*Samuel Cognolato,Luciano Serafini,Alessandro Sperduti,Andrea Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于一阶逻辑（FOL）的图扩散模型引导方法，通过分类器引导将领域本体约束融入生成过程，显著提高生成知识图谱满足结构约束的比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图扩散模型在生成复杂图结构数据时，难以精确捕捉严格的结构约束（如本体论规则），导致生成无效图。需要一种方法在推理阶段将领域知识作为硬性约束引入预训练模型，而无需重新训练。

Method: 利用一阶逻辑（FOL）形式化领域本体约束，将其转化为模糊可微的目标函数；在扩散模型的去噪轨迹中引入分类器引导，通过优化该目标函数引导生成过程满足逻辑规则。仅需在推理时指定 FOL 约束，即可指导预训练生成模型。

Result: 在知识图谱无条件生成任务中，逻辑引导框架显著提高了生成图满足本体论约束的比例，优于无约束的基线扩散模型。

Conclusion: 通过一阶逻辑与分类器引导相结合，可在不修改模型参数的情况下，将领域结构约束有效注入图扩散模型生成过程，实现可控、合规的图结构生成。

Abstract: Graph diffusion models are competitive approaches for generating complex graph-structured data. However, when such distributions have strict structural constraints, diffusion models may fail to capture them exactly, resulting in the generation of invalid graphs. In this paper, we propose a method for grounding graph diffusion models in domain-specific knowledge via First Order Logic (FOL), leveraging its expressive and interpretable formalism to impose structural constraints on the generative process. We incorporate domain knowledge using classifier guidance, a technique that conditions the diffusion model's distribution on external variables to exert control over the sampled data. During the generative process, classifier guidance steers the denoising trajectory to optimize an objective function, which we define as a fuzzy and differentiable relaxation of the logical rules. Our methodology requires only the specification of constraints in FOL, whose satisfaction is encouraged at inference time to guide a pre-trained generative model. We evaluate our approach by comparing the unconditional generation of knowledge graphs with our logic-guided framework, which incorporates domain ontologies as structural constraints during inference. This approach significantly increases the proportion of generated graphs that satisfy the ontological constraints.

</details>


### [233] [Explainable Machine Learning Analysis of Temperature Effects on Mechanical Strength of Fiber-Reinforced High-Performance Concrete](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23811)
*Jagaran Chakma,Zhiguang Zhou,Badhan Chakma,Priyanta Chakma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于机器学习的数据驱动框架，利用1204条实验记录预测纤维增强高性能混凝土在温度作用下的抗压强度。集成学习方法（梯度提升和极端梯度提升）表现最佳，R²均达0.903。温度是影响抗压强度的首要因素，随后为细骨料、外加剂、水泥用量、水胶比和用水量。强度随温度升高显著下降，但劣化程度取决于具体配合比设计。最佳模型已部署为Python图形界面，便于工程快速预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纤维增强高性能混凝土在温度暴露下的抗压强度预测极具挑战，因配合比组成、纤维性质、养护条件和温度之间存在复杂的相互依赖关系，传统经验公式难以准确捕捉这些非线性交互作用，亟需一种数据驱动的高精度预测方法。

Method: 收集1204条涵盖多样材料设计和暴露条件的实验数据；评估八种机器学习模型，重点对比集成学习与单一学习器；采用梯度提升和极端梯度提升作为最优模型；利用SHAP值和偏依赖分析解释模型，揭示特征重要性及非线性关系；开发基于Python的图形用户界面实现快速预测。

Result: 梯度提升和极端梯度提升在未见数据上均达到R²=0.903；梯度提升的RMSE最低（6.67 MPa），极端梯度提升的MAE最低（3.97 MPa）和MAPE最低（10.60%）；SHAP分析显示温度为首要影响因素，其次为细骨料、外加剂、水泥用量、水胶比和用水量；抗压强度随温度升高显著下降，但劣化程度取决于具体配合比组合。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了准确且可解释的温度依赖性抗压强度评价方法论，为纤维增强高性能混凝土的实用化、数据驱动设计提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Predicting the compressive strength of fiber-reinforced high-performance concrete subjected to temperature exposure is challenging because to the intricate interdependencies of mixture composition, fiber properties, curing conditions, and temperature. A data-driven framework to predict compressive strength utilizing 1,204 experimental records containing a diverse array of material designs and exposure situations. This study evaluates eight machine-learning models and demonstrate that ensemble approaches routinely surpass individual learners. Both gradient boosting and extreme gradient boosting exhibit superior performance on unseen data, each attaining a R^2 of 0.903. Gradient boosting produces the minimal root mean square error (6.67 MPa), whereas extreme gradient boosting achieves the lowest mean absolute error (3.97 MPa) and mean absolute percentage error (10.60%). The models are further interpreted utilizing Shapley additive explanations and partial dependence analysis, which reveal temperature as the primary factor influencing compressive strength, succeeded by fine aggregate, superplasticizer, cement content, water-to-binder ratio, and water content. Strength diminishes significantly with rising temperature, albeit the extent of deterioration is contingent upon the specific combination design. The best performing models are subsequently implemented in a Python-based graphical user interface for expedited prediction. Our findings offer a precise and comprehensible methodology for evaluating temperature-dependent compressive strength and facilitate the practical, data-driven design of fiber-reinforced high-performance concrete.

</details>


### [234] [Learning Personalized Memory Gating from Delayed One-Sided Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23812)
*Kaixuan Ji,Zixiang Chen,Wenting Zhao,Cheng Qian,Heng Wang,Weizhi Zhang,Jielin Qiu,Liangwei Yang,Quanquan Gu,Silvio Savarese,Shelby Heinecke,Huan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种个性化记忆门控策略 Warm-Start UCB，将事实级记忆门控建模为带延迟单侧反馈的上下文苹果品尝问题，结合离线预训练与在线自适应，理论证明了高概率遗憾上界，实验表明其在不同内存预算和历史长度下均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆门控策略通常在用户间共享，无法捕捉用户特定的未来复用模式，且有限记忆库无法保存所有提取的事实，因此需要个性化的事实级记忆门控方法。

Method: 提出基于检索的标签策略，将每个事实标记为是否被后续检索并融入回复；将在线记忆门控建模为带延迟单侧反馈的上下文苹果品尝问题；提出 Warm-Start UCB 算法，结合现有数据的离线学习与针对当前用户的在线自适应。

Result: 理论上建立了高概率遗憾上界$𝑂~(𝑇𝜀∧𝑑𝑇)$（延迟窗口至多$𝑂(𝑇)$）；实验表明该方法在不同内存预算和历史长度下均优于所有基线。

Conclusion: Warm-Start UCB 是一种有前景的个性化记忆门控策略，有效解决了用户特定的事实复用预测问题，在理论和实验上均表现优异。

Abstract: Long-term memory enables language-model agents to personalize future interactions, but a finite memory bank cannot preserve every extracted fact. Existing memory gating policies are typically shared across users and therefore fail to account for user-specific patterns of future reuse. In this paper, we study memory gating at the fact level. We first propose a retrieval-based labeling strategy that labels each fact by whether it is later retrieved and incorporated into responses. We then formulate online memory gating as contextual apple tasting with delayed one-sided feedback, and propose Warm-Start UCB, which combines offline learning from existing data with online adaptation to the current user. Our theoretical analysis establishes a high-probability regret upper bound of $\tilde{O}(Tε\wedge d\sqrt{T})$ when the delay window is at most $O(\sqrt{T})$, where $T$ is the total number of rounds, $ε$ is the initialization error, $d$ is the dimension of the feature map, and $\tilde{O}(\cdot)$ hides constant and poly-logarithmic factors. The results of our experiment demonstrate that our method outperforms all baselines under different memory budgets and history lengths, establishing it as a promising personalized memory gating policy.

</details>


### [235] [CocoRNA: Collective RNA Design with Cooperative Multi-agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23820)
*Tianmeng Hu,Biao Luo,Ke Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出协作多智能体强化学习框架CocoRNA用于RNA逆向设计，通过任务分解和中心化评论家指导，显著提高设计成功率和速度，并能泛化到未见结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RNA序列设计使其折叠成特定二级结构对于理解生物功能至关重要，但仍是计算难题；现有方法在复杂结构设计上效果有限。

Method: CocoRNA采用协作多智能体强化学习，将设计任务分解为子问题，多智能体协作求解；训练时使用中心化评论家利用全局结构信息指导智能体联合优化策略。

Result: 在Rfam数据集上，CocoRNA在成功率和设计速度上显著优于现有最先进方法；在其他生物序列设计任务上也展现出有效性和广泛潜力。

Conclusion: CocoRNA通过多智能体协作和中心化训练实现了高质量RNA设计策略的学习，能无需额外训练泛化到未见结构，为复杂生物序列设计提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Designing RNA sequences that reliably fold into specific secondary structures is essential for understanding their biological functions but remains a challenging computational problem. We propose CocoRNA, a cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning framework for RNA inverse design. CocoRNA simplifies the design task by decomposing it into smaller sub-problems, each solved collaboratively by multiple agents. This approach reduces the complexity of the problem and improves the exploration of design policies. During training, a centralized critic uses global structural information to guide the agents, enabling them to jointly optimize their design strategies. As a result, CocoRNA learns high-quality RNA design policies that generalize effectively to unseen structures without additional training. Experiments on the Rfam dataset demonstrate that CocoRNA substantially outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both success rate and design speed. Further experiments on other biological sequence design tasks highlight the effectiveness and broad potential of CocoRNA for complex design tasks.

</details>


### [236] [Waiting Buys Exactly Two Moments: Endogenous Identification in Prediction-Driven Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23830)
*Rama AlHamidi,Rasul Khanbayov,Erchin Serpedin,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究预算固定的规划者如何决定预防性援助的对象和时机。等待能揭示风险但会错失干预机会，因为高风险个体会优先流失。在经典模型中，长度为τ的观测前缀能精确识别潜在风险矩 m_j = E[p^j] (j=0,...,2τ-1)，每增加一个观测期获得两个新矩。这些矩虽能识别存活者的平均下一期流失率，但不一定能识别分配福利或优先治疗人群的流失情况。观测等价构造证明了固定观测长度下任何样本量都存在不可避免的时机遗憾，而精确证书可判断所有兼容人群是否共享唯一最优日期。实验显示：较长观测常能消除时机模糊，但最优日期可能已过；Bootstrap平均和早停能改善决策，但遗憾加权在汇总均方遗憾上无改善——更好的统计拟合不一定带来更好的分配福利。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规划者面临预防性援助的时机困境：等待观测能更准确识别高风险个体，但高风险个体会因流失而错失干预机会。需在风险揭示与干预机会保留之间权衡，且存在观测噪声和风险依赖性流失。现有方法未充分解决固定观测长度下的根本识别局限及其对分配福利的影响。

Method: 1. 理论分析：在经典模型下刻画观测前缀长度τ与可识别风险矩的关系，证明每增加一期获得两个新矩。2. 观测等价构造：建立不可区分的人群分布，证明固定观测长度下存在不可避免的时机遗憾。3. 精确证书：开发判断所有兼容人群是否共享唯一最优干预日期的标准。4. 匹配实验：分离估计误差、bootstrap平均和遗憾加权对决策质量的影响。5. 实证评估：在合成和真实数据上测试不同策略的汇总均方遗憾表现。

Result: 1. 长度τ的观测识别风险矩 m_j (j=0,...,2τ-1)，但这些矩仅识别存活者平均流失率，不识别分配福利或优先治疗人群流失。2. 存在观测等价构造，导致固定观测长度下任何样本量都有不可避免的时机遗憾。3. 精确证书可判断最优日期唯一性。4. 实验显示：较长观测常消除时机模糊，但最优日期可能已过；bootstrap平均和早停显著改善决策；遗憾加权在汇总均方遗憾上无增益；更好的统计拟合不保证更好的分配福利。

Conclusion: 预防性援助的时机决策面临根本识别局限：观测获得的风险矩不足以识别分配福利相关量。固定观测长度下存在不可避免的时机遗憾。实践中，bootstrap平均和早停是有效的改进手段，而单纯追求统计拟合优度（如遗憾加权）不一定提升分配福利。决策者需权衡观测时长与干预时机，警惕最优干预窗口已过的风险。

Abstract: A planner with a fixed budget of preventive help must decide whom to help and when. Waiting reveals risk but removes intervention opportunities as higher-risk individuals leave first. We characterize this tradeoff under repeated noisy signals and risk-dependent attrition: in the canonical model, a length-$τ$ observation prefix identifies exactly the latent risk moments $m_j=\mathbb E[p^j]$ for $j=0,\ldots,2τ-1$, buying two new moments per additional period. These identify average next-period attrition among survivors but need not identify allocation welfare or attrition among those prioritized for treatment. Our observationally equivalent constructions establish unavoidable timing regret at any sample size for a fixed observation length, while exact certificates determine whether all compatible populations share a unique optimal date. Matched experiments separate the effects of estimation, bootstrap averaging, and regret weighting. Longer observation often resolves timing ambiguity, but the optimal date may already have passed. In our experiments, bootstrap averaging and early stopping improve decisions, while regret weighting adds no improvement in pooled mean squared regret: better statistical fit need not yield better allocation welfare. The code and artifacts are available at https://github.com/KurbanIntelligenceLab/waiting-buys-two-moments.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [237] [Structured Authority Injection: Persistent Human Control for LLM Agents via Typed MCP Overrides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22373)
*Ryan Tran,YueMing Zhang,Lingfeng Tao,Nan Yang,Shengjie Xu,Yu Yin,Shuai Xu,Wenlu Zhang,Hailu Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出结构化权威注入（SAI）协议，通过模式验证的MCP工具调用和排名权威上下文，解决LLM代理中人类操作员指令持久性和权威衰减问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理作为自主编排器广泛使用，但缺乏协议级机制让人类操作员注入跨规划周期持久的约束性指令；自由文本覆盖存在歧义解读（缺乏模式验证）和权威衰减（指令在累积上下文中影响力减弱）问题。

Method: 提出SAI协议：将操作员纠正通过类型化、模式验证的MCP工具调用路由，维护排名权威上下文前置于每个LLM提示；定义三类覆盖（约束、优先级、策略）具有不同持久性语义，通过不可被规划过程访问的定向权威通道强制执行显式优先级顺序；无需模型微调或权重访问，适用于支持MCP工具使用的指令调优LLM。

Result: 在九个LLM和八个场景评估中，SAI减少歧义指令解读，缓解跨规划迭代的权威衰减，最强模型提示开销降低高达38%，但收益因模型而异。

Conclusion: SAI为需要显式且持久人类控制的代理系统提供通用设计模式。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly used as autonomous orchestrators in distributed and multi-agent systems, yet a control gap remains: no protocol-level mechanism allows human operators to inject binding directives that persist across planning cycles. Free-text overrides suffer from ambiguous interpretation due to lack of schema validation and from authority erosion as operator instructions lose influence within accumulating context. We propose Structured Authority Injection (SAI), a protocol that addresses these issues by routing operator corrections through typed, schema-validated Model Context Protocol (MCP) tool calls and maintaining a ranked authority context prepended to each LLM prompt. SAI defines three override classes (Constraint, Priority, and Strategy) with distinct persistence semantics and an explicit precedence order enforced via a directed authority channel inaccessible to the planning process. The method requires no model fine-tuning or access to model weights and applies to instruction-tuned LLMs supporting MCP tool use. Evaluation across nine LLMs and eight scenarios targeting agent failure modes shows reduced ambiguous directive interpretation and mitigated authority decay across planning iterations, with lower prompt overhead in the strongest models (up to 38% depending on model and scenario), though benefit varies substantially across models. SAI provides a general design pattern for agent systems requiring explicit and persistent human control.

</details>


### [238] [Rethinking User Encoders: Routing Diffusion States for Scenario-Adaptive User Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22400)
*Junru Zhang,Jinyong Wen,Bin Dou,Wuliang Huang,Baokun Wang,Yu Cheng,Weiqiang Wang,Cheng Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FINDERS框架，利用扩散语言模型（dLLM）的迭代去噪过程作为多粒度重构空间，通过四步操作（去噪、因子化、路由、压缩）学习用户表示，解决现有自回归用户编码器单向约束导致的双向信息流受限问题，并在十个工业场景中超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的用户编码器将异构行为日志序列化并通过自回归预测压缩为静态表示，但其单向约束限制了双向信息流动，阻碍跨模态交互和多源信号联合保存，导致学到的静态表示难以满足多样下游场景的不同证据需求。

Method: 提出FINDERS（FactorIzatioN of Diffusion statEs for Routing across Scenarios），包含四个操作：去噪、因子化、路由、压缩。利用可学习的元槽将互补的去噪状态组织为可迁移的跨模态语义和模态特定行为因子。构建BehaviorQA大规模语料库提供显式行为理由监督。轻量级路由器在跨源融合和最终压缩前，通过输入级残差路径根据场景查询动态强调相关槽方向。

Result: 在十个工业场景的大量实验和在线A/B测试中，FINDERS在统一和场景自适应设置下均持续优于最先进方法。

Conclusion: 该工作有效拓展了扩散语言模型超越语言生成的边界，展示了其作为强大用户表示学习器的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Existing large language model (LLM)-based user encoders serialize heterogeneous behavioral logs and compress them into static representations through autoregressive (AR) prediction. However, their unidirectional constraint limits bidirectional information flow, hindering cross-modal interactions and the joint preservation of multi-source signals. Consequently, the learned static representations struggle to accommodate the distinct evidence requirements of diverse downstream scenarios. We address these limitations by unlocking the iterative denoising process of diffusion language models (dLLMs) as a multi-granularity reconstruction space. Based on this insight, we propose FINDERS (FactorIzatioN of Diffusion statEs for Routing across Scenarios), a router-equipped dLLM framework comprising four operations: denoise, factorize, route, and compress. Learnable meta-slots organize complementary denoising states into transferable cross-modal semantics and modality-specific behavioral factors. To provide grounded supervision, we construct BehaviorQA, a large-scale corpus containing explicit behavioral rationales. Guided by scenario queries, a lightweight router dynamically emphasizes relevant slot directions via an input-level residual pathway before cross-source fusion and final compression. Extensive experiments and online A/B tests across ten industrial scenarios demonstrate that FINDERS consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both unified and scenario-adaptive settings. This work effectively pushes the boundaries of dLLMs beyond language generation, demonstrating their immense potential as powerful user representation learners.

</details>


### [239] [From Events to Reasoning: Hierarchical Rule Composition over Explicit States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22403)
*Cheng Lyu,Xueqi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出事件推理系统（ERS），通过显式事件图作为推理状态，结合语义特征提名候选对象与规则绑定，利用声明式规则与任务条件约束执行路径，实现可证明的依赖保持修正与预算感知控制。实验表明 ERS 在结构化事件图上能构建长达 12 步的依赖链，随前瞻增长将无关领域操作从 32 降至 0，在 24 个负例中零非支持输出；冻结规则下通过 12/12 聚焦检查与 6/6 事实变更案例，且在 14 个联合开发任务中动态子目标配置比单层全局控制节省 51.6% 推理耗时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理系统虽能给出结论，但难以提供可追溯的辩护：要求每步操作在可用事实下均可许、中间结果依赖能存活至路径终点、且前提变更时路径可修正。作者旨在构建一种能显式维护依赖图、支持组合表达与预算约束下的可修正推理系统。

Method: ERS 将推理状态建模为显式事件图。语义特征提名候选对象与规则绑定，声明式规则与任务条件决定可执行性，周期、单位、证据约束阻止非法候选进入路径。底层规则提供可复用局部变换，递归可组合的高层控制规则在当前目标、信息缺口与预算下组织子目标与合法路径，从而将有限规则库重绑定为未预存的完整解路径。形式化定义了可许转移、任务依赖控制、组合表达性、依赖保持修正，以及可达解与预算控制器找到解之间的差距。

Result: 在结构化事件图基准上，ERS 构建最长 12 步依赖链；前瞻增至 16 时无关领域操作从 32 降为 0；24 个负例中零非支持输出。冻结规则后：12/12 聚焦检查通过（含 9 个未见输入/路径组合的接受推导与 3 个合理拒绝），6/6 事实变更案例通过。14 个联合开发任务中，动态子目标配置比单层全局控制节省 51.6% 推理墙钟时间。

Conclusion: ERS 通过显式事件图与分层规则架构，实现了可证明的依赖保持、可修正性与预算感知控制，在结构化事件推理任务上显著优于单层全局控制，且具备强泛化与鲁棒性。

Abstract: What a reasoning system emits is a conclusion; what it owes is a justification. Producing one requires every operation to be admissible on the facts actually available, the dependencies of intermediate results to survive to the end of the path, and the path to be revised when a premise changes. We present the Event Reasoning System (ERS), in which the reasoning state is an explicit event graph. Semantic features nominate candidate objects and rule bindings, and declared rule and task conditions decide which may execute, so candidates that violate the represented period, unit, or evidence constraints cannot enter an accepted reasoning path. Low-level rules provide reusable local transformations, and recursively composable high-level control rules organize subgoals and legal paths under the current goal, information gaps, and budget; a finite library is thereby rebound into paths never stored as complete solutions. We formalize admissible transitions, task-dependent control, compositional expressivity, dependency-preserving revision, and the gap between reachable solutions and those a budgeted controller finds. On structured-event graphs, ERS builds dependency chains of up to twelve steps, cuts irrelevant executed domain operations from 32 to 0 as lookahead grows to 16, and produces no unsupported target output across 24 negative cases; with rules frozen, it passes 12/12 focused checks comprising nine accepted derivations involving unseen input/path combinations and three justified refusals, as well as 6/6 fact-change cases. On fourteen jointly solved development tasks, the recorded dynamic subgoal configuration uses 51.6% less inference wall-clock time than single-level global control under their respective frozen settings.

</details>


### [240] [ECHOES: Seeing Beyond the Current Image via Recursive History Compression and Privileged On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22408)
*Tengda Guo,Jiawang Hu,Yifei Tao,Yin Wang,Haotian Hu,Fanyi Wang,Liujuan Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ECHOES 通过训练时的特权信息指导文本历史压缩，解决了长时程 GUI 代理中保留历史截图增加推理成本与文本摘要丢失关键状态信息的矛盾，在部署时无需保留历史截图即可实现具有竞争力的性能和良好的性能-推理成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程 GUI 代理依赖视觉历史维持任务状态，但保留历史截图会增加视觉 token 使用量和推理成本，而文本摘要可能丢弃关键状态信息，损害长时程可靠性。需要一种在部署时不保留历史截图、又能保持高性能和高记忆保真度的方法。

Method: 提出 ECHOES 方法：采用 Compression-Think-Action (CTA) 格式递归更新文本历史状态并推理；利用拥有特权多图像输入的同一模型作为教师，对学生生成的 CTA 轨迹提供密集 token 级在线策略蒸馏；设计感知-记忆保真度 (PMF) Gap 引导的 OPD-RL 框架，动态平衡特权蒸馏与任务奖励优化，并通过周期性教师更新支持持续改进；构建 ECHOESBMK 基准，基于真实 GUI 代理失败轨迹细粒度评估视觉感知与跨模态状态保持。

Result: 在四个 GUI 基准和 ECHOESBMK 上实验显示，任务性能和记忆保真度显著提升。部署时无需保留历史截图，仍达成具有竞争力的性能，展现有利的性能-推理成本权衡，并表现出对未见视觉规划任务的正向零样本迁移能力。

Conclusion: ECHOES 有效利用训练时的特权信息指导文本历史压缩，解决了长时程 GUI 代理中视觉 token 开销与信息丢失的权衡，实现了部署时无需历史截图的高效推理，同时保持强劲性能和零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: Long-horizon GUI agents rely on visual history to maintain task states, but retaining historical screenshots increases visual-token usage and inference costs, while textual summaries can discard critical state information, undermining long-horizon reliability. We propose ECHOES, which leverages training-time privileged information to guide textual history compression and strengthen long-horizon reasoning. Specifically, ECHOES adopts a Compression-Think-Action (CTA) format to recursively update a textual history state and reason. The same model, given privileged multi-image inputs, serves as a teacher that provides dense token-level on-policy distillation on student-generated CTA rollouts. Our Perception-Memory Fidelity (PMF) Gap-guided OPD-RL framework dynamically balances privileged distillation and task-reward optimization, with periodic teacher updates supporting continued improvement. We further introduce ECHOESBMK, a fine-grained diagnostic benchmark derived from real GUI agent failure trajectories to jointly evaluate visual perception and cross-modal state retention. Experiments on four GUI benchmarks and ECHOESBMK demonstrate substantial gains in task performance and memory fidelity. Without retaining historical screenshots at deployment, ECHOES achieves competitive performance with a favorable performance-inference-cost trade-off and exhibits positive zero-shot transfer to unseen visual planning tasks.

</details>


### [241] [Sensitivity Analysis of Decision Rules in an Agent-Based Agro-Ecological Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22420)
*Benjamin Pianet,Baptiste Lesquoy,Paul Saves,Benoit Gaudou,Jean Villerd,Nicolas Verstaevel,Olivier Therond*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对基于代理的农发生态模型中的决策树状参数空间，提出了一种基于随机森林监督的HSIC-ANOVA方法，用于在层次化设计空间中进行敏感性分析，并将其应用于MAELIA平台的氮淋失、土壤有机碳变化和产量预测等模拟输出，实现了可解释的、面向决策的敏感性排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的全局敏感性分析假设输入参数独立且位于固定的笛卡尔积空间中，但农发生态决策系统（如作物管理路径）呈现树状层次结构：许多参数仅在高层决策激活时才出现。经典方法在这类分支空间上定义不良且存在结构性偏差，无法公平评估罕见激活与常见激活决策的影响。

Method: 将MAELIA平台的作物管理路径编码为包含分类变量与数值变量的层次化设计空间；采用先进的代理建模技术，提出随机森林监督的HSIC-ANOVA方法，原生支持在决策树上对主效应与交互效应进行排序，使罕见激活与常见激活决策处于同等地位。

Result: 该方法成功应用于氮淋失、土壤有机碳变化和产量预测等模拟输出，能够识别真正驱动系统行为的关键决策节点，提供可解释的、面向决策的敏感性分析结果，优于传统扁平参数空间方法。

Conclusion: 通过将决策结构作为一等建模对象，并结合随机森林监督的HSIC-ANOVA，本文为层次化决策空间的敏感性分析提供了新范式，使基于代理的农发生态模型能够产出可直接用于决策支持的洞见。

Abstract: Agent-based models are well-suited for modelling agro-ecological systems for decision-making, yet turning their simulations into actionable insight requires identifying which decisions actually drive the behaviours. In such a context, sensitivity analysis stumbles upon a structural feature of many complex systems: the parameters are not a flat vector but a decision tree in which many parameters appear only when a higher-level choice activates them. Classical global sensitivity analysis presupposes independent inputs over a fixed Cartesian product and is ill-posed on such a branched space and biased by construction. We instead treat the structure of the decisions as a first-class modelling object. Using advanced surrogate modelling techniques, we encode the crop-management itinerary of the high-resolution agro-ecological modelling platform MAELIA as a hierarchical design space featuring both categorical and numerical variables. We propose a novel dedicated method to analyse this kind of hierarchical space, a random-forest-supervised HSIC-ANOVA, which ranks main effects and interactions natively on the tree while placing rarely and always active decisions on an equal footing. We show how, when applied to simulation outputs such as nitrogen leaching, soil organic carbon variation, and yield predictions, it can deliver an interpretable, decision-oriented reading.

</details>


### [242] [PRIME: Towards Verifiable Evidence-Grounded Reasoning in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22426)
*Hamed Khosravi,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 PRIME 框架，通过预先固定证据路径和独立账本程序，强制 LLM 代理仅报告规则授权的结论，在 480 个公开记录任务上将不合理结论率从 26.7% 降为 0，并将合理结论平均得分从 30.1 提升至满分 59.2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 代理在依据组织需求检查记录（如漏洞报告是否准备好调查）时，常因需求允许多种满足方式而出现两类失败：一是检查失败仍接受报告，二是满足于较弱结论而未尝试其他可行路径。需要一种机制保证仅输出有证据支撑的最强结论。

Method: PRIME（Precommitted Rules for Inference from Minimal Evidence）包含两大组件：1) 预提交规则：在检查前固定每个结论的证据路由，明确何种证据组合可推导出何种结论。2) 独立账本程序：记录每次检查结果，拒绝无证据支持的结论，并主动指引或自动执行能支撑更强结论的额外检查。系统仅输出规则与账本双重授权的结论。

Result: 在 480 个公开记录任务上，仅展示需求的基线代理有 26.7% 任务输出不合理结论，PRIME 则为 0%。仅使用拒绝机制时合理结论平均分 30.1，加入主动指引与自动补全检查后提升至上限 59.2（满分 100）。

Conclusion: PRIME 通过预提交证据路由与独立账本的强制约束，实现了在给定正确需求、已知检查位置和足够预算下，必然收敛到证据支持的最强结论，有效解决了 LLM 代理在多路径合规检查中的不可靠问题。

Abstract: LLM agents are used to check records against an organization's requirements, for example whether a vulnerability report is ready for investigation. Requirements often allow several ways to be met, such as a severity rating with a curator-reviewed advisory or a published advisory link. Agents fail by accepting a report after a required check has failed, or by settling for a weaker conclusion without trying another way that could still succeed. We introduce PRIME (Precommitted Rules for Inference from Minimal Evidence). Before checking, it fixes evidence routes specifying how each conclusion may be justified. A separate program records each check result and refuses any conclusion the results do not justify. It also points the agent to checks that could justify a stronger conclusion, or runs them itself. PRIME reports only conclusions authorized by its rules and ledger. With correct requirements, exact checks at known locations, and sufficient budget at stopping, completion reaches the strongest supported conclusion. On 480 public-record tasks, an agent shown the requirements reported unjustified conclusions on 26.7% of tasks, while PRIME reported none. Its directions and added checks raised the average score for justified conclusions from 30.1, with refusal alone, to the maximum of 59.2 out of 100.

</details>


### [243] [From Natural Language Intent to Executable Control: Hierarchical Feedback-Guided LLM Planning for Multi-component Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22441)
*Jinglin Dai,Jiawan Wang,Ruiqi Hong,Lei Bu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个分层反馈框架，利用大语言模型（LLM）生成网络物理系统（CPS）的可执行任务计划。框架提示LLM同时生成结构化任务计划和信号时序逻辑（STL）约束，通过算法识别两者矛盾并提供修正反馈；在执行层面，当控制合成失败时提取诊断信息（如违反约束、组件冲突）反馈指导重新规划。在七个异构多组件CPS场景实验中，该框架配合GPT-5达到87.9%的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决LLM生成的CPS任务计划存在的两大问题：一是计划可能误解或遗漏用户意图，二是逻辑上合理的计划在组件动力学、交互或安全约束下可能不可行。现有基于LLM的规划方法缺乏关于计划失败原因的信息性反馈，难以指导计划修正。

Method: 提出分层反馈框架：1）任务层：提示LLM生成结构化任务计划及对应STL约束，设计可靠算法检测两者间的确定性矛盾并提供修正反馈；2）执行层：控制合成失败时提取诊断信息（违反约束、组件分配冲突等）反馈指导任务重规划。两层反馈协同迭代，直至生成可执行计划。

Result: 在七个异构多组件CPS场景实验中，该框架能生成满足原始自然语言任务的可执行计划及可行控制执行，配合GPT-5实现87.9%的任务成功率。

Conclusion: 分层反馈框架有效弥合了LLM生成计划与CPS实际执行之间的鸿沟，通过任务层逻辑一致性检查和执行层诊断反馈的双重保障，显著提升了LLM为CPS生成安全可执行计划的能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) provide a convenient interface for translating natural language commands into task plans for cyber-physical systems (CPSs). However, two gaps hinder their use for real execution: generated plans may misinterpret or omit the user's intent, and even a logically plausible plan may be infeasible under component dynamics, interactions, or safety constraints. Existing LLM-based planning approaches often lack informative feedback on why a generated plan fails, providing little guidance for revising the plan accordingly. To address these challenges, we present a hierarchical feedback framework that uses different types of feedback information to guide LLMs in generating executable plans for CPSs. We prompt LLMs to generate both a structured task plan and corresponding signal temporal logic (STL) constraints, and design a sound algorithm that identifies definite contradictions between them and provides feedback for revising inconsistencies. At the execution level, when control synthesis fails, we extract different types of diagnostic information, such as violated constraints and conflicting component assignments, and feed them back to guide task replanning.
